`README.md`:

```md
# Astral AntiCheat

An anti-cheat and anti-macro plugin for **Paper 1.21.11** (Java 21), by MrTahaDarvish.

**Astral blocks cheats, it does not ban.** Out of the box the hits of combat cheaters do not
register, flyers and speeders are pulled back, silent swaps, auto attribute swaps, auto stun
slams and mace exploits are cancelled, and macros are stopped. Nobody is kicked or banned
automatically. Staff get **one short alert line per player** that says what they might be
using ("Steve may be using KillAura (silent rotations) x6, Silent swap x2"), and every flag is
logged.

Minigames that move players themselves (a battle royale **battle bus**, launch pads, rides,
cutscenes) are handled automatically, see [Minigames and the battle bus](#minigames-and-the-battle-bus).

Astral reads the exact position of every client tick. Since Minecraft 1.21.2 the client
sends a "tick end" packet after each tick, and Paper exposes it as `ClientTickEndEvent`.
Astral uses it to rerun vanilla physics (gravity, friction, acceleration, jump, step) and
compare the result with what the client claims. It also knows the order in which a vanilla
client sends its packets inside a tick (slot change, actions, swing, movement, tick end), so
anything sent **after** the movement packet or **between** actions gives away silent
rotations and silent swaps. Combat checks use lag compensation (the target's position history
matched to the attacker's ping).

---

## Installation

1. Build it (see below) or take `Astral-<version>.jar`.
2. Put the jar in your server's `plugins/` folder (Paper **1.21.11**, Java 21).
3. Start the server. The config is written to `plugins/Astral/`.

Optional: **ViaVersion** (shows the real client version of players on other versions) and
**Floodgate/Geyser** (Bedrock players are detected and skipped by default).

### Building

```bash
mvn clean package
# -> target/Astral-1.1.0.jar   (also runs the cheat-client simulation tests)
```

---

## Block mode vs watch mode

| Setting | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `settings.mitigation` | `true` | `true` = **block cheats**: cancel hits, set back movement, cancel swaps / block breaks / clicks. `false` = watch only (alerts + logs). |
| `settings.max-setback-distance` | `12` | A setback never moves a player further than this. Further away (e.g. after a long fall from a bus) only the illegal move is undone. |
| `punishments.enabled` | `false` | `false` = Astral **never** kicks or bans anyone by itself. |
| `checks.<check>.punish` | `false` | Per-check auto punish (only used when `punishments.enabled` is on). |

Staff can still kick or ban by hand with `/astral kick` and `/astral ban`.

> **Updating from 1.0?** Astral 1.0 only watched. On the first start, 1.1 updates your
> `config.yml` (`config-version: 2`): blocking is switched on, new options are added, your other
> values are kept. Set `settings.mitigation: false` if you only want to watch.

---

## Compact alerts

Staff with `astral.alerts` no longer get one chat line per flag. Astral groups the flags of a
player and sends one line that says what he is most likely using:

```
[Astral] Steve may be using KillAura (silent rotations) x6, Silent swap x2 [VL 14.0 | 45ms | blocked]
```

Hover it for the details (every check and type, the last measurement, ping, TPS, client), click
it to teleport. When lines are sent (`config.yml` -> `alerts`):

| Option | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `compact` | `true` | `false` = old style, one line per flag |
| `gather-seconds` | `1` | The first flags of a player are collected this long, then sent as one line |
| `interval-seconds` | `5` | A **new** cheat of the same player is reported at most this often |
| `repeat-seconds` | `30` | The **same** cheats are only reminded this often (with the new count) |
| `reset-after-seconds` | `120` | After this long without flags everything counts as new again |

A cheater flagging 10 times a second for a minute gives 2 lines instead of 600. `/astral verbose`
and `/astral debug <check>` still show every single flag. The names ("KillAura (silent
rotations)", "Auto attribute swap" ...) can be changed in `messages.yml` under `cheat-names`.

---

## Minigames and the battle bus

Battle royale buses, launch pads, rides and cutscenes move players in ways no client can. Astral
recognises all the usual ways a plugin does that, with any ping:

* **Riding** an entity the plugin moves (armor stand / display / minecart / boat seat, happy
  ghast), and the jump off it: no flags while riding, the fall after it is checked normally.
* **Standing on** a moving platform made of shulkers, boats or minecarts.
* **Teleports every tick** along a route (Paper freezes the player until the client confirms a
  teleport; Astral waits for that instead of seeing a hovering player).
* **setVelocity every tick**, launch pads, double jump launchers (also when the plugin cancels
  the flight toggle).
* **setGravity(false)**, slow falling parachutes, levitation, gravity attribute changes (the
  client gets them half a ping later, Astral allows both for that moment).
* **Spectator** on the bus, back to survival in the air; elytra drops; plugin rockets.
* Boats a plugin lifts or moves are never "BoatFly".
* Setbacks never teleport a player back up to the bus or across the map.

For anything else:

* `settings.exempt-worlds: [lobby, bus_world]` - nobody is checked in these worlds.
* `/astral exempt <player|*> <seconds|off> [movement|combat|player|world|macro|all]` - skip checks
  for a while (default: movement). Works from the console, so a minigame can run it, e.g.
  `astral exempt %player% 20 movement` when a player boards the bus.

---

## Commands

Main command: `/astral` (aliases: `/astralac`, `/anticheat`, `/ac`)

| Command | Description | Permission |
|---|---|---|
| `/astral help` | List all commands | `astral.command.help` (everyone) |
| `/astral version [player]` | Astral version, mode, server version and **client version** (yours, or another player's) | `astral.command.version` (everyone), `astral.command.version.others` |
| `/astral reload` | Reload `config.yml` and `messages.yml` | `astral.command.reload` |
| `/astral alerts [on\|off]` | Toggle receiving flags | `astral.command.alerts` |
| `/astral verbose` | See **every** flag (no cooldown, no minimum VL) | `astral.command.verbose` |
| `/astral checks [category]` | List all checks (click to toggle) | `astral.command.checks` |
| `/astral toggle <check> [on\|off]` | Enable / disable a check (saved to config) | `astral.command.toggle` |
| `/astral info <player>` | Full profile: client, ping, CPS, bypass, VL | `astral.command.info` |
| `/astral violations <player>` | Violation level per check | `astral.command.violations` |
| `/astral logs <player> [page]` | Recent flags (hover for details) | `astral.command.logs` |
| `/astral reset <player\|*> [check]` | Reset violations | `astral.command.reset` |
| `/astral bypass <player\|list>` | Toggle anti-cheat bypass for a player (saved) | `astral.command.bypass` |
| `/astral exempt <player\|*> <seconds\|off> [category]` | Skip checks for a while (minigames, battle bus). Console friendly | `astral.command.exempt` |
| `/astral kick <player> [reason]` | Kick with the Astral screen (manual only) | `astral.command.kick` |
| `/astral ban <player> [time] [reason]` | Ban (`30m`, `12h`, `7d`, `1mo`, `perm`) (manual only) | `astral.command.ban` |
| `/astral unban <player>` | Unban | `astral.command.unban` |
| `/astral freeze <player>` | Freeze / unfreeze for a screenshare | `astral.command.freeze` |
| `/astral tp <player>` | Teleport to a player | `astral.command.tp` |
| `/astral cps <player>` | Show a player's CPS in your action bar | `astral.command.cps` |
| `/astral ping <player>` | Show ping | `astral.command.ping` |
| `/astral client <player>` | Client version, protocol, brand and Java/Bedrock | `astral.command.client` |
| `/astral gui` | Staff menu: players sorted by VL, violations, logs, check toggles | `astral.command.gui` |
| `/astral stats` | Uptime, flags, punishments, TPS | `astral.command.stats` |
| `/astral debug <check>` | See every flag of one check | `astral.command.debug` |

## Permissions

| Permission | Default | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `astral.bypass` | false | **Never checked, never flagged, never set back, never blocked.** |
| `astral.bypass.<check>` | false | Bypass one check only, e.g. `astral.bypass.reach` |
| `astral.alerts` | op | Receive flags |
| `astral.verbose` | op | Use verbose / debug output |
| `astral.notify.punish` | op | Get told when someone is punished |
| `astral.admin` | op | Every command and alert (does **not** include bypass) |
| `astral.*` | false | Everything **including** bypass |

> OPs are **not** bypassed by default, so you can test the anti-cheat as OP.
> Give `astral.bypass` (LuckPerms: `/lp user <name> permission set astral.bypass true`) or use
> `/astral bypass <name>` for players who should be ignored.

---

## Checks

The "When blocking" column is what happens with `settings.mitigation: true` (the default). In
watch mode every check only alerts and logs.

| Category | Check | Detects | When blocking |
|---|---|---|---|
| Combat | **Reach** | Hitting further than the vanilla range (lag compensated, spear `attack_range` aware) | Cancels the hit |
| Combat | **Hitbox** | Hits where the crosshair never pointed at the hitbox (hitbox expand, auras without rotations) | Blocks attacks |
| Combat | **KillAura** | Multi (2 targets in 1 tick), **Post** (attack after the movement packet = silent rotations), **Switch** (snapping between targets), NoSwing, Walls | Cancels / blocks attacks |
| Combat | **Aim** | **SilentAim** (flick to the target and straight back), GCD (rotations no mouse sensitivity can make), Robotic (identical steps) | SilentAim / Robotic: hits stop registering for a while. GCD: alert only |
| Combat | **Mace** | **FakeFall**: mace smash with a fall distance the player never fell (extra up/down packets before the hit = mace kill) | Cancels the smash |
| Combat | **CrystalAura** | Breaking an end crystal in the same tick it was placed (predict) | Cancels the hit |
| Combat | **AutoBlock** | Attacking while blocking, eating or drawing a bow | Cancels the hit |
| Combat | **Criticals** | Critical hits while standing on the ground (packet crits) | Cancels the hit |
| Combat | **Velocity** | Anti-knockback (vertical and horizontal) | Flag only |
| Movement | **Flight** | Gravity prediction: fly, glide, air jump, spider, hover, high jump, fast fall | Setback |
| Movement | **Speed** | Friction / acceleration prediction (speed, bhop, long jump, air strafe) | Setback |
| Movement | **NoFall** | On-ground spoofing | Applies the fall damage |
| Movement | **Timer** | Game running faster than 20 ticks/s (client tick balance) | Setback |
| Movement | **Step** | Stepping up more than the step height | Setback |
| Movement | **Jesus** | Walking on water / lava | Setback |
| Movement | **FastClimb** | Climbing faster than 0.1176 blocks/tick | Setback |
| Movement | **ElytraFly** | Elytra hover / climb without speed / impossible speed | Stops gliding + setback |
| Movement | **NoSlow** | Full speed while eating / blocking / sneaking | Setback |
| Movement | **OmniSprint** | Sprinting backwards / sideways (key input) or while starving | Stops sprint + setback |
| Movement | **Phase** | Moving through solid blocks, ending up inside a block (noclip / vclip) | Setback |
| Movement | **BoatFly** | A boat the player drives rising in the air (boats a plugin moves are ignored) | Puts the boat back (nobody is thrown out) |
| Player | **InventoryMove** | Moving / turning with an inventory open, inventory clicks in the same tick as attacks or after the movement packet (inventory macros) | Cancels the click |
| Player | **BadPackets** | Using items, digging or interacting after the movement packet (auto pot, silent rotation macros); more than one movement packet in a tick (rotate-act-rotate back, blink) | Cancels the action |
| Player | **FastBow** | Bow spam | Cancels the shot |
| Player | **ClientBrand** | Known hacked client brands / plugin channels | Kick (block mode only) |
| World | **FastBreak** | Mining faster than vanilla (tool, enchants, haste, water, ground) | Cancels the break |
| World | **Nuker** | Several blocks per tick, too many per second, blocks outside the view | Cancels the break |
| World | **BlockReach** | Breaking / placing too far | Cancels |
| World | **GhostHand** | Breaking / placing through walls | Cancels |
| World | **FastPlace** | Too many blocks per tick / second, robotic timing | Cancels |
| World | **Scaffold** | Air place, clicking hidden faces, placing without looking at the block, placing after the movement packet | Cancels / blocks placing |
| World | **XRay** | Rare ore ratio far above normal | Alert only |
| Macro | **AutoClicker** | Sustained inhuman CPS, and the repeating rhythm of fixed-delay clickers (any CPS) | Hits stop registering for a while |
| Macro | **SilentSwap** | **SilentSwap**: slot switch after an attack / use in the same tick (switch, hit, switch back). **AttributeSwap**: swap to the other weapon in the hit tick and swap back a fixed time later, every time (auto attribute swap). **StunSlam**: axe breaks the shield + swap to the mace in the same tick (auto stun slam), or the mace always exactly 1 tick after the axe. Post / Spam slot switches | The impossible swap is cancelled and the hits after it do not register; a caught attribute swapper / stun slammer's swap hits do not register for 30 s. The 1-tick stun slam streak only alerts (`stun-slam.block-streaks`) |
| Macro | **AutoTotem** | Totem put back in the off-hand a few ticks after popping | Cancels the click |
| Macro | **ChestStealer** | Container looting with perfectly timed shift clicks | Cancels the click |
| Macro | **AutoFish** | Robotic fishing reaction times | Flag only |
| Macro | **ChatSpam** | Spam, duplicate messages, chat macros, command spam | Cancels the message |

### False-flag protection

* Lag protection: no flags while the server TPS is below `settings.lag-protection.min-tps`.
* Exemptions for teleports, knockback, explosions / wind charges, elytra, riptide, vehicles,
  moving entity platforms, pistons, slime / honey / beds, liquids, ladders, cobwebs, powder
  snow, ghost blocks, spears. Windows scale with the player's ping.
* No gravity, slow falling, levitation and gravity attribute changes are simulated, including
  the half-ping delay before the client applies them.
* Every check uses a buffer, so a single odd tick never flags.
* Combat checks only block when the order of packets is impossible for a vanilla client, or a
  pattern repeated many times; players on clients older than 1.21.2 (ViaVersion) are not judged
  by packet order at all.
* The violation level (VL) decays over time (`decay` per check).

---

## Tested against simulated cheat clients

`mvn test` runs the real Astral code (every check, listener and the violation manager)
against simulated players on a voxel world with vanilla physics. Each simulated client sends
its packets in the same order a real client does, so cheats like silent aim, silent swap and
post-packet auras look exactly like they do on a real server.

* **Legit players** (wandering on terrain, bunny hopping, ice tunnels, long falls, human PvP
  fights with knockback, building, mining, crystal PvP, eating while walking, hotbar
  scrolling, inventory use, mace smashes, attribute swapping and stun slams by hand) must
  produce **zero** flags.
* **Battle bus** scenarios at 50 to 800 ms ping (riding the bus, standing on a moving shulker
  floor, teleported / pushed along the route, gravity off, spectator, slow falling parachute,
  elytra drop, launch pads, a boat a plugin lifts) must produce **zero** flags and **zero**
  setbacks with blocking on. The same scenarios against Astral 1.0 fail 10 times (gravity off,
  slow falling, launch pads and teleport streams at high ping), which is the kind of flood
  servers with a battle bus saw.
* **Cheat clients** (flight, speed, bhop, timer, phase, blink, reach, hitbox, kill aura,
  silent aim, silent rotations, silent swap, auto attribute swap, auto stun slam, mace kill,
  auto clicker, auto totem, chest stealer, scaffold, nuker, auto pot and more) must be flagged by
  the right check, and with blocking on their hits must stop registering / they get pulled back.
* **Alerts**: 600 flags in a minute must give at most 3 alert lines.
* **Watch mode** is tested too: a cheater is flagged but never cancelled, set back or kicked.

After a run the full table is written to `target/astral-test-report.md`.

<!-- TEST-RESULTS -->

> These are simulations of how cheat clients behave, not recordings of real clients on a
> real server. They prove the checks react to those behaviours and stay quiet for normal
> play, but tune `config.yml` on your own server too.

---

## Configuration

`config.yml`: global settings plus a section per check with:

```yaml
reach:
  enabled: true
  alert-vl: 2.0        # staff are alerted from this VL
  mitigate: true       # block the cheat (only used when settings.mitigation is true)
  mitigate-vl: 0.0     # VL needed before blocking starts
  punish: false        # auto punish (only used when punishments.enabled is true)
  punish-vl: 25
  punish-commands: []  # e.g. ["astral ban %player% 30d Cheating (%check%)"]
  decay: 2.0           # VL removed every violations.decay-interval-seconds
```

Punish commands support `%player% %uuid% %check% %type% %vl% %ping%`.

`messages.yml`: every message, alert format (with hover and click-to-teleport),
kick/ban screens and the punish broadcast. Colours: `&a` codes and `&#RRGGBB` hex.

Logs are written to `plugins/Astral/logs/<date>.log`.

---

## Notes

* No anti-cheat can catch everything. Use the alerts, `/astral logs`, `/astral info` and
  `/astral gui`, and tune `config.yml` for your server.
* **Aim** and **XRay** are statistical, so treat them as hints and watch the player.

```

`pom.xml`:

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 http://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>me.mrtahadarvish</groupId>
    <artifactId>astral-anticheat</artifactId>
    <version>1.1.0</version>
    <packaging>jar</packaging>

    <name>Astral</name>
    <description>Astral - anti-cheat and anti-macro plugin for Paper 1.21.11 by MrTahaDarvish</description>

    <properties>
        <java.version>21</java.version>
        <maven.compiler.release>21</maven.compiler.release>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
        <paper.version>1.21.11-R0.1-SNAPSHOT</paper.version>
    </properties>

    <repositories>
        <repository>
            <id>papermc</id>
            <url>https://repo.papermc.io/repository/maven-public/</url>
        </repository>
    </repositories>

    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>io.papermc.paper</groupId>
            <artifactId>paper-api</artifactId>
            <version>${paper.version}</version>
            <scope>provided</scope>
        </dependency>

        <!-- tests: simulated server + clients (legit and cheating) -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>
            <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
            <version>5.12.2</version>
            <scope>test</scope>
        </dependency>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/AstralAntiCheat.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.command.AstralCommand;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config.ConfigUpdater;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config.Messages;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config.Settings;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerDataManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.gui.MenuManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.DataListener;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.InteractionListener;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.MovementProcessor;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.AlertManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.BypassManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.FreezeManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.LogManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.ViolationManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.command.PluginCommand;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.plugin.PluginManager;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

/**
 * Astral anti-cheat for Paper 1.21.11, by MrTahaDarvish.
 */
public final class AstralAntiCheat extends JavaPlugin {

    public static final String DISPLAY_NAME = "Astral";
    public static final String AUTHOR = "MrTahaDarvish";

    private Settings settings;
    private Messages messages;
    private LogManager logs;
    private BypassManager bypass;
    private PlayerDataManager data;
    private ViolationManager violations;
    private AlertManager alerts;
    private FreezeManager freeze;
    private CheckManager checks;
    private MenuManager menus;
    private long startTime;
    private int secondsCounter;

    @Override
    public void onEnable() {
        startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        saveDefaultConfig();
        ConfigUpdater.update(this);
        settings = new Settings();
        settings.load(getConfig());
        messages = new Messages(this);
        messages.load();

        logs = new LogManager(this);
        bypass = new BypassManager(this);
        bypass.load();
        data = new PlayerDataManager(this);
        violations = new ViolationManager(this);
        alerts = new AlertManager(this);
        alerts.load();
        freeze = new FreezeManager(this);
        menus = new MenuManager(this);
        checks = new CheckManager(this);
        checks.registerAll();

        PluginManager pluginManager = getServer().getPluginManager();
        pluginManager.registerEvents(new DataListener(this), this);
        pluginManager.registerEvents(new MovementProcessor(this), this);
        pluginManager.registerEvents(new InteractionListener(this), this);
        pluginManager.registerEvents(freeze, this);
        pluginManager.registerEvents(menus, this);

        PluginCommand command = getCommand("astral");
        if (command != null) {
            AstralCommand executor = new AstralCommand(this);
            command.setExecutor(executor);
            command.setTabCompleter(executor);
        }

        // support /reload and plugin managers: create data for players that are already online
        for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            PlayerData created = data.create(player);
            created.lastSafe = player.getLocation();
        }

        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, data::tick, 1L, 1L);
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, this::everySecond, 20L, 20L);
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, alerts::tick, 10L, 10L);
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, freeze::tick, 60L, 60L);
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, this::cpsDisplay, 5L, 5L);

        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color("&8&m                                                  "));
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color(" &#00E5FF&l" + DISPLAY_NAME + " &7v" + getPluginMeta().getVersion()));
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color(" &7Loaded &f" + checks.all().size() + " &7checks &8(&a"
                + checks.enabledCount() + " enabled&8) &7for &f" + Bukkit.getName() + " " + Bukkit.getMinecraftVersion()));
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color(" &7Mode: " + (settings.mitigation ? "&cBLOCK &7(cheats are blocked)" : "&aWATCH &7(alerts only, nobody is kicked or blocked)")));
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color(" &7Bypass permission: &fastral.bypass &8| &7Command: &f/astral"));
        Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color("&8&m                                                  "));
    }

    @Override
    public void onDisable() {
        if (bypass != null) {
            bypass.save();
        }
        if (logs != null) {
            logs.shutdown();
        }
    }

    /** /astral reload */
    public void reload() {
        reloadConfig();
        ConfigUpdater.update(this);
        settings.load(getConfig());
        messages.load();
        alerts.load();
        checks.reload();
        bypass.load();
        for (PlayerData playerData : data.all()) {
            data.refreshBypass(playerData);
        }
    }

    private void everySecond() {
        secondsCounter++;
        if (secondsCounter % settings.decayIntervalSeconds == 0) {
            violations.decay();
        }
    }

    private void cpsDisplay() {
        for (PlayerData staff : data.all()) {
            if (staff.cpsTarget == null) {
                continue;
            }
            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(staff.cpsTarget);
            PlayerData targetData = target == null ? null : data.get(target);
            if (targetData == null) {
                staff.cpsTarget = null;
                continue;
            }
            int cps = targetData.cps();
            String color = cps >= 16 ? "&c" : cps >= 11 ? "&e" : "&a";
            staff.player().sendActionBar(Text.color("&#00E5FFAstral &8» &f" + target.getName() + " &7CPS: " + color + cps
                    + " &8| &7Ping: &f" + targetData.ping() + "ms"));
        }
    }

    public Settings settings() {
        return settings;
    }

    public Messages messages() {
        return messages;
    }

    public LogManager logs() {
        return logs;
    }

    public BypassManager bypass() {
        return bypass;
    }

    public PlayerDataManager data() {
        return data;
    }

    public ViolationManager violations() {
        return violations;
    }

    public AlertManager alerts() {
        return alerts;
    }

    public FreezeManager freeze() {
        return freeze;
    }

    public CheckManager checks() {
        return checks;
    }

    public MenuManager menus() {
        return menus;
    }

    public long startTime() {
        return startTime;
    }
}

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/Check.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection;
import org.bukkit.configuration.MemoryConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Base class of every check. A check listens to Bukkit / Paper events (and / or the client
 * tick through {@link TickCheck}) and calls {@link #flag} when it detects a cheat.
 */
public abstract class Check implements Listener {

    protected final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private final String id;
    private final String name;
    private final CheckCategory category;
    private final String description;

    private boolean enabled = true;
    private double alertVl;
    private boolean mitigate;
    private double mitigateVl;
    private boolean punish;
    private double punishVl;
    private List<String> punishCommands = List.of();
    private double decay;
    private long totalFlags;

    protected ConfigurationSection section = new MemoryConfiguration();

    protected Check(AstralAntiCheat plugin, String id, String name, CheckCategory category, String description) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.id = id;
        this.name = name;
        this.category = category;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public final void load(ConfigurationSection root) {
        ConfigurationSection loaded = root.getConfigurationSection("checks." + id);
        section = loaded != null ? loaded : new MemoryConfiguration();
        enabled = section.getBoolean("enabled", true);
        alertVl = section.getDouble("alert-vl", 1.0);
        mitigate = section.getBoolean("mitigate", true);
        mitigateVl = section.getDouble("mitigate-vl", 0.0);
        punish = section.getBoolean("punish", false);
        punishVl = section.getDouble("punish-vl", 20.0);
        punishCommands = section.getStringList("punish-commands");
        decay = section.getDouble("decay", 1.0);
        onLoad();
    }

    /** Read check specific options here. */
    protected void onLoad() {
    }

    protected double option(String key, double def) {
        return section.getDouble(key, def);
    }

    protected int option(String key, int def) {
        return section.getInt(key, def);
    }

    protected boolean option(String key, boolean def) {
        return section.getBoolean(key, def);
    }

    protected List<String> optionList(String key) {
        return section.getStringList(key);
    }

    protected PlayerData data(Player player) {
        return plugin.data().get(player);
    }

    /**
     * True when this check has to look at the player: check enabled, player not bypassing
     * (astral.bypass / astral.bypass.&lt;check&gt; / /astral bypass), not exempted for a while
     * (/astral exempt), not in an exempt world, not a Bedrock player and not just joined.
     */
    protected boolean shouldCheck(PlayerData data) {
        if (!enabled || data == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if (data.isBypassing(id)) {
            return false;
        }
        if (data.bedrock && plugin.settings().exemptBedrock) {
            return false;
        }
        if (data.isExempt(category)) {
            return false; // /astral exempt
        }
        if (!plugin.settings().exemptWorlds.isEmpty()
                && plugin.settings().exemptWorlds.contains(data.player().getWorld().getName().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT))) {
            return false;
        }
        return PlayerData.since(data.joinTick) > plugin.settings().joinExemptTicks;
    }

    /** Flags with 1 VL. Returns true when the cheat should be blocked (mitigation). */
    protected boolean flag(PlayerData data, String type, String debug) {
        return flag(data, type, 1.0, debug);
    }

    protected boolean flag(PlayerData data, String type, double vl, String debug) {
        return plugin.violations().flag(this, data, type, vl, debug);
    }

    /** Teleports the player back to the last legit position. */
    protected void setback(PlayerData data) {
        plugin.violations().setback(data);
    }

    public void incrementFlags() {
        totalFlags++;
    }

    public String id() {
        return id;
    }

    public String name() {
        return name;
    }

    public CheckCategory category() {
        return category;
    }

    public String description() {
        return description;
    }

    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    public double alertVl() {
        return alertVl;
    }

    /** True when this check may block cheats (also needs settings.mitigation, off = watch only). */
    public boolean mitigate() {
        return mitigate && plugin.settings().mitigation;
    }

    public double mitigateVl() {
        return mitigateVl;
    }

    public boolean punish() {
        return punish;
    }

    public double punishVl() {
        return punishVl;
    }

    public List<String> punishCommands() {
        return punishCommands;
    }

    public double decay() {
        return decay;
    }

    public long totalFlags() {
        return totalFlags;
    }
}

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/CheckCategory.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check;

import org.bukkit.Material;

public enum CheckCategory {
    COMBAT("Combat", "&c", Material.DIAMOND_SWORD),
    MOVEMENT("Movement", "&b", Material.FEATHER),
    PLAYER("Player", "&e", Material.PLAYER_HEAD),
    WORLD("World", "&a", Material.GRASS_BLOCK),
    MACRO("Macro", "&d", Material.REPEATER);

    private final String display;
    private final String color;
    private final Material icon;

    CheckCategory(String display, String color, Material icon) {
        this.display = display;
        this.color = color;
        this.icon = icon;
    }

    public String display() {
        return display;
    }

    public String color() {
        return color;
    }

    public Material icon() {
        return icon;
    }
}

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/CheckManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.AimCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.AutoBlockCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.CriticalsCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.CrystalAuraCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.HitboxCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.KillAuraCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.MaceCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.ReachCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.VelocityCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro.AutoClickerCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro.AutoFishCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro.AutoTotemCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro.ChatCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro.HotbarMacroCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro.InventoryMacroCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.BoatFlyCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.ElytraFlyCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.FastClimbCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.FlightCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.JesusCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.NoFallCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.NoSlowCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.PhaseCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.SpeedCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.SprintCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.StepCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement.TimerCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player.BadPacketsCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player.ClientBrandCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player.FastBowCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player.InventoryMoveCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world.BlockReachCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world.FastBreakCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world.FastPlaceCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world.InteractCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world.NukerCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world.ScaffoldCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world.XRayCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/** Creates, configures and registers every check. */
public final class CheckManager {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private final Map<String, Check> checks = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final List<TickCheck> tickChecks = new ArrayList<>();
    private KillAuraCheck killAura;

    public CheckManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void registerAll() {
        // movement
        add(new TimerCheck(plugin));
        add(new FlightCheck(plugin));
        add(new SpeedCheck(plugin));
        add(new NoFallCheck(plugin));
        add(new StepCheck(plugin));
        add(new JesusCheck(plugin));
        add(new FastClimbCheck(plugin));
        add(new ElytraFlyCheck(plugin));
        add(new NoSlowCheck(plugin));
        add(new SprintCheck(plugin));
        add(new PhaseCheck(plugin));
        add(new BoatFlyCheck(plugin));
        // combat
        add(new ReachCheck(plugin));
        add(new HitboxCheck(plugin));
        killAura = add(new KillAuraCheck(plugin));
        add(new AutoBlockCheck(plugin));
        add(new CriticalsCheck(plugin));
        add(new VelocityCheck(plugin));
        add(new AimCheck(plugin));
        add(new CrystalAuraCheck(plugin));
        add(new MaceCheck(plugin));
        // player
        add(new InventoryMoveCheck(plugin));
        add(new BadPacketsCheck(plugin));
        add(new FastBowCheck(plugin));
        add(new ClientBrandCheck(plugin));
        // world
        add(new FastBreakCheck(plugin));
        add(new NukerCheck(plugin));
        add(new BlockReachCheck(plugin));
        add(new InteractCheck(plugin));
        add(new FastPlaceCheck(plugin));
        add(new ScaffoldCheck(plugin));
        add(new XRayCheck(plugin));
        // macros
        add(new AutoClickerCheck(plugin));
        add(new AutoTotemCheck(plugin));
        add(new InventoryMacroCheck(plugin));
        add(new HotbarMacroCheck(plugin));
        add(new AutoFishCheck(plugin));
        add(new ChatCheck(plugin));

        reload();
        for (Check check : checks.values()) {
            Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(check, plugin);
        }
    }

    private <T extends Check> T add(T check) {
        checks.put(check.id(), check);
        if (check instanceof TickCheck tickCheck) {
            tickChecks.add(tickCheck);
        }
        return check;
    }

    public void reload() {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();
        for (Check check : checks.values()) {
            check.load(config);
        }
    }

    /** Enables / disables a check and saves it to config.yml. */
    public void setEnabled(Check check, boolean enabled) {
        check.setEnabled(enabled);
        plugin.getConfig().set("checks." + check.id() + ".enabled", enabled);
        plugin.saveConfig();
    }

    /** Blocks every attack of the player for a while (after a combat cheat was detected). */
    public void lockCombat(PlayerData data) {
        int ticks = killAura == null ? 60 : killAura.lockTicks();
        data.combatLockUntil = Math.max(data.combatLockUntil, PlayerData.now() + ticks);
    }

    public Check get(String id) {
        if (id == null) {
            return null;
        }
        String key = id.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        Check check = checks.get(key);
        if (check != null) {
            return check;
        }
        for (Check candidate : checks.values()) {
            if (candidate.name().equalsIgnoreCase(id)) {
                return candidate;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public List<Check> all() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(new ArrayList<>(checks.values()));
    }

    public List<TickCheck> tickChecks() {
        return tickChecks;
    }

    public long enabledCount() {
        return checks.values().stream().filter(Check::enabled).count();
    }
}

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/MovementCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.VelocityRecord;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;

/**
 * Base for checks that simulate movement every client tick.
 */
public abstract class MovementCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    protected MovementCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin, String id, String name, String description) {
        super(plugin, id, name, CheckCategory.MOVEMENT, description);
    }

    @Override
    public final void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        int increases = data.bufferIncreases;
        handle(data);
        if (data.bufferIncreases != increases) {
            data.movementSuspicious = true;
        }
    }

    protected abstract void handle(PlayerData data);

    /**
     * Situations where vanilla physics are replaced by something Astral does not simulate
     * (creative flight, elytra, vehicles, teleports, moving entity platforms ...). No gravity and
     * slow falling are simulated, not exempted.
     */
    protected boolean exempt(PlayerData data) {
        return exempt(data, false);
    }

    /** ignoreVehicleExit: still riding counts, the window after getting off does not. */
    protected boolean exempt(PlayerData data, boolean ignoreVehicleExit) {
        Player player = data.player();
        if (data.skipMovement || !data.chunkLoaded || data.inVehicle || data.gliding || data.riptiding || data.flying) {
            return true;
        }
        GameMode mode = player.getGameMode();
        if (mode == GameMode.CREATIVE || mode == GameMode.SPECTATOR || player.isDead() || player.isSleeping()) {
            return true;
        }
        return since(data.lastTeleport) < 5
                || since(data.lastFailMove) < 5
                || since(data.lastFlight) < 40
                || (!ignoreVehicleExit && since(data.lastVehicle) < data.vehicleExitTicks())
                || since(data.lastRiptide) < 40
                || since(data.lastGlide) < 20
                || since(data.lastPiston) < 20
                || since(data.lastRespawn) < 20
                || since(data.lastWorldChange) < 40
                || since(data.lastGameMode) < 20
                || since(data.lastGhostBlock) < 20
                || since(data.lastSpear) < 30
                || since(data.lastEntityPlatform) < 20
                || since(data.lastExplosion) < 30 + data.pingTicks();
    }

    /** True while a knockback the server sent can still arrive at the client. */
    protected boolean velocityPending(PlayerData data) {
        for (VelocityRecord record : data.velocities) {
            if (data.clientTicks - record.clientTick <= record.window + 2) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** Highest horizontal velocity that can currently be applied to the player. */
    protected double pendingHorizontal(PlayerData data) {
        double max = 0.0;
        for (VelocityRecord record : data.velocities) {
            if (data.clientTicks - record.clientTick <= record.window + 2) {
                max = Math.max(max, record.horizontal);
            }
        }
        return max;
    }

    /** True when the vertical movement of this tick equals a pending knockback. */
    protected boolean matchesVelocity(PlayerData data, double deltaY) {
        for (VelocityRecord record : data.velocities) {
            if (data.clientTicks - record.clientTick > record.window + 2) {
                continue;
            }
            if (Math.abs(deltaY - record.y) < 0.01 || Math.abs(deltaY - Math.max(record.y, data.jumpStrength)) < 0.01) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    protected double jumpBoost(Player player) {
        var effect = player.getPotionEffect(org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType.JUMP_BOOST);
        return effect == null ? 0.0 : 0.1 * (effect.getAmplifier() + 1);
    }

    /** Highest jump motion the player can have right now. */
    protected double maxJump(PlayerData data) {
        return data.jumpStrength + jumpBoost(data.player());
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/PacketOrder.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.ClientUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;

/**
 * Packet order inside one client tick. A vanilla client always sends its packets in this order:
 * <pre>
 *   slot change -> attacks / item use / placing / digging -> swing -> movement -> tick end
 * </pre>
 * Inventory clicks happen between two ticks (on the next tick, before the movement). So any
 * action that arrives <b>after</b> the movement packet of the same tick was sent by a modified
 * client: silent rotations (rotate in the movement packet, then attack / place), silent swaps
 * and packet macros all do this.
 */
public final class PacketOrder {

    /** 1.21.2: first protocol where the client sends a tick end packet itself. */
    public static final int NATIVE_TICK_END = 768;

    private PacketOrder() {
    }

    /**
     * True when the client sends real tick end packets (1.21.2+). Older clients joining through
     * ViaVersion have no tick end of their own, so the order inside a tick is not known for them.
     */
    public static boolean reliable(PlayerData data) {
        if (data.protocol < 0) {
            data.protocol = ClientUtil.protocol(data.player());
        }
        return data.protocol >= NATIVE_TICK_END;
    }

    /**
     * True while the packet order can't be trusted: client older than 1.21.2, no tick seen yet,
     * vehicles, or a server side position change (teleport, rejected move, piston, respawn, world
     * change) that makes the client answer with an extra movement packet.
     */
    public static boolean exempt(PlayerData data) {
        return !data.hasTickSnapshot || data.inVehicle || data.skipMovement || !reliable(data)
                || since(data.lastTeleport) <= 40 || since(data.lastFailMove) <= 40 || since(data.lastPiston) <= 20
                || since(data.lastVehicle) <= data.vehicleExitTicks() || since(data.lastRespawn) <= 40 || since(data.lastWorldChange) <= 40;
    }

    /**
     * True when the movement packet of the current client tick was already processed, i.e. the
     * player's position or rotation changed since the last tick end.
     */
    public static boolean isPost(PlayerData data) {
        if (exempt(data)) {
            return false;
        }
        Location location = data.player().getLocation();
        return location.getX() != data.tickEndX || location.getY() != data.tickEndY || location.getZ() != data.tickEndZ
                || location.getYaw() != data.tickEndYaw || location.getPitch() != data.tickEndPitch;
    }
}

package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;

/**
 * A check that runs once every client tick (Paper's ClientTickEndEvent), after Astral updated
 * the movement data of the player.
 */
public interface TickCheck {

    void onTick(PlayerData data);
}

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/AimCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Rotation analysis while fighting.
 * <ul>
 *   <li>GCD - a mouse turns the camera in steps of sensitivity^3 * 1.2 degrees. Rotations
 *       calculated by aimbots / auras do not share that step.</li>
 *   <li>Robotic - perfectly repeated rotation steps (smooth rotation auras).</li>
 *   <li>SilentAim - the rotation jumps to the target for the tick of the hit and then jumps
 *       straight back to where the camera really is. A hand on a mouse never returns to the
 *       exact same angle one tick after a flick.</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public final class AimCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    private boolean gcd;
    private boolean robotic;
    private boolean silentAim;

    public AimCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "aim", "Aim", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Inhuman rotations (aimbot, silent aim, rotation auras).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        gcd = option("gcd", true);
        robotic = option("robotic", true);
        silentAim = option("silent-aim", true);
    }

    private static final class State {
        int smoothTicks;
        int roboticTicks;
        boolean attackedLastTick;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        boolean attackedLastTick = state.attackedLastTick;
        state.attackedLastTick = data.attacksThisTick > 0;
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || !data.inCombat() || data.skipMovement || data.inVehicle
                || since(data.lastTeleport) < 20 || since(data.lastRespawn) < 20) {
            return;
        }
        float yaw = Math.abs(data.deltaYaw);
        float pitch = Math.abs(data.deltaPitch);
        float lastPitch = Math.abs(data.lastDeltaPitch);

        // cinematic camera (smooth zoom / optifine) creates fake inhuman rotations - ignore it
        boolean smooth = yaw > 0.0F && yaw < 1.5F && pitch > 0.0F && pitch < 1.5F;
        state.smoothTicks = smooth ? Math.min(20, state.smoothTicks + 1) : Math.max(0, state.smoothTicks - 1);
        boolean cinematic = state.smoothTicks > 8;

        if (gcd && !cinematic && pitch > 0.5F && pitch < 20.0F && lastPitch > 0.5F && lastPitch < 20.0F
                && Math.abs(data.pitch) < 85.0F) {
            double divisor = MathUtil.gcd(pitch, lastPitch);
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("aim.gcd");
            if (divisor < MathUtil.MIN_SENSITIVITY_STEP * 0.9) {
                if (buffer.increase() > 20.0) {
                    buffer.set(12.0);
                    flag(data, "GCD", "divisor=" + format(divisor, 5) + " dp=" + format(pitch, 4));
                }
            } else {
                buffer.decrease(0.75);
            }
        }

        if (robotic && yaw > 1.0F && data.deltaYaw == data.lastDeltaYaw && data.deltaPitch == data.lastDeltaPitch) {
            if (++state.roboticTicks >= 4) {
                state.roboticTicks = 0;
                if (flag(data, "Robotic", "yaw step=" + format(data.deltaYaw, 4) + " pitch step=" + format(data.deltaPitch, 4))) {
                    plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.roboticTicks = 0;
        }

        // lastDelta = turn towards the target, delta = turn of this tick. The hit happened in one
        // of the two ticks (pre or post order). Silent aim turns back by almost exactly the same amount.
        if (silentAim && (attackedLastTick || data.attacksThisTick > 0)) {
            double in = Math.hypot(data.lastDeltaYaw, data.lastDeltaPitch);
            double out = Math.hypot(data.deltaYaw, data.deltaPitch);
            double rest = Math.hypot(data.lastDeltaYaw + data.deltaYaw, data.lastDeltaPitch + data.deltaPitch);
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("aim.silent");
            if (in >= 8.0 && out >= in * 0.7 && rest <= in * 0.15) {
                if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                    buffer.set(0.5);
                    if (flag(data, "SilentAim", "turned " + format(in, 1) + " deg for the hit and " + format(out, 1)
                            + " deg back (diff " + format(rest, 2) + ")")) {
                        // the hits of a silent aim user do not register for a while
                        plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                buffer.decrease(0.05);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/AutoBlockCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.MovementProcessor;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;

/**
 * A vanilla client throws away every attack click while an item is in use (shield up, eating,
 * drawing a bow). Attacking at the same time is AutoBlock / multi action.
 */
public final class AutoBlockCheck extends Check {

    public AutoBlockCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "autoblock", "AutoBlock", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Attacking while blocking, eating or using an item.");
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || !CombatUtil.isCombatTarget(event.getAttacked())) {
            return;
        }
        String type = null;
        if (player.isBlocking()) {
            type = "Shield";
        } else if (player.hasActiveItem() && player.getActiveItemUsedTime() > 3 && player.getActiveItemRemainingTime() > 3) {
            ItemStack item = player.getActiveItem();
            if (!MovementProcessor.isSpear(item)) {
                type = "Use";
            }
        }
        if (type == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (flag(data, type, "item=" + player.getActiveItem().getType().name().toLowerCase()
                + " used=" + player.getActiveItemUsedTime())) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }
}

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/CombatUtil.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import io.papermc.paper.datacomponent.DataComponentTypes;
import io.papermc.paper.datacomponent.item.AttackRange;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.FluidCollisionMode;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.EnderCrystal;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/** Shared math for combat checks (reach, hitbox, walls). */
public final class CombatUtil {

    private CombatUtil() {
    }

    public static boolean isCombatTarget(Entity entity) {
        return entity instanceof LivingEntity || entity instanceof EnderCrystal;
    }

    /** Vanilla attack range: the entity_interaction_range attribute or the item's attack_range (spears). */
    public static double attackRange(Player player, Entity target) {
        AttributeInstance attribute = player.getAttribute(Attribute.ENTITY_INTERACTION_RANGE);
        double range = attribute == null ? (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE ? 5.0 : 3.0) : attribute.getValue();
        AttackRange attackRange = attackRange(player);
        if (attackRange != null) {
            double max = player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE ? attackRange.maxCreativeReach() : attackRange.maxReach();
            if (!(target instanceof Player)) {
                max *= Math.max(1.0, attackRange.mobFactor());
            }
            range = Math.max(range, max);
        }
        return range;
    }

    public static double hitboxMargin(Player player) {
        AttackRange attackRange = attackRange(player);
        return attackRange == null ? 0.0 : attackRange.hitboxMargin();
    }

    private static AttackRange attackRange(Player player) {
        ItemStack hand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
        if (hand.getType().isAir()) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return hand.getData(DataComponentTypes.ATTACK_RANGE);
        } catch (Throwable throwable) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Possible eye positions of the attacker: the client attacks from the camera of the last
     * rendered frame, which is between the last two client ticks.
     */
    public static List<Vector> eyes(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        List<Vector> eyes = new ArrayList<>(3);
        double eye = player.getEyeHeight();
        eyes.add(player.getEyeLocation().toVector());
        if (data.hasPosition) {
            eyes.add(new Vector(data.x, data.y + eye, data.z));
            eyes.add(new Vector(data.lastX, data.lastY + eye, data.lastZ));
        }
        return eyes;
    }

    /**
     * Lag compensated boxes of the target: where the attacker could have seen the target,
     * based on the attacker's ping.
     */
    public static List<BoundingBox> targetBoxes(AstralAntiCheat plugin, Entity target, PlayerData attacker) {
        int ping = Math.min(Math.max(0, attacker.ping()), plugin.settings().maxPingCompensation);
        int compensation = (int) Math.ceil(ping / 50.0) + 4;
        int now = Bukkit.getCurrentTick();
        List<BoundingBox> boxes = new ArrayList<>();
        if (target instanceof Player targetPlayer) {
            PlayerData targetData = plugin.data().get(targetPlayer);
            if (targetData != null) {
                boxes.addAll(targetData.history.between(now - compensation - 2, now));
            }
            boxes.add(targetPlayer.getBoundingBox());
            return boxes;
        }
        BoundingBox box = target.getBoundingBox().clone();
        double speed = target.getVelocity().length();
        box.expand(Math.min(1.2, 0.1 + speed * compensation));
        boxes.add(box);
        return boxes;
    }

    public static boolean rayHits(Vector eye, Vector direction, double range, BoundingBox box) {
        if (box.contains(eye)) {
            return true;
        }
        RayTraceResult result = box.rayTrace(eye, direction, range);
        return result != null;
    }

    /** True when every line from the eye to the target passes through a full solid block. */
    public static boolean allBlocked(World world, Vector eye, List<BoundingBox> boxes) {
        for (BoundingBox box : boxes) {
            Vector center = box.getCenter();
            double y = center.getY();
            Vector[] points = {
                    center,
                    new Vector(center.getX(), box.getMaxY() - 0.15, center.getZ()),
                    new Vector(center.getX(), box.getMinY() + 0.15, center.getZ()),
                    new Vector(box.getMinX() + 0.05, y, box.getMinZ() + 0.05),
                    new Vector(box.getMaxX() - 0.05, y, box.getMaxZ() - 0.05),
                    new Vector(box.getMinX() + 0.05, y, box.getMaxZ() - 0.05),
                    new Vector(box.getMaxX() - 0.05, y, box.getMinZ() + 0.05)
            };
            for (Vector point : points) {
                if (!blocked(world, eye, point)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    private static boolean blocked(World world, Vector from, Vector to) {
        Vector direction = to.clone().subtract(from);
        double distance = direction.length();
        if (distance < 0.01) {
            return false;
        }
        RayTraceResult result = world.rayTraceBlocks(new Location(world, from.getX(), from.getY(), from.getZ()),
                direction.normalize(), distance, FluidCollisionMode.NEVER, true);
        if (result == null || result.getHitBlock() == null) {
            return false;
        }
        Block block = result.getHitBlock();
        return block.getType().isOccluding() && block.getType().isSolid();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/CriticalsCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Critical hits need the player to fall. Packet criticals fake tiny jumps while the player is
 * really standing on the ground.
 */
public final class CriticalsCheck extends Check {

    public CriticalsCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "criticals", "Criticals", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Dealing critical hits while standing on the ground.");
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player player) || !event.isCritical()) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || data.inLiquid || data.climbing || data.inVehicle || data.inWeb) {
            return;
        }
        BoundingBox box = player.getBoundingBox();
        if (!BlockUtil.isLoaded(player.getWorld(), box)) {
            return;
        }
        boolean ground = BlockUtil.collides(player.getWorld(), box.getMinX(), box.getMinY() - 0.001, box.getMinZ(),
                box.getMaxX(), box.getMinY(), box.getMaxZ());
        if (ground && !BlockUtil.nearCollidable(player)) {
            if (flag(data, "Ground", "fall=" + format(player.getFallDistance(), 3) + " air=" + data.airTicks)) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/CrystalAuraCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.EnderCrystal;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityPlaceEvent;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Crystal aura with "ID predict" / instant break: the crystal is attacked in the same client
 * tick it was placed. A real client can only hit an entity after the server told it the entity
 * exists, which takes at least one tick (plus ping).
 */
public final class CrystalAuraCheck extends Check {

    public CrystalAuraCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "crystalaura", "CrystalAura", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Breaking end crystals before the client could see them (crystal aura).");
    }

    private static final class State {
        /** entity id -> client tick it was placed */
        final Map<Integer, Integer> placed = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(EntityPlaceEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (player == null || !(event.getEntity() instanceof EnderCrystal)) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        state.placed.put(event.getEntity().getEntityId(), data.clientTicks);
        while (state.placed.size() > 16) {
            state.placed.remove(state.placed.keySet().iterator().next());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getAttacked() instanceof EnderCrystal crystal)) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        Integer placedTick = data.state(State.class, State::new).placed.remove(crystal.getEntityId());
        if (placedTick == null) {
            return;
        }
        int ticks = data.clientTicks - placedTick;
        if (ticks <= 0) {
            if (flag(data, "Predict", "hit the crystal in the same tick it was placed")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/HitboxCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The look direction of the attacker must actually point at the target (hitbox expanders,
 * aura without rotations). The client picks its target with the rotation of a rendered frame
 * between two ticks, so the check waits for the next tick and tests every rotation in between.
 */
public final class HitboxCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    private double margin;
    private double maxBuffer;

    public HitboxCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "hitbox", "Hitbox", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Hitting entities without looking at their hitbox.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        margin = option("margin", 0.1);
        maxBuffer = option("buffer", 3.0);
    }

    private record Pending(List<Vector> eyes, List<BoundingBox> boxes, double range,
                           float yaw, float pitch, float previousYaw, float previousPitch) {
    }

    private static final class State {
        final List<Pending> pending = new ArrayList<>();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        Entity target = event.getAttacked();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || !CombatUtil.isCombatTarget(target) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || data.ping() > plugin.settings().maxCombatPing || data.inVehicle || !target.getWorld().equals(player.getWorld())) {
            return;
        }
        double expand = margin + CombatUtil.hitboxMargin(player);
        List<BoundingBox> boxes = new ArrayList<>();
        for (BoundingBox box : CombatUtil.targetBoxes(plugin, target, data)) {
            boxes.add(box.clone().expand(expand));
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (state.pending.size() < 10) {
            state.pending.add(new Pending(CombatUtil.eyes(player, data), boxes, CombatUtil.attackRange(player, target) + 1.0,
                    player.getLocation().getYaw(), player.getLocation().getPitch(), data.lastYaw, data.lastPitch));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (state.pending.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        List<Pending> pending = new ArrayList<>(state.pending);
        state.pending.clear();
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || PlayerData.since(data.lastTeleport) < 10) {
            return;
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("hitbox");
        for (Pending hit : pending) {
            if (hits(hit, data.yaw, data.pitch)) {
                buffer.decrease(0.5);
                continue;
            }
            if (buffer.increase() > maxBuffer) {
                if (flag(data, "Miss", "yaw=" + MathUtil.format(data.yaw, 1) + " pitch=" + MathUtil.format(data.pitch, 1))) {
                    plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean hits(Pending hit, float endYaw, float endPitch) {
        float[][] rotations = new float[7][];
        rotations[0] = new float[]{hit.previousYaw, hit.previousPitch};
        rotations[1] = new float[]{hit.yaw, hit.pitch};
        rotations[2] = new float[]{endYaw, endPitch};
        for (int i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
            float t = i / 5.0F;
            rotations[2 + i] = new float[]{MathUtil.lerpAngle(hit.yaw, endYaw, t), hit.pitch + (endPitch - hit.pitch) * t};
        }
        for (float[] rotation : rotations) {
            Vector direction = MathUtil.direction(rotation[0], rotation[1]);
            for (Vector eye : hit.eyes) {
                for (BoundingBox box : hit.boxes) {
                    if (CombatUtil.rayHits(eye, direction, hit.range, box)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/KillAuraCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.PacketOrder;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.MovementProcessor;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.List;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;

/**
 * KillAura / ForceField / TriggerBot:
 * <ul>
 *   <li>Multi - hitting two different entities in the same client tick (impossible with a mouse)</li>
 *   <li>Post - the attack is sent after the movement packet of the tick. A vanilla client always
 *       attacks first and moves after, silent rotation auras do it the other way around</li>
 *   <li>NoSwing - attacking without swinging the arm</li>
 *   <li>Walls - attacking through full solid blocks</li>
 *   <li>Switch - hitting two entities far apart from each other in back-to-back ticks</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public final class KillAuraCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    private boolean multi;
    private boolean post;
    private boolean noSwing;
    private boolean walls;
    private boolean switchCheck;
    private int lockTicks;

    public KillAuraCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "killaura", "KillAura", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Automatic attacking (aura, forcefield, triggerbot, multi aura).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        multi = option("multi", true);
        post = option("post", true);
        noSwing = option("no-swing", true);
        walls = option("walls", true);
        switchCheck = option("switch", true);
        lockTicks = option("block-attacks-ticks", 60);
    }

    public int lockTicks() {
        return lockTicks;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        Entity target = event.getAttacked();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || !CombatUtil.isCombatTarget(target) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || data.ping() > plugin.settings().maxCombatPing) {
            return;
        }

        if (post) {
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("killaura.post");
            if (PacketOrder.isPost(data)) {
                if (buffer.increase() > 2.0) {
                    buffer.set(1.0);
                    if (fail(data, "Post", "attack sent after the movement packet (silent rotations)")) {
                        event.setCancelled(true);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                buffer.decrease(0.1);
            }
        }

        if (switchCheck) {
            checkSwitch(event, player, target, data);
        }

        if (walls && !data.recentlyRode() && target.getWorld().equals(player.getWorld())
                && !MovementProcessor.isSpear(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand())) {
            List<BoundingBox> boxes = CombatUtil.targetBoxes(plugin, target, data);
            Vector eye = player.getEyeLocation().toVector();
            if (CombatUtil.allBlocked(player.getWorld(), eye, boxes)) {
                Buffer buffer = data.buffer("killaura.walls");
                if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                    if (fail(data, "Walls", "hit " + target.getType().name().toLowerCase() + " through a wall")) {
                        event.setCancelled(true);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        if (data.attacksThisTick == 0 || !shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        if (multi && data.targetsThisTick.size() >= 2) {
            fail(data, "Multi", "targets=" + data.targetsThisTick.size() + " in one tick");
        }
        if (noSwing && data.swingsThisTick == 0 && since(data.lastSpear) > 10) {
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("killaura.noswing");
            if (buffer.increase() > 2.0) {
                buffer.set(1.0);
                fail(data, "NoSwing", "attacks=" + data.attacksThisTick + " swings=0");
            }
        } else if (data.swingsThisTick > 0) {
            data.buffer("killaura.noswing").decrease(0.25);
        }
    }

    private static final class State {
        int lastTarget = -1;
        int lastAttackTick = -1000;
        Vector lastDirection;
    }

    /**
     * Switch aura: hitting two different entities in back-to-back ticks that are far apart from
     * each other (a mouse can not turn 100+ degrees between two ticks and hit both).
     */
    private void checkSwitch(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event, Player player, Entity target, PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        Vector eye = player.getEyeLocation().toVector();
        Vector direction = target.getBoundingBox().getCenter().subtract(eye);
        int ticks = data.clientTicks - state.lastAttackTick;
        if (state.lastDirection != null && state.lastTarget != target.getEntityId() && ticks <= 1
                && direction.lengthSquared() > 1.0 && state.lastDirection.lengthSquared() > 1.0) {
            double angle = MathUtil.angle(direction, state.lastDirection);
            if (angle > 100.0) {
                Buffer buffer = data.buffer("killaura.switch");
                if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                    buffer.set(0.5);
                    if (fail(data, "Switch", "switched " + MathUtil.format(angle, 1) + " deg between targets in " + ticks + " tick(s)")) {
                        event.setCancelled(true);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        state.lastTarget = target.getEntityId();
        state.lastAttackTick = data.clientTicks;
        state.lastDirection = direction;
    }

    private boolean fail(PlayerData data, String type, String debug) {
        boolean mitigate = flag(data, type, debug);
        if (mitigate) {
            plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
        }
        return mitigate;
    }
}
