`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/MaceCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Mace damage grows with the fall distance, and the server adds up the fall distance from every
 * movement packet. "Mace kill" cheats send extra position packets (up, then back down) right
 * before the hit, so the server thinks the player fell from very high and the smash one-shots
 * the target.
 * <p>
 * Astral follows the real position once per client tick and knows how far the player can have
 * fallen. A mace hit with a much bigger server fall distance is cancelled.
 */
public final class MaceCheck extends Check {

    private double tolerance;

    public MaceCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "mace", "Mace", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Mace smashes with a faked fall distance (mace kill).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        tolerance = option("tolerance", 1.5);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() != Material.MACE) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || !data.hasTickSnapshot) {
            return;
        }
        double server = player.getFallDistance();
        if (server <= 1.5) {
            return; // no smash attack
        }
        // moved down after the last tick end (attack sent after the movement packet)
        double pending = Math.max(0.0, data.tickEndY - player.getLocation().getY());
        double real = data.legitFall + pending;
        if (server > real + tolerance + real * 0.05) {
            if (flag(data, "FakeFall", "server fall=" + format(server, 2) + " real fall=" + format(real, 2))) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/ReachCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.List;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Lag compensated reach: distance from the attacker's eyes to the closest point of every box
 * the target had during the attacker's ping. Uses the real vanilla attack range (attribute,
 * creative bonus and the attack_range component of 1.21.11 spears).
 */
public final class ReachCheck extends Check {

    private double tolerance;
    private double maxBuffer;

    public ReachCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "reach", "Reach", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Hitting entities from further away than vanilla allows.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        tolerance = option("tolerance", 0.1);
        maxBuffer = option("buffer", 2.0);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        Entity target = event.getAttacked();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || !CombatUtil.isCombatTarget(target) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR
                || data.ping() > plugin.settings().maxCombatPing || !target.getWorld().equals(player.getWorld())) {
            return;
        }
        double range = CombatUtil.attackRange(player, target);
        double margin = CombatUtil.hitboxMargin(player);
        List<Vector> eyes = CombatUtil.eyes(player, data);
        List<BoundingBox> boxes = CombatUtil.targetBoxes(plugin, target, data);

        double best = Double.MAX_VALUE;
        for (BoundingBox box : boxes) {
            BoundingBox expanded = margin > 0.0 ? box.clone().expand(margin) : box;
            for (Vector eye : eyes) {
                best = Math.min(best, MathUtil.distanceToBox(eye, expanded));
            }
        }

        // the client sees its own vehicle late: its eyes trail the seat by (ping + lerp) x vehicle speed
        boolean riding = data.recentlyRode();
        double seatLag = riding ? Math.min(6.0, (data.deltaXZ + Math.abs(data.deltaY)) * (data.pingTicks() + 4)) : 0.0;
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("reach");
        if (best > range + tolerance + seatLag) {
            boolean cancel = !riding && best > range + tolerance + 0.3;
            if (buffer.increase() >= maxBuffer) {
                if (flag(data, best > range + 1.0 ? "Blatant" : "Distance",
                        "reach=" + format(best) + " max=" + format(range) + " ping=" + data.ping())) {
                    cancel = true;
                }
            }
            if (cancel && mitigate()) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.5);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/combat/VelocityCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.VelocityRecord;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Anti-KnockBack. After the server sends knockback, the client has to move with it within
 * its ping (+ a few ticks). Vertical knockback is exact (the first tick moves exactly the
 * sent motion), horizontal knockback is checked with a ratio.
 */
public final class VelocityCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    private double minVertical;
    private double minHorizontal;

    public VelocityCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "velocity", "Velocity", CheckCategory.COMBAT, "Taking less knockback than vanilla (anti-KB).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        minVertical = option("min-vertical", 0.98);
        minHorizontal = option("min-horizontal", 0.35);
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        if (data.velocities.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        boolean check = shouldCheck(data);
        boolean invalid = !check || data.skipMovement || data.headBlocked || data.inWeb || data.inLiquid || data.climbing
                || data.nearPowderSnow || data.nearHoney || data.nearSlime || data.nearScaffolding || data.gliding
                || data.inVehicle || data.flying || data.riptiding || since(data.lastTeleport) < 5
                || since(data.lastLevitation) < 5 || since(data.lastPiston) < 10 || since(data.lastFlight) < 20
                || since(data.lastExplosion) < 3
                || data.player().isDead();

        for (VelocityRecord record : data.velocities) {
            int age = data.clientTicks - record.clientTick;
            if (age < 1 || record.evaluated) {
                continue;
            }
            if (invalid) {
                record.invalid = true;
            }
            if (data.horizontalCollision || since(data.lastCollision) < 2) {
                record.horizontalInvalid = true;
            }
            record.maxDeltaY = Math.max(record.maxDeltaY, data.deltaY);
            record.maxDeltaXZ = Math.max(record.maxDeltaXZ, data.deltaXZ);
            if (data.deltaY >= record.y * minVertical - 0.003) {
                record.verticalTaken = true;
            }
            if (data.deltaXZ >= record.horizontal * minHorizontal) {
                record.horizontalTaken = true;
            }
            if (age < record.window) {
                continue;
            }
            record.evaluated = true;
            if (record.invalid || !check || overlaps(data, record)) {
                continue;
            }
            if (record.checkVertical && !record.verticalTaken) {
                double ratio = record.y <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : record.maxDeltaY / record.y;
                flag(data, "Vertical", "taken=" + format(Math.max(0.0, ratio) * 100.0, 1) + "% kb=" + format(record.y));
            } else if (record.checkHorizontal && !record.horizontalTaken && !record.horizontalInvalid) {
                Buffer buffer = data.buffer("velocity.horizontal");
                if (buffer.increase() > 2.0) {
                    buffer.set(1.0);
                    double ratio = record.maxDeltaXZ / record.horizontal;
                    flag(data, "Horizontal", "taken=" + format(ratio * 100.0, 1) + "% kb=" + format(record.horizontal));
                }
            } else if (record.checkHorizontal && record.horizontalTaken) {
                data.buffer("velocity.horizontal").decrease(0.5);
            }
        }
    }

    /** A second knockback inside the window replaces the first one on the client - skip both. */
    private boolean overlaps(PlayerData data, VelocityRecord record) {
        for (VelocityRecord other : data.velocities) {
            boolean verified = other.checkVertical || other.checkHorizontal;
            if (other != record && verified && Math.abs(other.clientTick - record.clientTick) <= record.window) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/macro/AutoClickerCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Click analysis on client ticks (immune to network jitter):
 * <ul>
 *   <li>CPS - clicking faster than a human for a whole second</li>
 *   <li>Pattern - an auto clicker / macro with a fixed delay repeats the same few 8-click
 *       rhythms. Thresholds come from simulating human click timing on 20 TPS ticks.</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public final class AutoClickerCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    private int maxCps;
    private int cancelCps;
    private int maxPatterns;

    public AutoClickerCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "autoclicker", "AutoClicker", CheckCategory.MACRO, "Auto clickers and click macros.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        maxCps = option("max-cps", 22);
        cancelCps = option("cancel-cps", 30);
        maxPatterns = option("max-patterns", 9);
    }

    private static final class State {
        final Deque<Integer> delays = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int ticksSinceClick = 100;
        int newSamples;
        int highCpsTicks;
        int patternWindows;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        state.ticksSinceClick++;
        if (data.clicksThisTick > 0) {
            if (state.ticksSinceClick <= 10) {
                add(state, state.ticksSinceClick);
            }
            for (int i = 1; i < data.clicksThisTick; i++) {
                add(state, 0);
            }
            state.ticksSinceClick = 0;
        }
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }

        int cps = data.cps();
        if (cps > maxCps) {
            if (++state.highCpsTicks >= 20) {
                state.highCpsTicks = 0;
                if (flag(data, "CPS", "cps=" + cps + " max=" + maxCps)) {
                    plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.highCpsTicks = Math.max(0, state.highCpsTicks - 2);
        }

        if (state.newSamples >= 50 && state.delays.size() >= 100) {
            state.newSamples = 0;
            double mean = MathUtil.mean(state.delays);
            double averageCps = mean <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : 20.0 / mean;
            if (averageCps < 8.0) {
                state.patternWindows = 0;
                return;
            }
            int patterns = distinctPatterns(state.delays);
            // a steady human at ~20 CPS can look rhythmic on 20 TPS ticks, so ask for more proof there
            int needed = averageCps > 17.0 ? 4 : 2;
            if (patterns <= maxPatterns) {
                if (++state.patternWindows >= needed) {
                    state.patternWindows = 1;
                    if (flag(data, "Pattern", "patterns=" + patterns + " cps=" + format(averageCps, 1)
                            + " dev=" + format(MathUtil.deviation(state.delays), 2))) {
                        plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                state.patternWindows = 0;
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Number of different 8-click delay sequences. Any clicker with a fixed delay produces a
     * "Sturmian" sequence on 20 TPS ticks, which has at most 9 different 8-click sequences.
     * Human timing noise creates far more (simulated: 0% of human windows below 10 except
     * extremely steady 20 CPS clicking).
     */
    private static int distinctPatterns(Deque<Integer> delays) {
        Integer[] values = delays.toArray(new Integer[0]);
        Set<Long> patterns = new HashSet<>();
        for (int i = 0; i + 8 <= values.length; i++) {
            long key = 0L;
            for (int j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
                key = key * 16L + Math.min(15, values[i + j]);
            }
            patterns.add(key);
        }
        return patterns.size();
    }

    private void add(State state, int delay) {
        state.delays.addLast(delay);
        while (state.delays.size() > 100) {
            state.delays.removeFirst();
        }
        state.newSamples++;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (shouldCheck(data) && mitigate() && data.cps() > cancelCps) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/macro/AutoFishCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerFishEvent;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * AFK fishing macros react to every bite with (almost) the same delay. Humans do not.
 */
public final class AutoFishCheck extends Check {

    public AutoFishCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "autofish", "AutoFish", CheckCategory.MACRO, "Fishing macros with robotic reaction times.");
    }

    private static final class State {
        int biteTick = -1;
        final Deque<Integer> reactions = new ArrayDeque<>();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onFish(PlayerFishEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        switch (event.getState()) {
            case BITE -> state.biteTick = data.clientTicks;
            case CAUGHT_FISH -> {
                if (state.biteTick < 0) {
                    return;
                }
                int reaction = data.clientTicks - state.biteTick;
                state.biteTick = -1;
                state.reactions.addLast(reaction);
                while (state.reactions.size() > 10) {
                    state.reactions.removeFirst();
                }
                if (state.reactions.size() >= 8 && shouldCheck(data)) {
                    double deviation = MathUtil.deviation(state.reactions);
                    double mean = MathUtil.mean(state.reactions);
                    if (deviation < 0.75 && mean < 15.0) {
                        flag(data, "Reaction", "avg=" + format(mean, 1) + " ticks dev=" + format(deviation, 2));
                        state.reactions.clear();
                    }
                }
            }
            case FAILED_ATTEMPT, REEL_IN, IN_GROUND, CAUGHT_ENTITY -> state.biteTick = -1;
            default -> {
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/macro/AutoTotemCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityResurrectEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.ClickType;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.PlayerInventory;

/**
 * AutoTotem: after a totem pops, a human needs to open the inventory, find a totem and move it.
 * Putting a new totem in the off-hand through the inventory a few ticks after the pop is a macro.
 */
public final class AutoTotemCheck extends Check {

    private int minTicks;

    public AutoTotemCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "autototem", "AutoTotem", CheckCategory.MACRO, "Re-equipping totems faster than humanly possible.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        minTicks = option("min-ticks", 4);
    }

    private static final class State {
        int lastPop = -10_000;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onResurrect(EntityResurrectEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = data(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.state(State.class, State::new).lastPop = data.clientTicks;
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        boolean totemToOffhand = false;
        if (event.getClick() == ClickType.SWAP_OFFHAND && isTotem(event.getCurrentItem())) {
            totemToOffhand = true;
        } else if (event.getClickedInventory() instanceof PlayerInventory && event.getSlot() == 40 && isTotem(event.getCursor())) {
            totemToOffhand = true;
        }
        if (!totemToOffhand) {
            return;
        }
        int ticks = data.clientTicks - data.state(State.class, State::new).lastPop;
        if (ticks <= minTicks) {
            if (flag(data, "Speed", "totem re-equipped " + ticks + " ticks after pop")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean isTotem(ItemStack item) {
        return item != null && item.getType() == Material.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/macro/ChatCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.AsyncChatEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Clock;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Chat spam, duplicate messages, chat macros (messages sent with a robotic interval) and
 * command spam. Chat is asynchronous, so this check keeps its own thread safe state.
 */
public final class ChatCheck extends Check {

    private int maxMessages;
    private long windowMs;
    private long duplicateMs;
    private int maxCommands;

    private final Map<UUID, ChatState> states = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public ChatCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "chat", "ChatSpam", CheckCategory.MACRO, "Chat spam, duplicate messages, chat macros and command spam.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        maxMessages = option("max-messages", 5);
        windowMs = (long) (option("window-seconds", 6.0) * 1000L);
        duplicateMs = (long) (option("duplicate-seconds", 20.0) * 1000L);
        maxCommands = option("max-commands-per-second", 5);
    }

    private static final class ChatState {
        final Deque<Long> messages = new ArrayDeque<>();
        final Deque<Long> intervals = new ArrayDeque<>();
        final Deque<Long> commands = new ArrayDeque<>();
        String lastMessage = "";
        long lastTime;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOW, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onChat(AsyncChatEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        String message = Text.plain(event.message()).trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        long now = Clock.millis();
        ChatState state = states.computeIfAbsent(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId(), uuid -> new ChatState());
        String type = null;
        String debug = "";
        synchronized (state) {
            state.messages.addLast(now);
            while (!state.messages.isEmpty() && now - state.messages.peekFirst() > windowMs) {
                state.messages.removeFirst();
            }
            if (state.lastTime > 0L && now - state.lastTime < 60_000L) {
                state.intervals.addLast(now - state.lastTime);
                while (state.intervals.size() > 5) {
                    state.intervals.removeFirst();
                }
            }
            if (state.messages.size() > maxMessages) {
                type = "Spam";
                debug = state.messages.size() + " messages in " + (windowMs / 1000) + "s";
            } else if (duplicateMs > 0L && message.length() > 3 && message.equals(state.lastMessage) && now - state.lastTime < duplicateMs) {
                type = "Duplicate";
                debug = "same message twice";
            } else if (state.intervals.size() == 5) {
                long min = Long.MAX_VALUE;
                long max = Long.MIN_VALUE;
                for (long interval : state.intervals) {
                    min = Math.min(min, interval);
                    max = Math.max(max, interval);
                }
                if (max - min < 120L && max < 30_000L) {
                    type = "Macro";
                    debug = "5 messages exactly ~" + (max / 1000.0) + "s apart";
                    state.intervals.clear();
                }
            }
            state.lastMessage = message;
            state.lastTime = now;
        }
        if (type != null && flag(data, type, debug)) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOW, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onCommand(PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        long now = Clock.millis();
        ChatState state = states.computeIfAbsent(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId(), uuid -> new ChatState());
        int count;
        synchronized (state) {
            state.commands.addLast(now);
            while (!state.commands.isEmpty() && now - state.commands.peekFirst() > 1000L) {
                state.commands.removeFirst();
            }
            count = state.commands.size();
        }
        if (count > maxCommands && flag(data, "Commands", count + " commands in 1s")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        states.remove(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/macro/HotbarMacroCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.PacketOrder;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemHeldEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

/**
 * Silent swap / swap macros (auto attribute swap, auto stun slam, mace swap, auto weapon,
 * shield breaker).
 * <p>
 * A vanilla client reads the number keys before attacks and item use, and mouse-wheel scrolling
 * happens between ticks. So within one client tick it always sends: slot change -> attacks.
 * <ul>
 *   <li>SilentSwap - a slot change after an attack / item use / block click in the same tick
 *       (switch, hit, switch back). Impossible for vanilla: the swap is cancelled and the rest of
 *       the hits of that tick do not register.</li>
 *   <li>Post - a slot change after the movement packet of the tick.</li>
 *   <li>Spam - more than two slot changes in one tick.</li>
 *   <li>AttributeSwap - "swap to the other weapon in the same tick as the hit, swap back a fixed
 *       time later", every single time. Humans land the swap in the same tick as the click about
 *       half of the time and swap back with a varying delay.</li>
 *   <li>StunSlam - axe hit that disables a shield and a swap to the mace for the smash in the same
 *       tick (impossible, blocked right away), or the mace hit exactly one tick after the axe hit
 *       for many stun slams in a row (alert only by default: very fast players get one tick now
 *       and then).</li>
 * </ul>
 * Once a player is caught, his swap hits / stun slam hits do not register for a while.
 */
public final class HotbarMacroCheck extends Check {

    private static final int NEVER = -100_000;

    private int samplesNeeded;
    private int maxSwapBackDelay;
    private int swapBackSpread;
    private double minSameTickRatio;
    private int stunSlamCount;
    private boolean blockStunSlamStreaks;
    private int blockTicks;

    public HotbarMacroCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "hotbarmacro", "SilentSwap", CheckCategory.MACRO,
                "Silent hotbar swapping (auto attribute swap, auto stun slam, mace swap, auto weapon).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        samplesNeeded = Math.max(3, option("attribute-swap.samples", 6));
        maxSwapBackDelay = Math.max(1, option("attribute-swap.max-swap-back-ticks", 10));
        swapBackSpread = Math.max(0, option("attribute-swap.max-spread-ticks", 0));
        minSameTickRatio = option("attribute-swap.min-same-tick-ratio", 0.9);
        stunSlamCount = Math.max(3, option("stun-slam.in-a-row", 8));
        blockStunSlamStreaks = option("stun-slam.block-streaks", false);
        blockTicks = Math.max(20, option("block-ticks", 600));
    }

    private static final class State {
        /** Client tick of a slot change that came after an action (rest of the tick is blocked). */
        int silentSwapTick = NEVER;
        int lastChangeTick = NEVER;
        int lastFromSlot = -1;
        boolean changeBeforeAction;

        /** A swap hit waiting for the swap back. */
        boolean pending;
        int pendingTick;
        int pendingFromSlot;
        final Deque<Integer> swapBackDelays = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int switchAttacks;
        int sameTickAttacks;
        int windows;
        int attributeSwapperUntil = NEVER;

        int axeTick = NEVER;
        int axeTarget = -1;
        int fastStunSlams;
        int stunSlammerUntil = NEVER;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onHeld(PlayerItemHeldEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.slotChangesThisTick++;
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        boolean reliable = PacketOrder.reliable(data);
        String slots = event.getPreviousSlot() + "->" + event.getNewSlot();
        boolean cancel = false;

        if (data.actionsThisTick > 0 && reliable) {
            // switch, hit, switch back in one tick: vanilla can not do this
            state.silentSwapTick = data.clientTicks;
            ItemStack next = event.getPlayer().getInventory().getItem(event.getNewSlot());
            boolean stunSlam = state.axeTick == data.clientTicks && next != null && next.getType() == Material.MACE;
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("hotbarmacro.swap");
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                buffer.set(0.5);
                boolean mitigated = stunSlam
                        ? flag(data, "StunSlam", "shield broken with the axe, swapped " + slots + " to the mace in the same tick")
                        : flag(data, "SilentSwap", "slot " + slots + " after " + data.actionsThisTick + " action(s) in the same tick");
                if (mitigated) {
                    plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                    if (stunSlam) {
                        state.stunSlammerUntil = PlayerData.now() + blockTicks;
                    }
                }
            }
            cancel = mitigate();
        } else if (PacketOrder.isPost(data)) {
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("hotbarmacro.post");
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                buffer.set(0.5);
                cancel = flag(data, "Post", "slot " + slots + " sent after the movement packet");
            }
        }
        if (data.slotChangesThisTick > 2) {
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("hotbarmacro.spam");
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                buffer.set(0.5);
                cancel |= flag(data, "Spam", "slot changes=" + data.slotChangesThisTick + " in one tick");
            }
        }

        // swap back after a swap hit (attribute swap macros)
        if (state.pending && event.getNewSlot() == state.pendingFromSlot) {
            state.pending = false;
            int delay = data.clientTicks - state.pendingTick;
            if (delay > 0 && delay <= maxSwapBackDelay) {
                addSwapBack(data, state, delay);
            }
        }

        state.lastChangeTick = data.clientTicks;
        state.lastFromSlot = event.getPreviousSlot();
        state.changeBeforeAction = data.actionsThisTick == 0;
        if (cancel) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    // not ignoreCancelled: an axe hit that was already blocked still tells what the macro does next
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        int tick = data.clientTicks;
        if (event.isCancelled()) {
            ItemStack hand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
            if (hand.getType().name().endsWith("_AXE") && event.getAttacked() instanceof Player victim && victim.isBlocking()) {
                state.axeTick = tick;
                state.axeTarget = victim.getEntityId();
            }
            return;
        }

        // hits after an impossible swap in the same tick never count
        if (mitigate() && state.silentSwapTick == tick) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            return;
        }

        // ---- attribute swap
        boolean swappedThisTick = state.lastChangeTick == tick && state.changeBeforeAction;
        if (tick - state.lastChangeTick <= 3 && data.attacksThisTick <= 1) {
            state.switchAttacks++;
            if (swappedThisTick) {
                state.sameTickAttacks++;
            }
        }
        if (swappedThisTick && data.attacksThisTick <= 1) {
            state.pending = true;
            state.pendingTick = tick;
            state.pendingFromSlot = state.lastFromSlot;
            if (mitigate() && PlayerData.now() < state.attributeSwapperUntil) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
        }

        // ---- stun slam: axe breaks the shield, the mace smashes one tick later
        ItemStack hand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
        Entity target = event.getAttacked();
        Material type = hand.getType();
        if (type.name().endsWith("_AXE") && target instanceof Player victim && victim.isBlocking()) {
            state.axeTick = tick;
            state.axeTarget = target.getEntityId();
        } else if (type == Material.MACE && target.getEntityId() == state.axeTarget && tick - state.axeTick <= 6) {
            int delay = tick - state.axeTick;
            state.axeTarget = -1;
            // humans need 2+ ticks now and then, a macro is always exactly one tick later
            state.fastStunSlams = delay <= 1 ? state.fastStunSlams + 1 : 0;
            if (state.fastStunSlams >= stunSlamCount) {
                state.fastStunSlams = 0;
                // very fast players manage one tick now and then, so this only alerts by default
                if (flag(data, "StunSlam", stunSlamCount + " stun slams in a row with the mace hit exactly "
                        + delay + " tick after the axe hit") && blockStunSlamStreaks) {
                    state.stunSlammerUntil = PlayerData.now() + blockTicks;
                    plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                }
            }
            if (mitigate() && PlayerData.now() < state.stunSlammerUntil) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }

    private void addSwapBack(PlayerData data, State state, int delay) {
        state.swapBackDelays.addLast(delay);
        if (state.swapBackDelays.size() < samplesNeeded) {
            return;
        }
        int min = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        int max = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        for (int value : state.swapBackDelays) {
            min = Math.min(min, value);
            max = Math.max(max, value);
        }
        double ratio = state.switchAttacks == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) state.sameTickAttacks / state.switchAttacks;
        state.swapBackDelays.clear();
        state.switchAttacks = 0;
        state.sameTickAttacks = 0;
        if (max - min <= swapBackSpread && ratio >= minSameTickRatio) {
            if (++state.windows >= 2) {
                state.windows = 1;
                if (flag(data, "AttributeSwap", "swap in the same tick as the hit (" + Math.round(ratio * 100)
                        + "%), swap back after " + min + (max != min ? "-" + max : "") + " ticks every time")) {
                    state.attributeSwapperUntil = PlayerData.now() + blockTicks;
                    plugin.checks().lockCombat(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.windows = 0;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/macro/InventoryMacroCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.macro;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.ClickType;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryType;
import org.bukkit.inventory.Inventory;

/**
 * ChestStealer / inventory macros: a long row of shift clicks out of a container with the exact
 * same delay every time.
 */
public final class InventoryMacroCheck extends Check {

    private int minSequence;

    public InventoryMacroCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "inventorymacro", "ChestStealer", CheckCategory.MACRO, "Looting containers with a macro (chest stealer).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        minSequence = option("min-sequence", 9);
    }

    private static final class State {
        int lastClick = -10_000;
        int lastDelay = -1;
        int same;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {
            return;
        }
        Inventory top = event.getView().getTopInventory();
        InventoryType type = top.getType();
        if (type == InventoryType.CRAFTING || type == InventoryType.CREATIVE || type == InventoryType.PLAYER) {
            return;
        }
        if (event.getClick() != ClickType.SHIFT_LEFT && event.getClick() != ClickType.SHIFT_RIGHT) {
            return;
        }
        if (event.getClickedInventory() == null || !event.getClickedInventory().equals(top)) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        int delay = data.clientTicks - state.lastClick;
        state.lastClick = data.clientTicks;
        if (delay >= 1 && delay <= 6 && delay == state.lastDelay) {
            state.same++;
        } else {
            state.same = 0;
        }
        state.lastDelay = delay;
        if (state.same >= minSequence - 1) {
            state.same = 0;
            if (flag(data, "Steal", minSequence + " clicks with exactly " + delay + " tick delay")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/BoatFlyCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Boat;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

import java.util.List;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Boats can not rise in the air (BoatFly / vehicle fly).
 * <p>
 * Only movement the player's own client wrote is judged: Paper fires PlayerMoveEvent for a
 * passenger only from the client's vehicle move packet. Boats a plugin moves (battle bus, rides,
 * no gravity, teleports) are therefore never flagged. When blocking, the boat is put back where
 * it was last supported - nobody is thrown out of it.
 */
public final class BoatFlyCheck extends Check {

    public BoatFlyCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "boatfly", "BoatFly", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT, "Flying with a boat.");
    }

    private static final class State {
        int boatId = -1;
        double lastY;
        int riseTicks;
        int serverMoved = -100_000;
        Location supported;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!(player.getVehicle() instanceof Boat boat)) {
            return;
        }
        List<Entity> passengers = boat.getPassengers();
        if (passengers.isEmpty() || passengers.getFirst() != player) {
            return; // only the driver steers the boat
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (state.boatId != boat.getEntityId()) {
            // first packet in this boat: "from" is still where the player stood before
            state.boatId = boat.getEntityId();
            state.lastY = to.getY();
            state.riseTicks = 0;
            state.supported = null;
            return;
        }
        double deltaY = to.getY() - state.lastY;
        state.lastY = to.getY();

        World world = boat.getWorld();
        BoundingBox box = boat.getBoundingBox();
        boolean pushedUp = boat.getVelocity().getY() > 0.05;
        if (!boat.hasGravity() || pushedUp || Math.abs(deltaY) > 3.0) {
            state.serverMoved = PlayerData.now(); // a plugin moves this boat
        }
        if (!BlockUtil.isLoaded(world, box) || since(state.serverMoved) < 40 || since(data.lastTeleport) < 20
                || since(data.lastExplosion) < 40 || since(data.lastPiston) < 40 || bouncy(world, box)) {
            state.riseTicks = 0;
            return;
        }
        // box right under the boat (must not overlap the boat itself, boats are collidable)
        BoundingBox below = new BoundingBox(box.getMinX() - 0.1, box.getMinY() - 0.4, box.getMinZ() - 0.1,
                box.getMaxX() + 0.1, box.getMinY() - 0.001, box.getMaxZ() + 0.1);
        boolean supported = world.hasCollisionsIn(below) || touchesFluid(world, box.clone().expand(0.1, 0.6, 0.1));
        if (supported) {
            state.supported = boat.getLocation();
        }
        if (deltaY > 0.0 && !supported) {
            if (++state.riseTicks >= 4) {
                state.riseTicks = 0;
                if (flag(data, "Rise", "dy=" + format(deltaY)) && state.supported != null
                        && state.supported.getWorld() == world && state.supported.distanceSquared(boat.getLocation()) < 400.0) {
                    boat.setVelocity(new org.bukkit.util.Vector());
                    boat.teleport(state.supported); // passengers stay in the boat (Paper 1.21.10+)
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.riseTicks = 0;
        }
    }

    /** Slime, honey and bubble columns push boats up for real. */
    private boolean bouncy(World world, BoundingBox box) {
        for (int x = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinX()); x <= (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxX()); x++) {
            for (int y = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinY()) - 2; y <= (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxY()); y++) {
                for (int z = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinZ()); z <= (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxZ()); z++) {
                    Material type = world.getBlockAt(x, y, z).getType();
                    if (type == Material.SLIME_BLOCK || type == Material.HONEY_BLOCK || type == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean touchesFluid(World world, BoundingBox box) {
        for (int x = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinX()); x <= (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxX()); x++) {
            for (int y = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinY()); y <= (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxY()); y++) {
                for (int z = (int) Math.floor(box.getMinZ()); z <= (int) Math.floor(box.getMaxZ()); z++) {
                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, y, z);
                    if (BlockUtil.isLiquid(block)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/ElytraFlyCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.VelocityRecord;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Elytra physics can not hover, can not climb without speed and have a top speed. Limits were
 * found by simulating the vanilla glide formula with a search over every pitch (a legit
 * elytra hovers for at most ~8 ticks and climbs at low speed for at most ~22 ticks).
 */
public final class ElytraFlyCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    private double maxSpeed;

    public ElytraFlyCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "elytrafly", "ElytraFly", CheckCategory.MOVEMENT, "Elytra flight that ignores vanilla glide physics.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        maxSpeed = option("max-speed", 4.3);
    }

    private static final class State {
        int hoverTicks;
        int climbTicks;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (!data.gliding || data.skipMovement || !data.chunkLoaded || data.inLiquid || data.inVehicle
                || since(data.lastFirework) < 60 || since(data.lastRiptide) < 40 || since(data.lastTeleport) < 10
                || since(data.lastExplosion) < 40 || since(data.lastLevitation) < 20 || data.player().isFlying()
                || velocityPending(data)) {
            state.hoverTicks = 0;
            state.climbTicks = 0;
            return;
        }
        double horizontal = data.deltaXZ;
        double vertical = data.deltaY;
        double total = Math.sqrt(horizontal * horizontal + vertical * vertical);
        // slow falling / low or no gravity: a legit elytra can hover and climb slowly for a long time
        boolean lowGravity = data.gravity < 0.075 || data.slowFalling || since(data.gravityChange) < 20 + data.pingTicks();
        if (lowGravity) {
            state.hoverTicks = 0;
            state.climbTicks = 0;
        }

        if (!lowGravity && horizontal < 0.3 && Math.abs(vertical) < 0.06 && !data.horizontalCollision && !data.headBlocked) {
            if (++state.hoverTicks >= 20) {
                state.hoverTicks = 10;
                fail(data, "Hover", "speed=" + format(horizontal) + " dy=" + format(vertical));
            }
        } else {
            state.hoverTicks = 0;
        }

        if (!lowGravity && vertical > 0.0 && horizontal < 0.2) {
            if (++state.climbTicks >= 30) {
                state.climbTicks = 15;
                fail(data, "Ascend", "speed=" + format(horizontal) + " dy=" + format(vertical));
            }
        } else {
            state.climbTicks = 0;
        }

        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("elytrafly.speed");
        if (total > maxSpeed) {
            if (buffer.increase() > 2.0 && !rocketAttached(data)) {
                fail(data, "Speed", "speed=" + format(total) + " max=" + format(maxSpeed));
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.1);
        }
    }

    private boolean velocityPending(PlayerData data) {
        for (VelocityRecord record : data.velocities) {
            if (data.clientTicks - record.clientTick <= record.window + 2) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** Rockets a plugin attached to the player (no PlayerElytraBoostEvent for those). */
    private boolean rocketAttached(PlayerData data) {
        for (org.bukkit.entity.Entity entity : data.player().getNearbyEntities(4.0, 4.0, 4.0)) {
            if (entity instanceof org.bukkit.entity.Firework firework && firework.getAttachedTo() == data.player()) {
                data.lastFirework = PlayerData.now();
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private void fail(PlayerData data, String type, String debug) {
        if (flag(data, type, debug)) {
            data.player().setGliding(false);
            setback(data);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/FastClimbCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Ladders, vines and scaffolding have a fixed climb speed of ~0.1176 blocks per tick.
 */
public final class FastClimbCheck extends MovementCheck {

    private static final double CLIMB_SPEED = (0.2 - 0.08) * 0.98;

    public FastClimbCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "fastclimb", "FastClimb", "Climbing ladders, vines or scaffolding too fast.");
    }

    private static final class State {
        boolean wasClimbing;
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        boolean climbing = data.climbing;
        boolean wasClimbing = state.wasClimbing;
        state.wasClimbing = climbing;
        if (!climbing || !wasClimbing || exempt(data) || data.deltaY <= 0.0 || data.lastOnGround
                || data.inLiquid || since(data.lastLevitation) < 20 || since(data.lastSlime) < 20) {
            return;
        }
        // jumping next to a ladder keeps the normal jump arc
        double arc = (data.lastDeltaY - data.gravity) * 0.98;
        double allowed = Math.max(CLIMB_SPEED, arc) + 0.005;
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("fastclimb");
        if (data.deltaY > allowed && !matchesVelocity(data, data.deltaY)) {
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                if (flag(data, "Speed", "dy=" + format(data.deltaY) + " max=" + format(allowed))) {
                    setback(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.1);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/FlightCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Simulates vanilla gravity every client tick: motionY = (lastMotionY - gravity) * 0.98.
 * Any difference while in the air means the client is flying, gliding, air jumping,
 * spidering, hovering or fast falling.
 */
public final class FlightCheck extends MovementCheck {

    private double tolerance;
    private double maxBuffer;

    public FlightCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "flight", "Flight", "Moving in the air against gravity (fly, glide, air jump, spider, hover).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        tolerance = option("tolerance", 0.005);
        maxBuffer = option("buffer", 3.0);
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        if (exempt(data) || environmentExempt(data)) {
            return;
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("flight");
        double deltaY = data.deltaY;

        if (data.lastOnGround) {
            if (deltaY <= 0.0) {
                return;
            }
            double maxJump = maxJump(data);
            double max = Math.max(maxJump, data.stepHeight) + 0.001;
            if (deltaY > max && !matchesVelocity(data, deltaY)) {
                fail(data, "HighJump", "dy=" + format(deltaY) + " max=" + format(max));
                return;
            }
            // left the ground with a hop smaller than a jump (packet criticals / low hop)
            if (!data.onGround && deltaY < maxJump - 0.01 && !data.headBlocked && since(data.lastHeadBlocked) > 3
                    && !matchesVelocity(data, deltaY) && !data.groundLenient && !BlockUtil.nearCollidable(data.player())) {
                if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                    fail(data, "MiniJump", "dy=" + format(deltaY) + " jump=" + format(maxJump));
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        if (since(data.lastHeadBlocked) < 3 && deltaY <= 0.0) {
            return;
        }

        double predicted = predict(data.lastDeltaY, data.gravity, data.slowFalling);
        // gravity / slow falling changed: the client may still use the old values for a round trip
        if (since(data.gravityChange) <= data.pingTicks() + 3) {
            double old = predict(data.lastDeltaY, data.oldGravity, data.oldSlowFalling);
            if (Math.abs(deltaY - old) < Math.abs(deltaY - predicted)) {
                predicted = old;
            }
        }
        double difference = deltaY - predicted;

        if (data.onGround) {
            // landing: a collision can only slow the fall down, never speed it up
            if (difference < -tolerance - 0.01 && !matchesVelocity(data, deltaY)) {
                if (buffer.increase() > maxBuffer) {
                    fail(data, "FastFall", "dy=" + format(deltaY) + " pred=" + format(predicted));
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        if (Math.abs(difference) > tolerance && !matchesVelocity(data, deltaY)) {
            if (data.headBlocked && difference < 0.0) {
                return;
            }
            // > 0.25 blocks away from gravity in one tick is never legit (air jump, jetpack, blink up)
            double weight = Math.abs(difference) > 0.25 ? maxBuffer + 1.0 : Math.abs(difference) > 0.1 ? 1.5 : 1.0;
            if (buffer.increase(weight) > maxBuffer) {
                if (BlockUtil.nearCollidable(data.player())) {
                    buffer.reset();
                    return;
                }
                String type;
                if (difference > 0.0) {
                    type = deltaY > 0.0 ? (data.horizontalCollision ? "Spider" : "Ascend") : (Math.abs(deltaY) < 0.01 ? "Hover" : "Glide");
                } else {
                    type = "FastFall";
                }
                fail(data, type, "dy=" + format(deltaY) + " pred=" + format(predicted) + " air=" + data.airTicks);
                buffer.set(maxBuffer * 0.5); // one streak of bad ticks = a few flags, not one per tick
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.2);
        }
    }

    /** Vanilla: motionY = (motionY - gravity) * 0.98, slow falling caps gravity at 0.01 while falling. */
    private static double predict(double lastDeltaY, double gravity, boolean slowFalling) {
        double g = slowFalling && lastDeltaY <= 0.0 ? Math.min(gravity, 0.01) : gravity;
        double predicted = (lastDeltaY - g) * 0.98;
        return Math.abs(predicted) < 0.003 ? 0.0 : predicted;
    }

    private void fail(PlayerData data, String type, String debug) {
        if (flag(data, type, debug)) {
            setback(data);
        }
    }

    private boolean environmentExempt(PlayerData data) {
        return data.inLiquid || since(data.lastLiquid) < 8
                || data.climbing || since(data.lastClimb) < 6
                || data.inWeb || since(data.lastWeb) < 6
                || since(data.lastPowderSnow) < 10
                || since(data.lastHoney) < 10
                || since(data.lastSlime) < 40
                || since(data.lastBed) < 40
                || since(data.lastScaffolding) < 10
                || since(data.lastLevitation) < 20
                || data.swimming
                || data.player().isInPowderedSnow();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/JesusCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Walking on water / lava. Type A: standing on top of a fluid surface. Type B: skimming the
 * surface with the feet barely in the fluid at walking speed.
 */
public final class JesusCheck extends MovementCheck {

    public JesusCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "jesus", "Jesus", "Walking on water or lava.");
    }

    private static final class State {
        int surfaceTicks;
        int skimTicks;
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (exempt(data) || data.swimming || since(data.lastVelocity) < 10 || since(data.lastClimb) < 5) {
            state.surfaceTicks = 0;
            state.skimTicks = 0;
            return;
        }

        // A - standing above the fluid surface with nothing solid below
        if (data.liquidBelow && !data.onGround && !data.groundLenient && Math.abs(data.deltaY) < 0.02) {
            state.surfaceTicks++;
            if (state.surfaceTicks >= 6) {
                if (BlockUtil.nearCollidable(data.player())) {
                    state.surfaceTicks = 0;
                    return;
                }
                state.surfaceTicks = 3;
                if (flag(data, "Walk", "dy=" + format(data.deltaY) + " speed=" + format(data.deltaXZ) + " clientGround=" + data.clientOnGround)) {
                    setback(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.surfaceTicks = 0;
        }

        // B - feet barely in the fluid, no vertical movement, moving fast
        if (data.inLiquid && data.liquidDepth > 0.0 && data.liquidDepth < 0.3 && !data.onGround && !data.groundLenient
                && Math.abs(data.deltaY) < 0.02 && data.deltaXZ > 0.13 && since(data.lastDolphin) > 20) {
            state.skimTicks++;
            if (state.skimTicks >= 10) {
                if (BlockUtil.nearCollidable(data.player())) {
                    state.skimTicks = 0;
                    return;
                }
                state.skimTicks = 5;
                if (flag(data, "Skim", "depth=" + format(data.liquidDepth) + " speed=" + format(data.deltaXZ))) {
                    setback(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.skimTicks = 0;
        }
    }
}
```
