`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/NoFallCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.Tag;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.damage.DamageSource;
import org.bukkit.damage.DamageType;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Vanilla trusts the "on ground" flag of the client for fall damage. NoFall cheats always send
 * "on ground". Astral compares the flag with real block collisions and gives the player the
 * fall damage he tried to avoid.
 */
public final class NoFallCheck extends MovementCheck {

    private boolean applyDamage;

    public NoFallCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "nofall", "NoFall", "Spoofing the on-ground state to avoid fall damage.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        applyDamage = option("apply-damage", true);
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        if (exempt(data, true) || resets(data)) {
            data.fallDistance = 0.0;
            data.groundSpoofed = false;
            data.fallDamageTaken = 0.0;
            return;
        }

        if (!data.onGround && data.deltaY < 0.0) {
            data.fallDistance -= data.deltaY;
        }
        if (exempt(data)) {
            // just got off a vehicle (battle bus): keep counting the fall, but do not judge yet
            if (data.onGround) {
                data.fallDistance = 0.0;
                data.fallDamageTaken = 0.0;
            }
            return;
        }

        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("nofall");
        // the client says "on ground" but there is nothing under its feet
        if (data.clientOnGround && !data.onGround && !data.groundLenient
                && since(data.lastGhostBlock) > 20 && since(data.lastPowderSnow) > 10 && since(data.lastScaffolding) > 10
                && !BlockUtil.nearCollidable(data.player())) {
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                data.groundSpoofed = true;
                flag(data, "GroundSpoof", "fall=" + format(data.fallDistance, 2) + " dy=" + format(data.deltaY));
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.25);
        }

        // landed for real
        if (data.onGround && !data.lastOnGround) {
            if (data.groundSpoofed && applyDamage && mitigate() && data.violation(id()) >= mitigateVl()) {
                compensate(data);
            }
            data.fallDistance = 0.0;
            data.groundSpoofed = false;
            data.fallDamageTaken = 0.0;
        } else if (data.onGround) {
            data.fallDistance = 0.0;
            data.fallDamageTaken = 0.0;
        }
    }

    private boolean resets(PlayerData data) {
        return data.inLiquid || data.climbing || data.inWeb || data.nearPowderSnow
                || since(data.lastSlowFalling) < 2 || since(data.lastLevitation) < 2
                || since(data.lastVelocity) < 2 || data.player().isInPowderedSnow();
    }

    private void compensate(PlayerData data) {
        if (since(data.lastFallCancelled) < 100) {
            return; // a minigame cancels fall damage on purpose
        }
        Player player = data.player();
        Block below = player.getLocation().subtract(0.0, 0.2, 0.0).getBlock();
        Material type = below.getType();
        if (type == Material.SLIME_BLOCK || type == Material.HAY_BLOCK || type == Material.HONEY_BLOCK
                || type == Material.POWDER_SNOW || type == Material.COBWEB || Tag.BEDS.isTagged(type)
                || data.inLiquid || data.nearLiquid) {
            return;
        }
        double safe = attribute(player, Attribute.SAFE_FALL_DISTANCE, 3.0);
        double multiplier = attribute(player, Attribute.FALL_DAMAGE_MULTIPLIER, 1.0);
        PotionEffect jump = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.JUMP_BOOST);
        double jumpBonus = jump == null ? 0.0 : jump.getAmplifier() + 1.0;
        double expected = Math.floor((data.fallDistance - safe - jumpBonus) * multiplier);
        double missing = expected - data.fallDamageTaken;
        if (missing >= 1.0) {
            player.damage(missing, DamageSource.builder(DamageType.FALL).build());
        }
    }

    private static double attribute(Player player, Attribute attribute, double def) {
        AttributeInstance instance = player.getAttribute(attribute);
        return instance == null ? def : instance.getValue();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/NoSlowCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import io.papermc.paper.datacomponent.DataComponentTypes;
import io.papermc.paper.datacomponent.item.UseEffects;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.MovementProcessor;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Using an item (eating, blocking, drawing a bow) slows vanilla players to 20% and sneaking to
 * 30%. NoSlow cheats keep full speed.
 */
public final class NoSlowCheck extends MovementCheck {

    public NoSlowCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "noslow", "NoSlow", "Moving at full speed while using items or sneaking.");
    }

    private static final class State {
        int sneakTicks;
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        Player player = data.player();
        state.sneakTicks = data.sneaking && player.getCurrentInput().isSneak() ? state.sneakTicks + 1 : 0;

        if (exempt(data) || !data.onGround || !data.lastOnGround || !data.lastLastOnGround || data.inLiquid
                || since(data.lastLiquid) < 5 || velocityPending(data) || since(data.lastSlime) < 10
                || data.climbing || data.swimming) {
            return;
        }

        double multiplier;
        String type;
        if (player.hasActiveItem() && player.getActiveItemUsedTime() >= 5 && player.getActiveItemRemainingTime() > 3) {
            ItemStack item = player.getActiveItem();
            if (MovementProcessor.isSpear(item)) {
                return;
            }
            UseEffects effects = item.getData(DataComponentTypes.USE_EFFECTS);
            multiplier = effects != null ? effects.speedMultiplier() : 0.2;
            type = "Item";
        } else if (state.sneakTicks >= 5) {
            AttributeInstance sneak = player.getAttribute(Attribute.SNEAKING_SPEED);
            multiplier = sneak == null ? 0.3 : sneak.getValue();
            type = "Sneak";
        } else {
            return;
        }
        if (multiplier >= 0.99) {
            return;
        }

        double base = data.sprinting ? data.speedAttribute : data.speedAttribute * 1.3;
        double f = data.lastFriction;
        double momentum = data.lastDeltaXZ * data.lastLastFriction * 0.91;
        double acceleration = base * multiplier * (0.21600002 / (f * f * f));
        double limit = momentum + acceleration + 0.02;

        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("noslow");
        if (data.deltaXZ > limit && data.deltaXZ > 0.1) {
            if (buffer.increase() > 5.0) {
                if (flag(data, type, "speed=" + format(data.deltaXZ) + " limit=" + format(limit) + " mult=" + format(multiplier, 2))) {
                    setback(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.25);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/PhaseCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PlayerFailMoveEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.FluidCollisionMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Moving through solid blocks (Phase / NoClip / VClip). Type A ray traces the movement of the
 * feet and of the eyes - both passing through a full solid block is impossible. Type B uses
 * Paper's own movement validation (moved wrongly / clipped into block).
 */
public final class PhaseCheck extends MovementCheck {

    public PhaseCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "phase", "Phase", "Moving through solid blocks (phase / noclip / vclip).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        if (exempt(data) || since(data.lastPiston) < 40) {
            return;
        }
        double distance = Math.sqrt(data.deltaXZ * data.deltaXZ + data.deltaY * data.deltaY);
        if (distance < 0.1 || (data.deltaXZ > 0.8 && Math.abs(data.deltaY) < 0.6)) {
            return;
        }
        World world = data.player().getWorld();
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        boolean inside = insideSolid(world, data.player().getBoundingBox());
        boolean wasInside = state.inside;
        state.inside = inside;
        if (inside && !wasInside && since(data.lastGhostBlock) > 20) {
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("phase.inside");
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                buffer.set(0.5);
                if (flag(data, "Inside", "moved into a solid block dist=" + format(distance))) {
                    setback(data);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        Vector feetFrom = new Vector(data.lastX, data.lastY + 0.1, data.lastZ);
        Vector feetTo = new Vector(data.x, data.y + 0.1, data.z);
        Vector eyeFrom = new Vector(data.lastX, data.lastY + data.eyeHeight, data.lastZ);
        Vector eyeTo = new Vector(data.x, data.y + data.eyeHeight, data.z);
        Block feet = blocked(world, feetFrom, feetTo);
        if (feet == null) {
            return;
        }
        Block eye = blocked(world, eyeFrom, eyeTo);
        if (eye == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (flag(data, "Clip", "dist=" + format(distance) + " through=" + feet.getType().name().toLowerCase())) {
            setback(data);
        }
    }

    /** Returns the full solid block between the two points, or null when the path is clear. */
    private Block blocked(World world, Vector from, Vector to) {
        if (isSolid(world.getBlockAt(from.getBlockX(), from.getBlockY(), from.getBlockZ()))) {
            // started inside a block (suffocating, sand, ...) - the player is only getting out
            return null;
        }
        Vector direction = to.clone().subtract(from);
        double length = direction.length();
        if (length < 1.0E-4) {
            return null;
        }
        RayTraceResult result = world.rayTraceBlocks(new Location(world, from.getX(), from.getY(), from.getZ()),
                direction.normalize(), length, FluidCollisionMode.NEVER, true);
        if (result == null || result.getHitBlock() == null) {
            return null;
        }
        Block hit = result.getHitBlock();
        return isSolid(hit) ? hit : null;
    }

    private static final class State {
        boolean inside;
    }

    /** True when the (slightly shrunk) player box overlaps a full solid block. */
    private static boolean insideSolid(World world, org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox box) {
        org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox shrunk = box.clone().expand(-0.05, -0.1, -0.05);
        for (int x = (int) Math.floor(shrunk.getMinX()); x <= (int) Math.floor(shrunk.getMaxX()); x++) {
            for (int y = (int) Math.floor(shrunk.getMinY()); y <= (int) Math.floor(shrunk.getMaxY()); y++) {
                for (int z = (int) Math.floor(shrunk.getMinZ()); z <= (int) Math.floor(shrunk.getMaxZ()); z++) {
                    if (isSolid(world.getBlockAt(x, y, z))) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isSolid(Block block) {
        return block.getType().isOccluding() && block.getType().isSolid();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onFailMove(PlayerFailMoveEvent event) {
        if (event.isAllowed()) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || since(data.lastTeleport) < 40 || since(data.lastPiston) < 40
                || since(data.lastGhostBlock) < 40 || data.inVehicle) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerFailMoveEvent.FailReason reason = event.getFailReason();
        if (reason != PlayerFailMoveEvent.FailReason.CLIPPED_INTO_BLOCK && reason != PlayerFailMoveEvent.FailReason.MOVED_WRONGLY) {
            return;
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("phase.vanilla");
        if (buffer.increase() > 6.0) {
            buffer.set(3.0);
            flag(data, "Vanilla", "reason=" + reason.name().toLowerCase());
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/SpeedCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Horizontal movement prediction using vanilla friction and acceleration:
 * <pre>
 *   speed = lastSpeed * friction + acceleration (+0.2 on a sprint jump)
 *   ground acceleration = movementSpeed * 0.216 / blockFriction^3
 *   air acceleration    = 0.026
 * </pre>
 * Type A catches speed / bhop / long jump, type B catches air strafe (changing direction in
 * the air faster than vanilla allows).
 */
public final class SpeedCheck extends MovementCheck {

    private double tolerance;
    private double maxBuffer;

    public SpeedCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "speed", "Speed", "Moving faster than vanilla physics allow (speed, bhop, strafe).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        tolerance = option("tolerance", 0.01);
        maxBuffer = option("buffer", 4.0);
    }

    private static final class State {
        double lastBase = 0.13;
        /** Speed of the last tick, capped to what was allowed (so cheats can not build on their own speed). */
        double allowed;
        boolean allowedValid;
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        double attribute = data.speedAttribute;
        // the attribute already contains the sprint bonus while sprinting; stay lenient otherwise
        double base = data.sprinting ? attribute : attribute * 1.3;
        double usedBase = Math.max(base, state.lastBase);
        state.lastBase = base;

        if (exempt(data) || data.inLiquid || since(data.lastLiquid) < 6 || data.inWeb
                || data.climbing || since(data.lastSlime) < 5 || since(data.lastBed) < 5) {
            state.allowedValid = false;
            return;
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- A: speed limit
        double momentumFriction = data.lastLastOnGround ? data.lastLastFriction * 0.91 : 0.91;
        double last = state.allowedValid ? Math.min(data.lastDeltaXZ, state.allowed) : data.lastDeltaXZ;
        double momentum = last * momentumFriction;
        double acceleration;
        if (data.lastOnGround) {
            double f = data.lastFriction;
            acceleration = usedBase * (0.21600002 / (f * f * f));
        } else {
            acceleration = 0.026;
        }
        double jump = data.lastOnGround && (data.deltaY > 0.0 || data.headBlocked) ? 0.2 : 0.0;
        double limit = momentum + acceleration + jump + tolerance;

        double velocity = pendingHorizontal(data);
        if (velocity > 0.0) {
            limit = Math.max(limit, velocity + acceleration + jump + 0.05);
        }

        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("speed");
        state.allowed = data.deltaXZ;
        state.allowedValid = true;
        if (data.deltaXZ > limit) {
            double excess = data.deltaXZ - limit;
            if (excess < 0.15) {
                // small but impossible gain: the next tick may only build on the allowed speed
                state.allowed = limit;
            }
            if (buffer.increase(excess > 0.1 ? 2.0 : 1.0) > maxBuffer) {
                if (nearEntities(data.player())) {
                    buffer.reset();
                    state.allowed = data.deltaXZ;
                } else if (flag(data, "Limit", "speed=" + format(data.deltaXZ) + " limit=" + format(limit)
                        + " ground=" + data.lastOnGround + " f=" + format(data.lastFriction, 3))) {
                    setback(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.1);
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- B: air strafe
        if (data.lastOnGround || data.lastLastOnGround || data.onGround || velocity > 0.0
                || data.horizontalCollision || since(data.lastCollision) < 3 || since(data.lastClimb) < 5
                || since(data.lastWeb) < 5 || since(data.lastPowderSnow) < 5 || since(data.lastHoney) < 5
                || since(data.lastScaffolding) < 5 || data.nearLiquid) {
            return;
        }
        double predictedX = data.lastDeltaX * 0.91;
        double predictedZ = data.lastDeltaZ * 0.91;
        if (Math.abs(predictedX) < 0.003) {
            predictedX = 0.0;
        }
        if (Math.abs(predictedZ) < 0.003) {
            predictedZ = 0.0;
        }
        double accel = Math.hypot(data.deltaX - predictedX, data.deltaZ - predictedZ);
        Buffer strafe = data.buffer("speed.strafe");
        if (accel > 0.026 + 0.004 + tolerance * 0.2) {
            if (strafe.increase() > maxBuffer) {
                if (nearEntities(data.player())) {
                    strafe.reset();
                } else if (flag(data, "Strafe", "accel=" + format(accel, 4) + " max=0.026")) {
                    setback(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            strafe.decrease(0.05);
        }
    }

    /** Other entities push players (entity cramming) - never flag then. */
    private boolean nearEntities(Player player) {
        for (Entity entity : player.getNearbyEntities(1.0, 1.5, 1.0)) {
            if (entity instanceof LivingEntity || entity.getType().name().contains("BOAT") || entity.getType().name().contains("MINECART")) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return BlockUtil.nearCollidable(player);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/SprintCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Input;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Uses the key inputs the client sends since 1.21.2: vanilla stops sprinting without forward
 * input (OmniSprint) and when the food level is 6 or lower.
 */
public final class SprintCheck extends MovementCheck {

    public SprintCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "sprint", "OmniSprint", "Sprinting backwards / sideways or while starving.");
    }

    private static final class State {
        int omniTicks;
        int hungerTicks;
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        Player player = data.player();
        if (exempt(data) || data.inLiquid || data.swimming || since(data.lastLiquid) < 10 || !data.sprinting) {
            state.omniTicks = 0;
            state.hungerTicks = 0;
            return;
        }
        Input input = player.getCurrentInput();
        int forward = (input.isForward() ? 1 : 0) - (input.isBackward() ? 1 : 0);
        boolean moving = input.isForward() || input.isBackward() || input.isLeft() || input.isRight();

        if (forward <= 0 && moving && data.deltaXZ > 0.15 && velocityNotPending(data)) {
            if (++state.omniTicks >= 4) {
                state.omniTicks = 0;
                if (flag(data, "Omni", "forward=" + forward + " speed=" + format(data.deltaXZ))) {
                    player.setSprinting(false);
                    setback(data);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.omniTicks = 0;
        }

        GameMode mode = player.getGameMode();
        if (player.getFoodLevel() <= 6 && !player.getAllowFlight() && (mode == GameMode.SURVIVAL || mode == GameMode.ADVENTURE)
                && data.deltaXZ > 0.2) {
            if (++state.hungerTicks >= data.pingTicks() + 20) {
                state.hungerTicks = 0;
                if (flag(data, "Hunger", "food=" + player.getFoodLevel() + " speed=" + format(data.deltaXZ))) {
                    player.setSprinting(false);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.hungerTicks = 0;
        }
    }

    private boolean velocityNotPending(PlayerData data) {
        return !velocityPending(data);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/StepCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Vanilla players can only step up blocks lower than their step height (0.6). Step cheats walk
 * straight up full blocks.
 */
public final class StepCheck extends MovementCheck {

    public StepCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "step", "Step", "Stepping up blocks higher than vanilla allows.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        if (exempt(data) || !data.onGround || data.deltaY <= 0.0) {
            return;
        }
        if (data.inLiquid || since(data.lastLiquid) < 5 || data.climbing || since(data.lastClimb) < 5
                || since(data.lastSlime) < 20 || since(data.lastBed) < 20 || since(data.lastScaffolding) < 10
                || since(data.lastPowderSnow) < 10) {
            return;
        }
        double allowed = data.lastOnGround ? Math.max(data.stepHeight, maxJump(data)) : data.stepHeight;
        if (data.deltaY > allowed + 0.001 && !matchesVelocity(data, data.deltaY)) {
            if (BlockUtil.nearCollidable(data.player())) {
                return;
            }
            if (flag(data, data.lastOnGround ? "Height" : "AirStep", "dy=" + format(data.deltaY) + " max=" + format(allowed))) {
                setback(data);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/movement/TimerCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.movement;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.MovementCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Clock;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerChangedWorldEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRespawnEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * A vanilla client runs exactly 20 ticks per second and sends one "tick end" packet per tick.
 * Timer cheats speed the game up. Astral keeps a balance: +50ms per client tick, minus the real
 * time that passed. A client that runs too fast builds up a positive balance.
 */
public final class TimerCheck extends MovementCheck {

    private static final long TICK = 50_000_000L;

    private long maxBalance;
    private long maxLagBalance;

    public TimerCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "timer", "Timer", "Running the game faster than 20 ticks per second.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        maxBalance = (long) option("max-balance", 150.0) * 1_000_000L;
        maxLagBalance = (long) option("max-lag-balance", 3000.0) * 1_000_000L;
    }

    private static final class State {
        long lastTick;
        long balance;
        long windowStart;
        int windowTicks;
        double lastSpeed = 1.0;
        boolean suspect;
        long suspectBalance;
        long suspectSince;
    }

    @Override
    protected void handle(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        long now = Clock.nanos();
        if (state.lastTick == 0L) {
            state.lastTick = now;
            state.windowStart = now;
            return;
        }
        long elapsed = now - state.lastTick;
        state.lastTick = now;

        state.windowTicks++;
        if (now - state.windowStart >= 1_000_000_000L) {
            state.lastSpeed = state.windowTicks / ((now - state.windowStart) / 1_000_000_000.0) / 20.0;
            state.windowTicks = 0;
            state.windowStart = now;
        }

        if (data.player().isDead()) {
            state.balance = 0L;
            return;
        }

        state.balance += TICK - elapsed;
        if (state.balance < -maxLagBalance) {
            state.balance = -maxLagBalance;
        }
        // stored lag slowly disappears so it cannot be saved for later
        if (state.balance < 0L) {
            state.balance = Math.min(0L, state.balance + 2_500_000L);
        }

        if (state.balance > maxBalance) {
            if (!state.suspect) {
                // could be a one time catch-up after the ping dropped - wait for more
                state.suspect = true;
                state.suspectBalance = state.balance;
                state.suspectSince = now;
            } else if (state.balance - state.suspectBalance > maxBalance / 2) {
                double ahead = state.balance / 1_000_000.0;
                state.balance = 0L;
                state.suspect = false;
                if (flag(data, "Balance", "ahead=" + format(ahead, 1) + "ms speed=" + format(state.lastSpeed * 100.0, 1) + "%")) {
                    setback(data);
                }
            } else if (now - state.suspectSince > 5_000_000_000L) {
                // the balance stopped growing: it was lag, not timer
                state.balance = 0L;
                state.suspect = false;
            }
        } else if (state.suspect && now - state.suspectSince > 5_000_000_000L) {
            state.suspect = false;
        }
    }

    private void reset(PlayerData data) {
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        state.lastTick = 0L;
        state.balance = 0L;
        state.suspect = false;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        reset(data(event.getPlayer()));
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent event) {
        reset(data(event.getPlayer()));
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onWorld(PlayerChangedWorldEvent event) {
        reset(data(event.getPlayer()));
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/player/BadPacketsCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.PacketOrder;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

/**
 * Packets in an order a vanilla client never sends.
 * <ul>
 *   <li>Post - item use, block clicks, digging and entity interactions are all handled before the
 *   client moves, so receiving them after the movement packet of the same tick means silent
 *   rotations (auto pot looking down, auto crystal, aura interactions, packet macros).</li>
 *   <li>Multi - a vanilla client sends at most one movement packet per tick. More than one means
 *   extra rotation packets (rotate, act, rotate back) or held back movement released at once
 *   (blink / fake lag).</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public final class BadPacketsCheck extends Check {

    public BadPacketsCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "badpackets", "BadPackets", CheckCategory.PLAYER, "Packets sent in an order no vanilla client uses.");
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        // Paper only fires the event for a real change (1/16 block or 10 degrees), so tiny
        // jitter packets are never counted
        int moves = ++data.movesThisTick;
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || PacketOrder.exempt(data)) {
            return;
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("badpackets.multi");
        if (moves == 1) {
            buffer.decrease(0.02);
            return;
        }
        if (buffer.increase() > 2.0) {
            buffer.set(1.0);
            flag(data, "Multi", moves + " movement packets in one tick (extra rotations / blink)");
        }
    }

    // not ignoreCancelled: right clicks on air (potions, pearls) are fired already "cancelled"
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        Action action = event.getAction();
        if (action == Action.PHYSICAL || action == Action.LEFT_CLICK_AIR
                || (action == Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK && event.isBlockInHand())) {
            return; // block placing is handled by Scaffold (Post)
        }
        if (check(data(event.getPlayer()), action == Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK ? "Dig" : "Use")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteractEntity(PlayerInteractEntityEvent event) {
        if (check(data(event.getPlayer()), "Interact")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    /** Returns true when the action should be cancelled (block mode only). */
    private boolean check(PlayerData data, String what) {
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return false;
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("badpackets.post");
        if (PacketOrder.isPost(data)) {
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                buffer.set(0.5);
                return flag(data, "Post" + what, what.toLowerCase() + " sent after the movement packet (silent rotations)");
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.05);
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/player/ClientBrandCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.ClientUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRegisterChannelEvent;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Blocks known hacked clients that announce themselves through their client brand or the plugin
 * channels they register.
 */
public final class ClientBrandCheck extends Check {

    private List<String> blockedBrands = List.of();
    private List<String> blockedChannels = List.of();

    public ClientBrandCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "clientbrand", "ClientBrand", CheckCategory.PLAYER, "Joining with a known hacked client.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        blockedBrands = lower(optionList("blocked-brands"));
        blockedChannels = lower(optionList("blocked-channels"));
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(plugin, () -> {
            if (!player.isOnline()) {
                return;
            }
            PlayerData data = data(player);
            if (data == null || !enabled() || data.isBypassing(id())) {
                return;
            }
            String brand = ClientUtil.brand(player).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            for (String blocked : blockedBrands) {
                if (brand.contains(blocked)) {
                    punishClient(data, "Brand", "brand=" + brand);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }, 40L);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onChannel(PlayerRegisterChannelEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data == null || !enabled() || data.isBypassing(id())) {
            return;
        }
        String channel = event.getChannel().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        for (String blocked : blockedChannels) {
            if (channel.contains(blocked)) {
                punishClient(data, "Channel", "channel=" + channel);
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private void punishClient(PlayerData data, String type, String debug) {
        if (flag(data, type, debug)) {
            Player player = data.player();
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> player.kick(plugin.messages().lines("kick-screen",
                    "reason", "Disallowed client modification")));
        }
    }

    private static List<String> lower(List<String> values) {
        return values.stream().map(value -> value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)).toList();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/player/FastBowCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityShootBowEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

/**
 * Bow spam: shooting more arrows per second than drawing a bow allows.
 */
public final class FastBowCheck extends Check {

    private int maxShots;

    public FastBowCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "fastbow", "FastBow", CheckCategory.PLAYER, "Shooting arrows faster than a bow can be drawn.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        maxShots = option("max-shots-per-second", 6);
    }

    private static final class State {
        final Deque<Integer> shots = new ArrayDeque<>();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onShoot(EntityShootBowEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player)) {
            return;
        }
        ItemStack bow = event.getBow();
        if (bow == null || bow.getType() != Material.BOW) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        state.shots.addLast(data.clientTicks);
        while (!state.shots.isEmpty() && data.clientTicks - state.shots.peekFirst() >= 20) {
            state.shots.removeFirst();
        }
        if (state.shots.size() > maxShots) {
            if (flag(data, "Rate", "shots/s=" + state.shots.size() + " force=" + Math.round(event.getForce() * 100) + "%")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/player/InventoryMoveCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.player;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.PacketOrder;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Input;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryCreativeEvent;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData.since;
import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * When a screen opens, vanilla releases every key and tells the server (player input packet,
 * 1.21.2+). Clicking in an inventory while still holding movement keys, or while the camera
 * keeps turning, is InventoryMove / InvWalk (also used by AutoArmor and ChestStealer).
 * Clicks that arrive after an attack / item use or after the movement packet of the same tick
 * come from inventory macros that never open the inventory (auto totem, auto armor, auto pot).
 */
public final class InventoryMoveCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    public InventoryMoveCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "inventorymove", "InventoryMove", CheckCategory.PLAYER, "Moving or turning while using an inventory.");
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (event instanceof InventoryCreativeEvent || !(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player)) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.inventoryClicksThisTick++;
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || data.inVehicle || data.gliding
                || since(data.lastTeleport) < 20 || player.isDead()) {
            return;
        }
        // inventory clicks happen between ticks, so they always come before the actions and the
        // movement of a tick - a click after them was sent by a macro / packet module
        Buffer order = data.buffer("inventorymove.order");
        if (data.actionsThisTick > 0) {
            if (order.increase() > 1.0 && flag(data, "Action", "click sent after " + data.actionsThisTick + " attack/use action(s) in the same tick")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
            return;
        }
        if (PacketOrder.isPost(data)) {
            if (order.increase() > 1.0 && flag(data, "Post", "click sent after the movement packet")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
            return;
        }
        order.decrease(0.05);
        Input input = player.getCurrentInput();
        boolean moving = input.isForward() || input.isBackward() || input.isLeft() || input.isRight() || input.isJump();
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("inventorymove.input");
        if (moving) {
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                if (flag(data, "Input", keys(input))) {
                    event.setCancelled(true);
                }
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.25);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        if (data.inventoryClicksThisTick == 0 || !shouldCheck(data) || data.skipMovement || data.inVehicle
                || since(data.lastTeleport) < 20) {
            return;
        }
        // clicked in an inventory and attacked in the same tick (inventory must be closed to attack)
        if (data.attacksThisTick > 0) {
            Buffer combo = data.buffer("inventorymove.combo");
            if (combo.increase() > 2.0) {
                combo.set(1.0);
                flag(data, "Combo", "inventory click and attack in the same tick");
            }
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("inventorymove.rotation");
        if (Math.abs(data.deltaYaw) > 5.0F && Math.abs(data.lastDeltaYaw) > 5.0F) {
            if (buffer.increase() > 3.0) {
                buffer.set(1.5);
                flag(data, "Rotation", "turned " + format(Math.abs(data.deltaYaw), 1) + " deg while clicking");
            }
        } else {
            buffer.decrease(0.1);
        }
    }

    private static String keys(Input input) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("keys=");
        if (input.isForward()) sb.append('W');
        if (input.isLeft()) sb.append('A');
        if (input.isBackward()) sb.append('S');
        if (input.isRight()) sb.append('D');
        if (input.isJump()) sb.append(" jump");
        return sb.toString();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/world/BlockReachCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.CombatUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Breaking / placing blocks further away than the block_interaction_range attribute.
 */
public final class BlockReachCheck extends Check {

    private double tolerance;

    public BlockReachCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "blockreach", "BlockReach", CheckCategory.WORLD, "Breaking or placing blocks too far away.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        tolerance = option("tolerance", 0.3);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockBreakEvent.class) {
            return;
        }
        if (check(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlock(), "Break")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockPlaceEvent.class) {
            return;
        }
        if (check(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlockAgainst(), "Place")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    private boolean check(Player player, Block block, String type) {
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {
            return false;
        }
        AttributeInstance attribute = player.getAttribute(Attribute.BLOCK_INTERACTION_RANGE);
        double range = attribute == null ? (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE ? 5.0 : 4.5) : attribute.getValue();
        BoundingBox box = BlockUtil.box(block);
        double best = Double.MAX_VALUE;
        for (Vector eye : CombatUtil.eyes(player, data)) {
            best = Math.min(best, MathUtil.distanceToBox(eye, box));
        }
        if (best > range + tolerance) {
            return flag(data, type, "distance=" + format(best) + " max=" + format(range));
        }
        return false;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/world/FastBreakCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockDamageAbortEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockDamageEvent;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Compares the real mining time (in client ticks, so timer can not hide it) with the vanilla
 * mining time of the block for the tool, enchantments, haste, water and ground state.
 */
public final class FastBreakCheck extends Check {

    private double minRatio;

    public FastBreakCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "fastbreak", "FastBreak", CheckCategory.WORLD, "Mining blocks faster than vanilla.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        minRatio = option("min-ratio", 0.75);
    }

    private static final class State {
        int x;
        int y;
        int z;
        boolean active;
        int startTick;
        float startSpeed;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamage(BlockDamageEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (event.getInstaBreak()) {
            state.active = false;
            return;
        }
        Block block = event.getBlock();
        state.x = block.getX();
        state.y = block.getY();
        state.z = block.getZ();
        state.startTick = data.clientTicks;
        state.startSpeed = block.getBreakSpeed(event.getPlayer());
        state.active = true;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onAbort(BlockDamageAbortEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.state(State.class, State::new).active = false;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockBreakEvent.class) {
            return; // fired by another plugin (tree feller, vein miner ...)
        }
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        Block block = event.getBlock();
        if (!state.active || state.x != block.getX() || state.y != block.getY() || state.z != block.getZ()) {
            return;
        }
        state.active = false;
        float speed = Math.max(state.startSpeed, block.getBreakSpeed(player));
        if (speed <= 0.0F || speed >= 1.0F) {
            return;
        }
        int expected = (int) Math.ceil(1.0 / speed);
        if (expected < 4) {
            return;
        }
        int elapsed = data.clientTicks - state.startTick + 1;
        double ratio = elapsed / (double) expected;
        if (ratio < minRatio) {
            if (flag(data, "Time", "ticks=" + elapsed + " vanilla=" + expected + " ratio=" + format(ratio, 2))) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/world/FastPlaceCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

/**
 * Placing blocks too fast (FastPlace, auto bridge). Holding right click places one block every
 * 4 ticks; clicking is limited by the human hand. Perfectly timed 1-tick placing is a macro.
 */
public final class FastPlaceCheck extends Check {

    private int maxPerSecond;

    public FastPlaceCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "fastplace", "FastPlace", CheckCategory.WORLD, "Placing blocks faster than humanly possible.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        maxPerSecond = option("max-blocks-per-second", 25);
    }

    private static final class State {
        final Deque<Integer> places = new ArrayDeque<>();
        int lastPlaceTick = -1000;
        int sameDelay;
        int lastDelay = -1;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockPlaceEvent.class) {
            return;
        }
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        if (PlayerData.now() < data.placeLockUntil) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            return;
        }
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        data.placedThisTick++;
        if (data.placedThisTick > 4) {
            if (flag(data, "Multi", "blocks=" + data.placedThisTick + " in one tick")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
        }

        state.places.addLast(data.clientTicks);
        while (!state.places.isEmpty() && data.clientTicks - state.places.peekFirst() >= 20) {
            state.places.removeFirst();
        }
        if (state.places.size() > maxPerSecond) {
            if (flag(data, "Speed", "blocks/s=" + state.places.size())) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
        }

        int delay = data.clientTicks - state.lastPlaceTick;
        state.lastPlaceTick = data.clientTicks;
        if (delay >= 1 && delay <= 2 && delay == state.lastDelay) {
            if (++state.sameDelay >= 20) {
                state.sameDelay = 10;
                if (flag(data, "Robotic", "20 blocks with exactly " + delay + " tick delay")) {
                    event.setCancelled(true);
                }
            }
        } else {
            state.sameDelay = 0;
        }
        state.lastDelay = delay;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/world/InteractCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.FluidCollisionMode;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

/**
 * GhostHand / interacting through walls: the block has to be visible from the player's eyes.
 * Rays are cast to the center, the 6 face centers and the 8 corners of the block; if a full
 * solid block hides every one of them, the player can not see it.
 */
public final class InteractCheck extends Check {

    public InteractCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "interact", "GhostHand", CheckCategory.WORLD, "Breaking or using blocks through walls.");
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockBreakEvent.class) {
            return;
        }
        if (check(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlock(), "Break")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockPlaceEvent.class || event.getBlockAgainst().equals(event.getBlockPlaced())) {
            return;
        }
        if (check(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlockAgainst(), "Place")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    private boolean check(Player player, Block block, String type) {
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR || data.inVehicle) {
            return false;
        }
        Vector eye = player.getEyeLocation().toVector();
        if (visible(player.getWorld(), eye, block)) {
            data.buffer("interact").decrease(0.5);
            return false;
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("interact");
        if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
            return flag(data, type, "block=" + block.getType().name().toLowerCase() + " hidden behind solid blocks");
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean visible(World world, Vector eye, Block block) {
        double x = block.getX();
        double y = block.getY();
        double z = block.getZ();
        // inside or touching the block
        if (eye.getX() >= x - 0.5 && eye.getX() <= x + 1.5 && eye.getY() >= y - 0.5 && eye.getY() <= y + 1.5
                && eye.getZ() >= z - 0.5 && eye.getZ() <= z + 1.5) {
            return true;
        }
        // the eyes are stuck inside a block (suffocating) - the client can still mine out
        if (world.getBlockAt(eye.getBlockX(), eye.getBlockY(), eye.getBlockZ()).getType().isOccluding()) {
            return true;
        }
        double[][] points = {
                {0.5, 0.5, 0.5},
                {0.5, 0.999, 0.5}, {0.5, 0.001, 0.5}, {0.001, 0.5, 0.5}, {0.999, 0.5, 0.5}, {0.5, 0.5, 0.001}, {0.5, 0.5, 0.999},
                {0.05, 0.05, 0.05}, {0.95, 0.05, 0.05}, {0.05, 0.95, 0.05}, {0.05, 0.05, 0.95},
                {0.95, 0.95, 0.05}, {0.95, 0.05, 0.95}, {0.05, 0.95, 0.95}, {0.95, 0.95, 0.95}
        };
        for (double[] point : points) {
            Vector target = new Vector(x + point[0], y + point[1], z + point[2]);
            Vector direction = target.clone().subtract(eye);
            double distance = direction.length();
            if (distance < 0.01) {
                return true;
            }
            RayTraceResult result = world.rayTraceBlocks(new Location(world, eye.getX(), eye.getY(), eye.getZ()),
                    direction.normalize(), distance + 0.05, FluidCollisionMode.NEVER, true);
            if (result == null || result.getHitBlock() == null) {
                return true;
            }
            Block hit = result.getHitBlock();
            if (hit.equals(block) || !hit.getType().isOccluding()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/world/NukerCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * Nuker: vanilla can break at most one block per client tick, only the block the crosshair is
 * on, and only a limited amount per second.
 */
public final class NukerCheck extends Check {

    private int maxPerSecond;
    private double maxAngle;

    public NukerCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "nuker", "Nuker", CheckCategory.WORLD, "Breaking many blocks at once or blocks the player is not looking at.");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        maxPerSecond = option("max-blocks-per-second", 22);
        maxAngle = option("max-angle", 75.0);
    }

    private static final class State {
        final Deque<Integer> breaks = new ArrayDeque<>();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockBreakEvent.class) {
            return;
        }
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        Block block = event.getBlock();
        data.brokenThisTick++;
        if (data.brokenThisTick > 1) {
            if (flag(data, "Multi", "blocks=" + data.brokenThisTick + " in one tick")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
        }

        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        state.breaks.addLast(data.clientTicks);
        while (!state.breaks.isEmpty() && data.clientTicks - state.breaks.peekFirst() >= 20) {
            state.breaks.removeFirst();
        }
        if (state.breaks.size() > maxPerSecond) {
            if (flag(data, "Speed", "blocks/s=" + state.breaks.size())) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
        }

        Vector eye = player.getEyeLocation().toVector();
        Vector toBlock = BlockUtil.center(block).subtract(eye);
        if (toBlock.lengthSquared() > 2.25) {
            double angle = MathUtil.angle(player.getEyeLocation().getDirection(), toBlock);
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("nuker.angle");
            if (angle > maxAngle) {
                if (buffer.increase() > 2.0) {
                    if (flag(data, "Angle", "angle=" + format(angle, 1))) {
                        event.setCancelled(true);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                buffer.decrease(0.5);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/world/ScaffoldCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.PacketOrder;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.combat.CombatUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.Buffer;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Scaffold / auto bridge / tower:
 * <ul>
 *   <li>AirPlace - placing against air</li>
 *   <li>Face - clicking a block face that faces away from the player (impossible to see)</li>
 *   <li>Rotation - the crosshair never pointed at the clicked block (checked on the next tick
 *       with every rotation between the two ticks)</li>
 *   <li>Post - the block was placed after the movement packet (silent rotations)</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public final class ScaffoldCheck extends Check implements TickCheck {

    public ScaffoldCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "scaffold", "Scaffold", CheckCategory.WORLD, "Automatic block placing (scaffold, tower, airplace).");
    }

    private record Pending(List<Vector> eyes, BoundingBox box, double range, float yaw, float pitch, float previousYaw, float previousPitch) {
    }

    private static final class State {
        final List<Pending> pending = new ArrayList<>();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockPlaceEvent.class) {
            return;
        }
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR || data.inVehicle) {
            return;
        }
        Block against = event.getBlockAgainst();
        Block placed = event.getBlockPlaced();
        if (against.equals(placed)) {
            return;
        }

        // placed after the movement packet of the tick: silent rotation scaffold / tower
        if (PacketOrder.isPost(data)) {
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("scaffold.post");
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0) {
                buffer.set(0.5);
                if (fail(data, "Post", "block placed after the movement packet (silent rotations)")) {
                    event.setCancelled(true);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        if (against.getType().isAir()) {
            Buffer buffer = data.buffer("scaffold.air");
            if (buffer.increase() > 1.0 && fail(data, "AirPlace", "placed against " + against.getType().name().toLowerCase())) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
            }
            return;
        }

        BlockFace face = against.getFace(placed);
        List<Vector> eyes = CombatUtil.eyes(player, data);
        if (face != null && BlockUtil.isFullCube(against) && !faceVisible(eyes, BlockUtil.box(against), face)) {
            if (fail(data, "Face", "clicked the " + face.name().toLowerCase() + " face from behind")) {
                event.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
        }

        AttributeInstance attribute = player.getAttribute(Attribute.BLOCK_INTERACTION_RANGE);
        double range = (attribute == null ? 4.5 : attribute.getValue()) + 1.0;
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (state.pending.size() < 10) {
            state.pending.add(new Pending(eyes, BlockUtil.box(against).expand(0.1), range,
                    player.getLocation().getYaw(), player.getLocation().getPitch(), data.lastYaw, data.lastPitch));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick(PlayerData data) {
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (state.pending.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        List<Pending> pending = new ArrayList<>(state.pending);
        state.pending.clear();
        if (!shouldCheck(data) || PlayerData.since(data.lastTeleport) < 10) {
            return;
        }
        Buffer buffer = data.buffer("scaffold.rotation");
        for (Pending place : pending) {
            if (looked(place, data.yaw, data.pitch)) {
                buffer.decrease(0.5);
                continue;
            }
            if (buffer.increase() > 2.0) {
                buffer.set(1.0);
                fail(data, "Rotation", "yaw=" + MathUtil.format(data.yaw, 1) + " pitch=" + MathUtil.format(data.pitch, 1));
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean looked(Pending place, float endYaw, float endPitch) {
        float[][] rotations = new float[7][];
        rotations[0] = new float[]{place.previousYaw, place.previousPitch};
        rotations[1] = new float[]{place.yaw, place.pitch};
        rotations[2] = new float[]{endYaw, endPitch};
        for (int i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
            float t = i / 5.0F;
            rotations[2 + i] = new float[]{MathUtil.lerpAngle(place.yaw, endYaw, t), place.pitch + (endPitch - place.pitch) * t};
        }
        for (float[] rotation : rotations) {
            Vector direction = MathUtil.direction(rotation[0], rotation[1]);
            for (Vector eye : place.eyes) {
                if (CombatUtil.rayHits(eye, direction, place.range, place.box)) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** The eye must be on the outer side of the clicked face. */
    private boolean faceVisible(List<Vector> eyes, BoundingBox box, BlockFace face) {
        double tolerance = 0.03;
        for (Vector eye : eyes) {
            boolean visible = switch (face) {
                case UP -> eye.getY() >= box.getMaxY() - tolerance;
                case DOWN -> eye.getY() <= box.getMinY() + tolerance;
                case NORTH -> eye.getZ() <= box.getMinZ() + tolerance;
                case SOUTH -> eye.getZ() >= box.getMaxZ() - tolerance;
                case WEST -> eye.getX() <= box.getMinX() + tolerance;
                case EAST -> eye.getX() >= box.getMaxX() - tolerance;
                default -> true;
            };
            if (visible) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean fail(PlayerData data, String type, String debug) {
        boolean mitigate = flag(data, type, debug);
        if (mitigate) {
            data.placeLockUntil = PlayerData.now() + 20;
        }
        return mitigate;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/check/world/XRayCheck.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.world;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

import static me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.format;

/**
 * X-Ray heuristics: legit miners find a few ore veins per hundred blocks of stone. X-Ray users
 * dig straight to diamonds / ancient debris. Alert only by default (staff should spectate).
 */
public final class XRayCheck extends Check {

    private int minStone;
    private double maxRatio;

    public XRayCheck(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        super(plugin, "xray", "XRay", CheckCategory.WORLD, "Finding far too many rare ores (x-ray).");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onLoad() {
        minStone = option("min-stone", 120);
        maxRatio = option("max-ratio", 4.5);
    }

    private static final class State {
        int stone;
        int veins;
        final Deque<int[]> recentOres = new ArrayDeque<>();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (event.getClass() != BlockBreakEvent.class) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (!shouldCheck(data)) {
            return;
        }
        Block block = event.getBlock();
        Material type = block.getType();
        State state = data.state(State.class, State::new);
        if (BlockUtil.isStoneLike(type)) {
            state.stone++;
        } else if (BlockUtil.isOre(type)) {
            boolean sameVein = false;
            for (int[] ore : state.recentOres) {
                if (Math.abs(ore[0] - block.getX()) <= 3 && Math.abs(ore[1] - block.getY()) <= 3 && Math.abs(ore[2] - block.getZ()) <= 3) {
                    sameVein = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            state.recentOres.addFirst(new int[]{block.getX(), block.getY(), block.getZ()});
            while (state.recentOres.size() > 16) {
                state.recentOres.removeLast();
            }
            if (!sameVein) {
                state.veins++;
                if (state.stone >= minStone) {
                    double ratio = state.veins * 100.0 / state.stone;
                    if (ratio > maxRatio) {
                        flag(data, "Ratio", "veins=" + state.veins + " stone=" + state.stone + " ratio=" + format(ratio, 2) + "/100");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        // keep a rolling window
        if (state.stone > 2000) {
            state.stone /= 2;
            state.veins /= 2;
        }
    }
}
```
