`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/command/AstralCommand.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.command;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.FlagRecord;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.ClientUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.DurationUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text;
import io.papermc.paper.ban.BanListType;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.ClickEvent;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.HoverEvent;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.OfflinePlayer;
import org.bukkit.command.Command;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.command.TabExecutor;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.NotNull;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * /astral - every staff command of Astral.
 */
public final class AstralCommand implements TabExecutor {

    /** name, usage, description */
    private static final String[][] COMMANDS = {
            {"help", "", "Show this help"},
            {"version", "[player]", "Plugin, server and client versions"},
            {"reload", "", "Reload config.yml and messages.yml"},
            {"alerts", "[on|off]", "Toggle receiving cheat alerts (flags)"},
            {"verbose", "", "See EVERY flag (no cooldown / minimum VL)"},
            {"checks", "[category]", "List every check and its state"},
            {"toggle", "<check> [on|off]", "Enable / disable a check"},
            {"info", "<player>", "Full anticheat profile of a player"},
            {"violations", "<player>", "Violation levels of a player"},
            {"logs", "<player> [page]", "Recent flags of a player"},
            {"reset", "<player|*> [check]", "Reset violations"},
            {"bypass", "<player|list>", "Toggle anticheat bypass for a player"},
            {"exempt", "<player|*> <seconds|off> [movement|combat|player|world|macro|all]", "Skip checks for a while (minigames, battle bus)"},
            {"kick", "<player> [reason]", "Kick a player with the Astral screen"},
            {"ban", "<player> [time] [reason]", "Ban a player (7d, 12h, 30m, perm)"},
            {"unban", "<player>", "Unban a player"},
            {"freeze", "<player>", "Freeze / unfreeze a player"},
            {"tp", "<player>", "Teleport to a player"},
            {"cps", "<player>", "Watch the CPS of a player"},
            {"ping", "<player>", "Show the ping of a player"},
            {"client", "<player>", "Client version, brand and platform"},
            {"gui", "", "Open the staff menu"},
            {"stats", "", "Anticheat statistics"},
            {"debug", "<check>", "Toggle debug output of one check"}
    };

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;

    public AstralCommand(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean onCommand(@NotNull CommandSender sender, @NotNull Command command, @NotNull String label, @NotNull String[] args) {
        String sub = args.length == 0 ? "help" : args[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        sub = alias(sub);
        if (!isKnown(sub)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "unknown-subcommand");
            return true;
        }
        if (!sender.hasPermission("astral.command." + sub)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "no-permission");
            return true;
        }
        String[] rest = args.length == 0 ? new String[0] : Arrays.copyOfRange(args, 1, args.length);
        switch (sub) {
            case "help" -> help(sender, label);
            case "version" -> version(sender, rest);
            case "reload" -> reload(sender);
            case "alerts" -> alerts(sender, rest);
            case "verbose" -> verbose(sender);
            case "checks" -> checks(sender, rest);
            case "toggle" -> toggle(sender, rest);
            case "info" -> info(sender, rest);
            case "violations" -> violations(sender, rest);
            case "logs" -> logs(sender, rest);
            case "reset" -> reset(sender, rest);
            case "bypass" -> bypass(sender, rest);
            case "exempt" -> exempt(sender, rest);
            case "kick" -> kick(sender, rest);
            case "ban" -> ban(sender, rest);
            case "unban" -> unban(sender, rest);
            case "freeze" -> freeze(sender, rest);
            case "tp" -> teleport(sender, rest);
            case "cps" -> cps(sender, rest);
            case "ping" -> ping(sender, rest);
            case "client" -> client(sender, rest);
            case "gui" -> gui(sender);
            case "stats" -> stats(sender);
            case "debug" -> debug(sender, rest);
            default -> plugin.messages().send(sender, "unknown-subcommand");
        }
        return true;
    }

    private static String alias(String sub) {
        return switch (sub) {
            case "ver", "v", "about" -> "version";
            case "vl", "violation" -> "violations";
            case "log", "history" -> "logs";
            case "alert", "notify" -> "alerts";
            case "check", "list" -> "checks";
            case "profile", "player", "lookup" -> "info";
            case "ss", "unfreeze" -> "freeze";
            case "teleport" -> "tp";
            case "menu", "panel" -> "gui";
            case "statistics" -> "stats";
            case "brand" -> "client";
            case "?" -> "help";
            default -> sub;
        };
    }

    private static boolean isKnown(String sub) {
        for (String[] entry : COMMANDS) {
            if (entry[0].equals(sub)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers

    private void send(CommandSender sender, String message) {
        sender.sendMessage(Text.color(message));
    }

    private String prefix() {
        return plugin.messages().prefix();
    }

    private void usage(CommandSender sender, String sub) {
        for (String[] entry : COMMANDS) {
            if (entry[0].equals(sub)) {
                send(sender, prefix() + "&cUsage: &f/astral " + entry[0] + (entry[1].isEmpty() ? "" : " " + entry[1]));
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private Player online(CommandSender sender, String name) {
        Player player = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(name);
        if (player == null) {
            player = Bukkit.getPlayer(name);
        }
        if (player == null) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-not-found", "player", name);
        }
        return player;
    }

    private static String line() {
        return "&8&m                                                    ";
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ sub commands

    private void help(CommandSender sender, String label) {
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&l" + AstralAntiCheat.DISPLAY_NAME + " &7v" + plugin.getPluginMeta().getVersion());
        send(sender, "");
        for (String[] entry : COMMANDS) {
            if (!sender.hasPermission("astral.command." + entry[0])) {
                continue;
            }
            String usage = "/" + label + " " + entry[0] + (entry[1].isEmpty() ? "" : " " + entry[1]);
            sender.sendMessage(Text.color(" &#00E5FF" + usage + " &8- &7" + entry[2])
                    .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(Text.color("&7Click to use")))
                    .clickEvent(ClickEvent.suggestCommand("/" + label + " " + entry[0] + " ")));
        }
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void version(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            if (!sender.hasPermission("astral.command.version.others")) {
                plugin.messages().send(sender, "no-permission");
                return;
            }
            Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
            if (target != null) {
                clientInfo(sender, target);
            }
            return;
        }
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&l" + AstralAntiCheat.DISPLAY_NAME + " &7AntiCheat &8by &f" + AstralAntiCheat.AUTHOR);
        send(sender, " &7Plugin version: &f" + plugin.getPluginMeta().getVersion());
        send(sender, " &7Author: &f" + AstralAntiCheat.AUTHOR);
        send(sender, " &7Mode: " + (plugin.settings().mitigation ? "&cBLOCK" : "&aWATCH &7(alerts only)"));
        send(sender, " &7Server: &f" + Bukkit.getName() + " " + Bukkit.getMinecraftVersion() + " &8(" + Bukkit.getVersion() + ")");
        send(sender, " &7Server protocol: &f" + ClientUtil.serverProtocol());
        send(sender, " &7Checks: &f" + plugin.checks().all().size() + " loaded &8(&a" + plugin.checks().enabledCount() + " enabled&8)");
        if (sender instanceof Player player) {
            send(sender, " &7Your client: &f" + ClientUtil.version(player) + " &8| &7brand: &f" + ClientUtil.brand(player));
        }
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void clientInfo(CommandSender sender, Player target) {
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&l" + target.getName() + "&7's client");
        send(sender, " &7Version: &f" + ClientUtil.versionName(ClientUtil.protocol(target)));
        send(sender, " &7Protocol: &f" + ClientUtil.protocol(target));
        send(sender, " &7Brand: &f" + ClientUtil.brand(target));
        send(sender, " &7Platform: &f" + (ClientUtil.isBedrock(target.getUniqueId()) ? "Bedrock (Geyser)" : "Java Edition"));
        send(sender, " &7Ping: &f" + target.getPing() + "ms");
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void reload(CommandSender sender) {
        long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
        plugin.reload();
        plugin.messages().send(sender, "reloaded", "time", String.valueOf(System.currentTimeMillis() - start));
    }

    private void alerts(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-only");
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            data.alerts = args[0].equalsIgnoreCase("on") || args[0].equalsIgnoreCase("true");
        } else {
            data.alerts = !data.alerts;
        }
        plugin.messages().send(sender, data.alerts ? "alerts-enabled" : "alerts-disabled");
    }

    private void verbose(CommandSender sender) {
        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-only");
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.verbose = !data.verbose;
        plugin.messages().send(sender, data.verbose ? "verbose-enabled" : "verbose-disabled");
    }

    private void checks(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        CheckCategory filter = null;
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            for (CheckCategory category : CheckCategory.values()) {
                if (category.name().equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) || category.display().equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {
                    filter = category;
                }
            }
        }
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&lChecks &8(&a" + plugin.checks().enabledCount() + "&7/&f" + plugin.checks().all().size() + "&8)");
        boolean canToggle = sender.hasPermission("astral.command.toggle");
        for (CheckCategory category : CheckCategory.values()) {
            if (filter != null && filter != category) {
                continue;
            }
            Component row = Text.color(" " + category.color() + category.display() + "&8: ");
            boolean first = true;
            for (Check check : plugin.checks().all()) {
                if (check.category() != category) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!first) {
                    row = row.append(Text.color("&8, "));
                }
                first = false;
                Component entry = Text.color((check.enabled() ? "&a" : "&c&m") + check.name())
                        .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(Text.color("&f" + check.name() + "\n&7" + check.description()
                                + "\n&7Id: &f" + check.id() + "\n&7Flags: &f" + check.totalFlags()
                                + (canToggle ? "\n\n&eClick to " + (check.enabled() ? "disable" : "enable") : ""))));
                if (canToggle) {
                    entry = entry.clickEvent(ClickEvent.runCommand("/astral toggle " + check.id()));
                }
                row = row.append(entry);
            }
            sender.sendMessage(row);
        }
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void toggle(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "toggle");
            return;
        }
        Check check = plugin.checks().get(args[0]);
        if (check == null) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "unknown-check", "check", args[0]);
            return;
        }
        boolean enable = args.length >= 2 ? args[1].equalsIgnoreCase("on") || args[1].equalsIgnoreCase("true") : !check.enabled();
        plugin.checks().setEnabled(check, enable);
        plugin.messages().send(sender, enable ? "check-enabled" : "check-disabled", "check", check.name());
    }

    private void info(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "info");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(target);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        boolean permission = target.hasPermission("astral.bypass");
        boolean manual = plugin.bypass().contains(target.getUniqueId());
        long online = (System.currentTimeMillis() - data.joinTime) / 1000L;
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&lAstral &7» &f" + target.getName());
        send(sender, " &7Client: &f" + ClientUtil.describe(target) + " &8(protocol " + ClientUtil.protocol(target) + ")");
        send(sender, " &7Ping: &f" + data.ping() + "ms &8| &7CPS: &f" + data.cps() + " &8| &7Online: &f" + DurationUtil.format(Duration.ofSeconds(online)));
        send(sender, " &7Bypass: " + (data.bypassPermission ? "&aYES" : "&cno") + " &8(&7permission: &f" + (permission ? "yes" : "no")
                + "&8, &7manual: &f" + (manual ? "yes" : "no") + "&8)");
        if (!data.bypassChecks.isEmpty()) {
            send(sender, " &7Bypassed checks: &f" + String.join(", ", data.bypassChecks));
        }
        if (data.exemptTicksLeft() > 0) {
            send(sender, " &7Exempt (/astral exempt): &e" + (data.exemptTicksLeft() / 20) + "s left");
        }
        if (PlayerData.now() < data.combatLockUntil) {
            send(sender, " &7Hits blocked: &c" + ((data.combatLockUntil - PlayerData.now()) / 20 + 1) + "s left");
        }
        send(sender, " &7Frozen: " + (plugin.freeze().isFrozen(target) ? "&bYES" : "&fno") + " &8| &7Gamemode: &f"
                + target.getGameMode().name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        send(sender, " &7Total flags: &f" + data.totalFlags + " &8| &7Total VL: &c" + MathUtil.format(data.totalViolations(), 1));
        send(sender, " &7Top checks: " + plugin.violations().topChecks(data, 5));
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void violations(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "violations");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(target);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&lViolations &7» &f" + target.getName());
        if (data.violations().isEmpty()) {
            send(sender, " &aNo violations.");
        }
        List<Map.Entry<String, Double>> entries = new ArrayList<>(data.violations().entrySet());
        entries.sort((a, b) -> Double.compare(b.getValue(), a.getValue()));
        for (Map.Entry<String, Double> entry : entries) {
            Check check = plugin.checks().get(entry.getKey());
            String name = check == null ? entry.getKey() : check.name();
            String extra = check == null ? "" : " &8(&7alert " + MathUtil.format(check.alertVl(), 1) + " &8| &7punish "
                    + MathUtil.format(check.punishVl(), 1) + "&8)";
            sender.sendMessage(Text.color(" &8- &f" + name + "&7: &c" + MathUtil.format(entry.getValue(), 2) + extra)
                    .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(Text.color(check == null ? "" : "&7" + check.description()))));
        }
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void logs(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "logs");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(target);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        int page = 1;
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            try {
                page = Math.max(1, Integer.parseInt(args[1]));
            } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
                page = 1;
            }
        }
        List<FlagRecord> records = new ArrayList<>(data.flags);
        int pages = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(records.size() / 10.0));
        page = Math.min(page, pages);
        SimpleDateFormat format = new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss");
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&lLogs &7» &f" + target.getName() + " &8(&7page " + page + "/" + pages + "&8)");
        if (records.isEmpty()) {
            send(sender, " &aNo flags this session.");
        }
        for (int i = (page - 1) * 10; i < Math.min(records.size(), page * 10); i++) {
            FlagRecord record = records.get(i);
            sender.sendMessage(Text.color(" &8[" + format.format(new Date(record.time())) + "] &f" + record.check()
                            + " &8(&7" + record.type() + "&8) &7vl &c" + MathUtil.format(record.vl(), 1))
                    .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(Text.color("&7" + record.debug()))));
        }
        if (page < pages) {
            sender.sendMessage(Text.color(" &e» Next page").clickEvent(ClickEvent.runCommand("/astral logs " + target.getName() + " " + (page + 1))));
        }
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void reset(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "reset");
            return;
        }
        if (args[0].equals("*") || args[0].equalsIgnoreCase("all")) {
            for (PlayerData data : plugin.data().all()) {
                data.resetViolations();
            }
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "reset-all");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(target);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            Check check = plugin.checks().get(args[1]);
            if (check == null) {
                plugin.messages().send(sender, "unknown-check", "check", args[1]);
                return;
            }
            data.resetViolations(check.id());
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "reset-check", "check", check.name(), "player", target.getName());
            return;
        }
        data.resetViolations();
        plugin.messages().send(sender, "reset-player", "player", target.getName());
    }

    private void bypass(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "bypass");
            return;
        }
        if (args[0].equalsIgnoreCase("list")) {
            List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
            for (UUID uuid : plugin.bypass().all()) {
                OfflinePlayer player = Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(uuid);
                names.add(player.getName() == null ? uuid.toString() : player.getName());
            }
            for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                if (player.hasPermission("astral.bypass") && !plugin.bypass().contains(player.getUniqueId())) {
                    names.add(player.getName() + " (permission)");
                }
            }
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "bypass-list", "list", names.isEmpty() ? "none" : String.join(", ", names));
            return;
        }
        UUID uuid;
        String name;
        Player online = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[0]);
        if (online != null) {
            uuid = online.getUniqueId();
            name = online.getName();
        } else {
            OfflinePlayer offline = Bukkit.getOfflinePlayerIfCached(args[0]);
            if (offline == null) {
                plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-not-found", "player", args[0]);
                return;
            }
            uuid = offline.getUniqueId();
            name = offline.getName() == null ? args[0] : offline.getName();
        }
        boolean now = plugin.bypass().toggle(uuid);
        if (online != null) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(online);
            if (data != null) {
                plugin.data().refreshBypass(data);
                data.resetViolations();
            }
        }
        plugin.messages().send(sender, now ? "bypass-enabled" : "bypass-disabled", "player", name);
        if (!now && online != null && online.hasPermission("astral.bypass")) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "bypass-permission-note", "player", name);
        }
        plugin.logs().log("BYPASS " + name + " -> " + now + " by " + sender.getName());
    }

    /**
     * /astral exempt &lt;player|*&gt; &lt;seconds|off&gt; [category] - lets minigame plugins (battle bus,
     * launchers, cutscenes) or staff switch checks off for a player for a while. Works from the
     * console, so other plugins can run it as a command.
     */
    private void exempt(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 2) {
            usage(sender, "exempt");
            return;
        }
        List<Player> targets = new ArrayList<>();
        if (args[0].equals("*") || args[0].equalsIgnoreCase("@a")) {
            targets.addAll(Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers());
        } else {
            Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
            if (target == null) {
                return;
            }
            targets.add(target);
        }
        CheckCategory category = CheckCategory.MOVEMENT;
        String categoryName = "movement";
        if (args.length >= 3) {
            categoryName = args[2].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            if (categoryName.equals("all")) {
                category = null;
            } else {
                category = null;
                for (CheckCategory value : CheckCategory.values()) {
                    if (value.display().equalsIgnoreCase(categoryName) || value.name().equalsIgnoreCase(categoryName)) {
                        category = value;
                    }
                }
                if (category == null) {
                    send(sender, prefix() + "&cUnknown category &f" + args[2] + "&c. Use movement, combat, player, world, macro or all.");
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        boolean off = args[1].equalsIgnoreCase("off") || args[1].equals("0");
        int ticks = 0;
        if (!off) {
            try {
                ticks = (int) Math.round(Double.parseDouble(args[1]) * 20.0);
            } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
                send(sender, prefix() + "&c" + args[1] + " is not a number of seconds.");
                return;
            }
            if (ticks <= 0) {
                off = true;
            }
        }
        for (Player target : targets) {
            PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(target);
            if (data == null) {
                continue;
            }
            if (off) {
                data.clearExemptions();
            } else {
                data.exempt(category, ticks);
            }
        }
        String who = targets.size() == 1 ? targets.getFirst().getName() : targets.size() + " players";
        if (off) {
            send(sender, prefix() + "&f" + who + " &ais checked again.");
        } else {
            send(sender, prefix() + "&f" + who + " &7is not checked (&f" + categoryName + "&7) for &f" + args[1] + "s&7.");
        }
        if (sender instanceof Player) {
            plugin.logs().log("EXEMPT " + who + " " + (off ? "off" : args[1] + "s " + categoryName) + " by " + sender.getName());
        }
    }

    private void kick(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "kick");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        String reason = args.length >= 2 ? String.join(" ", Arrays.copyOfRange(args, 1, args.length)) : "Unfair Advantage";
        target.kick(plugin.messages().lines("kick-screen", "reason", reason));
        plugin.messages().send(sender, "kicked", "player", target.getName(), "reason", reason);
        plugin.logs().log("KICK " + target.getName() + " by " + sender.getName() + ": " + reason);
    }

    private void ban(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "ban");
            return;
        }
        Duration duration;
        int reasonStart = 1;
        try {
            if (args.length >= 2 && DurationUtil.isDuration(args[1])) {
                duration = DurationUtil.parse(args[1]);
                reasonStart = 2;
            } else if (args.length >= 2 && (args[1].equalsIgnoreCase("perm") || args[1].equalsIgnoreCase("permanent"))) {
                duration = null;
                reasonStart = 2;
            } else {
                duration = DurationUtil.parse(plugin.settings().defaultBanDuration);
            }
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            duration = null;
        }
        String reason = args.length > reasonStart ? String.join(" ", Arrays.copyOfRange(args, reasonStart, args.length)) : "Unfair Advantage";
        String source = AstralAntiCheat.DISPLAY_NAME + " (" + sender.getName() + ")";
        String expires = duration == null ? "Never" : DurationUtil.format(duration);

        Player online = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[0]);
        String name;
        if (online != null) {
            name = online.getName();
            online.ban(reason, duration, source, false);
            online.kick(plugin.messages().lines("ban-screen", "reason", reason, "expires", expires));
        } else {
            OfflinePlayer offline = Bukkit.getOfflinePlayerIfCached(args[0]);
            if (offline == null) {
                plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-not-found", "player", args[0]);
                return;
            }
            name = offline.getName() == null ? args[0] : offline.getName();
            offline.ban(reason, duration, source);
        }
        plugin.messages().send(sender, "banned", "player", name, "duration", expires, "reason", reason);
        plugin.logs().log("BAN " + name + " for " + expires + " by " + sender.getName() + ": " + reason);
    }

    private void unban(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "unban");
            return;
        }
        OfflinePlayer offline = Bukkit.getOfflinePlayerIfCached(args[0]);
        if (offline == null) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-not-found", "player", args[0]);
            return;
        }
        String name = offline.getName() == null ? args[0] : offline.getName();
        if (!offline.isBanned()) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "not-banned", "player", name);
            return;
        }
        Bukkit.getBanList(BanListType.PROFILE).pardon(offline.getPlayerProfile());
        plugin.messages().send(sender, "unbanned", "player", name);
        plugin.logs().log("UNBAN " + name + " by " + sender.getName());
    }

    private void freeze(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "freeze");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        boolean frozen = plugin.freeze().toggle(target);
        plugin.messages().send(sender, frozen ? "frozen" : "unfrozen", "player", target.getName());
        if (!frozen) {
            plugin.messages().send(target, "unfrozen", "player", target.getName());
        }
        plugin.logs().log((frozen ? "FREEZE " : "UNFREEZE ") + target.getName() + " by " + sender.getName());
    }

    private void teleport(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-only");
            return;
        }
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "tp");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        player.teleport(target.getLocation(), PlayerTeleportEvent.TeleportCause.COMMAND);
        plugin.messages().send(sender, "teleported", "player", target.getName());
    }

    private void cps(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-only");
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (args.length < 1 || (data.cpsTarget != null && Bukkit.getPlayer(data.cpsTarget) != null
                && Bukkit.getPlayer(data.cpsTarget).getName().equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]))) {
            data.cpsTarget = null;
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "cps-stop");
            return;
        }
        Player target = online(sender, args[0]);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.cpsTarget = target.getUniqueId();
        plugin.messages().send(sender, "cps-start", "player", target.getName());
    }

    private void ping(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        Player target;
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            target = online(sender, args[0]);
        } else if (sender instanceof Player player) {
            target = player;
        } else {
            usage(sender, "ping");
            return;
        }
        if (target != null) {
            send(sender, prefix() + "&f" + target.getName() + "&7's ping: &f" + target.getPing() + "ms");
        }
    }

    private void client(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        Player target;
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            target = online(sender, args[0]);
        } else if (sender instanceof Player player) {
            target = player;
        } else {
            usage(sender, "client");
            return;
        }
        if (target != null) {
            clientInfo(sender, target);
        }
    }

    private void gui(CommandSender sender) {
        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-only");
            return;
        }
        plugin.menus().openPlayers(player);
    }

    private void stats(CommandSender sender) {
        long uptime = (System.currentTimeMillis() - plugin.startTime()) / 1000L;
        long bypassing = plugin.data().all().stream().filter(data -> data.bypassPermission).count();
        send(sender, line());
        send(sender, " &#00E5FF&l" + AstralAntiCheat.DISPLAY_NAME + " &7statistics");
        send(sender, " &7Uptime: &f" + DurationUtil.format(Duration.ofSeconds(uptime)));
        send(sender, " &7Flags: &f" + plugin.violations().totalFlags() + " &8| &7Punishments: &f" + plugin.violations().totalPunishments());
        send(sender, " &7Players: &f" + Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers().size() + " &8| &7Bypassing: &f" + bypassing);
        send(sender, " &7Checks: &a" + plugin.checks().enabledCount() + "&7/&f" + plugin.checks().all().size() + " enabled");
        send(sender, " &7TPS: &f" + MathUtil.format(plugin.violations().currentTps(), 2)
                + (plugin.violations().isLagging() ? " &c(lag protection active)" : ""));
        send(sender, line());
    }

    private void debug(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "player-only");
            return;
        }
        if (args.length < 1) {
            usage(sender, "debug");
            return;
        }
        Check check = plugin.checks().get(args[0]);
        if (check == null) {
            plugin.messages().send(sender, "unknown-check", "check", args[0]);
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        boolean enabled;
        if (data.debugChecks.remove(check.id())) {
            enabled = false;
        } else {
            data.debugChecks.add(check.id());
            enabled = true;
        }
        plugin.messages().send(sender, enabled ? "debug-enabled" : "debug-disabled", "check", check.name());
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ tab completion

    @Override
    public List<String> onTabComplete(@NotNull CommandSender sender, @NotNull Command command, @NotNull String alias, @NotNull String[] args) {
        if (args.length == 1) {
            List<String> subs = new ArrayList<>();
            for (String[] entry : COMMANDS) {
                if (sender.hasPermission("astral.command." + entry[0])) {
                    subs.add(entry[0]);
                }
            }
            return filter(subs, args[0]);
        }
        String sub = alias(args[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        if (!isKnown(sub) || !sender.hasPermission("astral.command." + sub)) {
            return List.of();
        }
        if (args.length == 2) {
            return switch (sub) {
                case "version", "info", "violations", "logs", "kick", "ban", "freeze", "tp", "cps", "ping", "client" -> filter(players(), args[1]);
                case "reset" -> filter(concat(players(), List.of("*")), args[1]);
                case "bypass" -> filter(concat(players(), List.of("list")), args[1]);
                case "exempt" -> filter(concat(players(), List.of("*")), args[1]);
                case "toggle", "debug" -> filter(checkIds(), args[1]);
                case "alerts" -> filter(List.of("on", "off"), args[1]);
                case "checks" -> filter(Arrays.stream(CheckCategory.values()).map(c -> c.display().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)).toList(), args[1]);
                case "unban" -> filter(Bukkit.getBannedPlayers().stream().map(OfflinePlayer::getName).filter(n -> n != null).toList(), args[1]);
                default -> List.of();
            };
        }
        if (args.length == 4 && sub.equals("exempt")) {
            return filter(List.of("movement", "combat", "player", "world", "macro", "all"), args[3]);
        }
        if (args.length == 3) {
            return switch (sub) {
                case "reset" -> filter(checkIds(), args[2]);
                case "toggle" -> filter(List.of("on", "off"), args[2]);
                case "exempt" -> filter(List.of("5", "10", "30", "60", "off"), args[2]);
                case "ban" -> filter(List.of("1h", "12h", "1d", "7d", "30d", "perm"), args[2]);
                case "kick" -> filter(List.of("Unfair Advantage", "Cheating"), args[2]);
                default -> List.of();
            };
        }
        return List.of();
    }

    private static List<String> players() {
        return Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers().stream().map(Player::getName).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    private List<String> checkIds() {
        return plugin.checks().all().stream().map(Check::id).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    private static List<String> concat(List<String> a, List<String> b) {
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>(a);
        out.addAll(b);
        return out;
    }

    private static List<String> filter(List<String> values, String prefix) {
        String lower = prefix.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        return values.stream().filter(value -> value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith(lower)).sorted().collect(Collectors.toList());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/config/ConfigUpdater.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config;

import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

/**
 * Brings config.yml files written by older Astral versions up to date. New options are added
 * with their default value, existing values are kept unless a version note below says why
 * a value changes.
 */
public final class ConfigUpdater {

    public static final int CURRENT = 2;

    private ConfigUpdater() {
    }

    /** Returns true when the file was changed (and saved). */
    public static boolean update(JavaPlugin plugin) {
        FileConfiguration config = plugin.getConfig();
        int version = config.getInt("config-version", 1);
        if (version >= CURRENT) {
            return false;
        }
        if (version < 2) {
            // 1.0 only watched. Since 1.1 Astral blocks cheats by default (no automatic kicks / bans),
            // including silent swaps and aim cheats, and sends compact alerts.
            config.set("settings.mitigation", true);
            config.set("checks.aim.mitigate", true);
            config.set("checks.hotbarmacro.mitigate", true);
            plugin.getLogger().info("Updated config.yml to version 2: blocking is now ON (settings.mitigation: true),"
                    + " alerts are compact. Set settings.mitigation: false to only watch.");
        }
        config.set("config-version", CURRENT);
        config.options().copyDefaults(true);
        plugin.saveConfig();
        return true;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/config/Messages.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/** messages.yml */
public final class Messages {

    private final JavaPlugin plugin;
    private YamlConfiguration config;
    private YamlConfiguration defaults;

    public Messages(JavaPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void load() {
        File file = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "messages.yml");
        if (!file.exists()) {
            plugin.saveResource("messages.yml", false);
        }
        config = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(file);
        InputStream stream = plugin.getResource("messages.yml");
        if (stream != null) {
            defaults = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(new InputStreamReader(stream, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            config.setDefaults(defaults);
        }
    }

    /** A section of messages.yml (falls back to the default messages). */
    public ConfigurationSection section(String key) {
        return config.getConfigurationSection(key);
    }

    public String prefix() {
        return config.getString("prefix", "&8[&bAstral&8] &r");
    }

    /** Raw message with %prefix% and the given placeholders replaced (key, value, key, value ...). */
    public String raw(String key, String... placeholders) {
        String message = config.getString(key);
        if (message == null) {
            message = key;
        }
        return apply(message, placeholders);
    }

    public List<String> rawList(String key, String... placeholders) {
        List<String> lines = config.getStringList(key);
        if (lines.isEmpty() && config.isString(key)) {
            lines = List.of(config.getString(key, ""));
        }
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>(lines.size());
        for (String line : lines) {
            out.add(apply(line, placeholders));
        }
        return out;
    }

    public Component get(String key, String... placeholders) {
        return Text.color(raw(key, placeholders));
    }

    public Component lines(String key, String... placeholders) {
        return Text.lines(rawList(key, placeholders));
    }

    public void send(CommandSender sender, String key, String... placeholders) {
        String message = raw(key, placeholders);
        if (!message.isEmpty()) {
            sender.sendMessage(Text.color(message));
        }
    }

    private String apply(String message, String... placeholders) {
        String out = message.replace("%prefix%", prefix());
        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < placeholders.length; i += 2) {
            out = out.replace("%" + placeholders[i] + "%", placeholders[i + 1] == null ? "" : placeholders[i + 1]);
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config;

import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;

import java.util.List;

/** Global options from config.yml. */
public final class Settings {

    public boolean lagProtection;
    public double minTps;
    public int maxCombatPing;
    public int maxPingCompensation;
    public int joinExemptTicks;
    public boolean exemptBedrock;
    /** true = block cheats (cancel hits, setbacks ...), false = watch only (alerts + logs). */
    public boolean mitigation;
    public double maxSetbackDistance;
    /** Lower case names of worlds where nobody is checked. */
    public java.util.Set<String> exemptWorlds = java.util.Set.of();

    public boolean alertsByDefault;
    /** One grouped line per player instead of one line per flag. */
    public boolean alertCompact;
    public long alertGatherMs;
    public long alertIntervalMs;
    public long alertRepeatMs;
    public long alertQuietMs;
    public long alertCooldownMs;
    public boolean alertConsole;
    public String alertClickCommand;

    public int decayIntervalSeconds;

    public boolean punishEnabled;
    public boolean punishBroadcast;
    public String defaultBanDuration;

    public List<String> freezeQuitCommands;
    public List<String> freezeAllowedCommands;

    public boolean logFile;
    public int logMemorySize;

    public void load(FileConfiguration config) {
        lagProtection = config.getBoolean("settings.lag-protection.enabled", true);
        minTps = config.getDouble("settings.lag-protection.min-tps", 17.5);
        maxCombatPing = config.getInt("settings.max-combat-ping", 800);
        maxPingCompensation = config.getInt("settings.max-ping-compensation", 600);
        joinExemptTicks = config.getInt("settings.join-exempt-ticks", 60);
        exemptBedrock = config.getBoolean("settings.exempt-bedrock-players", true);
        mitigation = config.getBoolean("settings.mitigation", true);
        maxSetbackDistance = Math.max(1.0, config.getDouble("settings.max-setback-distance", 12.0));
        java.util.Set<String> worlds = new java.util.HashSet<>();
        for (String world : config.getStringList("settings.exempt-worlds")) {
            worlds.add(world.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT));
        }
        exemptWorlds = worlds;

        alertsByDefault = config.getBoolean("alerts.enabled-by-default", true);
        alertCompact = config.getBoolean("alerts.compact", true);
        alertGatherMs = Math.max(0L, (long) (config.getDouble("alerts.gather-seconds", 1.0) * 1000.0));
        alertIntervalMs = Math.max(0L, (long) (config.getDouble("alerts.interval-seconds", 5.0) * 1000.0));
        alertRepeatMs = Math.max(alertIntervalMs, (long) (config.getDouble("alerts.repeat-seconds", 30.0) * 1000.0));
        alertQuietMs = Math.max(alertRepeatMs, (long) (config.getDouble("alerts.reset-after-seconds", 120.0) * 1000.0));
        alertCooldownMs = config.getLong("alerts.cooldown-ms", 400L);
        alertConsole = config.getBoolean("alerts.console", true);
        alertClickCommand = config.getString("alerts.click-command", "/astral tp %player%");

        decayIntervalSeconds = Math.max(1, config.getInt("violations.decay-interval-seconds", 30));

        punishEnabled = config.getBoolean("punishments.enabled", false);
        punishBroadcast = config.getBoolean("punishments.broadcast", false);
        defaultBanDuration = config.getString("punishments.default-ban-duration", "30d");

        freezeQuitCommands = config.getStringList("freeze.quit-commands");
        freezeAllowedCommands = config.getStringList("freeze.allowed-commands");

        logFile = config.getBoolean("logging.file", true);
        logMemorySize = Math.max(10, config.getInt("logging.memory-size", 150));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/data/Buffer.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data;

/**
 * A small "suspicion" counter. Checks add to it when something looks wrong and remove from it
 * when everything is fine, so one strange tick never flags a player on its own.
 */
public final class Buffer {

    private final PlayerData owner;
    private double value;

    public Buffer() {
        this(null);
    }

    Buffer(PlayerData owner) {
        this.owner = owner;
    }

    public double increase(double amount) {
        value += amount;
        if (owner != null && amount > 0.0) {
            owner.bufferIncreases++;
        }
        return value;
    }

    public double increase() {
        return increase(1.0);
    }

    public double decrease(double amount) {
        value = Math.max(0.0, value - amount);
        return value;
    }

    public double get() {
        return value;
    }

    public void set(double value) {
        this.value = value;
    }

    public void reset() {
        value = 0.0;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/data/PlayerData.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.AlertDigest;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

/**
 * Everything Astral knows about one online player. All fields are only touched on the main
 * thread (chat checks keep their own thread safe state).
 */
public final class PlayerData {

    private static final int NEVER = -100_000;

    private final UUID uuid;
    private final Player player;
    public final int joinTick;
    public final long joinTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ bypass / staff
    public volatile boolean bypassPermission;
    public volatile Set<String> bypassChecks = Collections.emptySet();
    public boolean bedrock;
    public boolean alerts;
    public boolean verbose;
    public final Set<String> debugChecks = new HashSet<>();
    public UUID cpsTarget;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ violations
    private final Map<String, Double> violations = new HashMap<>();
    public final Map<String, Long> lastAlert = new HashMap<>();
    /** Flags waiting for the next compact staff alert. */
    public final AlertDigest alertDigest = new AlertDigest();
    public final Set<String> punished = new HashSet<>();
    public final Deque<FlagRecord> flags = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public int totalFlags;

    private final Map<String, Buffer> buffers = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Class<?>, Object> states = new HashMap<>();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ client ticks
    /** Number of client ticks received (ClientTickEndEvent). */
    public int clientTicks;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ movement (per client tick)
    public boolean hasPosition;
    public boolean resetPosition = true;
    public UUID world;
    public double x, y, z, lastX, lastY, lastZ;
    public float yaw, pitch, lastYaw, lastPitch;
    public double deltaX, deltaY, deltaZ, deltaXZ;
    public double lastDeltaX, lastDeltaY, lastDeltaZ, lastDeltaXZ, lastLastDeltaY;
    public float deltaYaw, deltaPitch, lastDeltaYaw, lastDeltaPitch;
    public double eyeHeight = 1.62;

    /** Ground calculated by Astral from real block collisions. */
    public boolean onGround, lastOnGround, lastLastOnGround;
    /** Ground with a small margin (edges, rounding). */
    public boolean groundLenient;
    /** Ground state the client claims in its movement packets. */
    public boolean clientOnGround, lastClientOnGround;
    public int airTicks, groundTicks;
    public double friction = 0.6, lastFriction = 0.6, lastLastFriction = 0.6;

    public boolean inLiquid, nearLiquid, liquidBelow;
    /** How deep the feet are inside a fluid (0 when not in a fluid). */
    public double liquidDepth;
    public boolean climbing, nearClimbable, inWeb, nearPowderSnow, nearHoney, nearSlime, nearBed, nearPiston, nearScaffolding;
    public boolean headBlocked, horizontalCollision;
    public boolean gliding, swimming, riptiding, inVehicle, flying, sneaking, sprinting;
    public boolean chunkLoaded = true;
    /** True when the movement of this tick must not be checked (teleport, first tick ...). */
    public boolean skipMovement = true;

    public double speedAttribute = 0.1;
    public double jumpStrength = 0.42;
    public double stepHeight = 0.6;
    /** Gravity of the player: the gravity attribute, or 0 while a plugin turned gravity off. */
    public double gravity = 0.08;
    public boolean slowFalling;
    /**
     * Gravity / slow falling before the last change, and when it changed. The client applies such
     * a change about half a round trip later, so for a short while both are possible.
     */
    public double oldGravity = 0.08;
    public boolean oldSlowFalling;
    public int gravityChange = NEVER;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ exemption timestamps (server ticks)
    public int lastTeleport = NEVER, lastVelocity = NEVER, lastFlight = NEVER, lastGlide = NEVER, lastRiptide = NEVER;
    public int lastVehicle = NEVER, lastLiquid = NEVER, lastClimb = NEVER, lastWeb = NEVER, lastPowderSnow = NEVER;
    public int lastHoney = NEVER, lastSlime = NEVER, lastBed = NEVER, lastPiston = NEVER, lastLevitation = NEVER;
    public int lastGameMode = NEVER, lastRespawn = NEVER, lastWorldChange = NEVER, lastGhostBlock = NEVER;
    public int lastFirework = NEVER, lastSpear = NEVER, lastSetback = NEVER, lastHeadBlocked = NEVER;
    public int lastCollision = NEVER, lastScaffolding = NEVER, lastDolphin = NEVER, lastSlowFalling = NEVER;
    public int lastDamage = NEVER, lastPush = NEVER, lastFailMove = NEVER, lastExplosion = NEVER;
    /** Standing on / touching a boat, minecart, shulker or happy ghast (moving platforms). */
    public int lastEntityPlatform = NEVER;
    /** A plugin turned gravity off for the player (Entity#setGravity(false)). */
    public int lastNoGravity = NEVER;
    public boolean nearEntityPlatform;
    /** A fall damage event was cancelled by another plugin (minigames cancel landing damage). */
    public int lastFallCancelled = NEVER;
    /**
     * Paper ignores movement until the client confirms a teleport, so the position stays at the
     * teleport target for about one round trip (a teleport every tick = frozen the whole time).
     */
    public boolean teleportPending;
    public double teleportX, teleportY, teleportZ;
    public int teleportTick = NEVER;
    /** Left a vehicle: the client may still answer once with its old seat position. */
    public boolean dismountPending;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ lag compensation
    /** Bounding boxes of this player for the last 40 server ticks. */
    public final PositionHistory history = new PositionHistory(40);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ velocity
    public final Deque<VelocityRecord> velocities = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public double lastVelocityHorizontal, lastVelocityVertical;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ combat
    public int attacksThisTick, swingsThisTick;
    public final Set<Integer> targetsThisTick = new HashSet<>();
    public int lastAttackTick = NEVER;
    public int lastAttackClientTick = NEVER;
    public int combatLockUntil = NEVER;
    public boolean hasTickSnapshot;
    public double tickEndX, tickEndY, tickEndZ;
    public float tickEndYaw, tickEndPitch;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ clicks / interactions
    public int clicksThisTick;
    private final int[] clickWindow = new int[20];
    private int clickWindowIndex;
    public int placedThisTick, brokenThisTick, inventoryClicksThisTick, slotChangesThisTick;
    /** Attacks, item uses, block / entity interactions and dig starts in the current client tick. */
    public int actionsThisTick;
    /** PlayerMoveEvents (movement packets) in the current client tick. Vanilla sends one at most. */
    public int movesThisTick;
    /** Real client protocol (ViaVersion aware), looked up once. -1 = not looked up yet. */
    public int protocol = -1;
    public boolean attackedBeforeSlotChange;
    public int placeLockUntil = NEVER;
    public boolean digging;
    /** Set when a movement check flagged during the current client tick. */
    public boolean movementFlagged;
    /** Set when a movement check grew a suspicion buffer this tick (the position is not trusted). */
    public boolean movementSuspicious;
    /** Counts every Buffer#increase of this player. */
    public int bufferIncreases;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ setback / nofall
    /** Last position on real ground that passed every movement check. */
    public Location lastSafe;
    /** Last position (anywhere, also in the air) that passed every movement check. */
    public boolean hasLegit;
    public double legitX, legitY, legitZ;
    public UUID legitWorld;
    public double fallDistance;
    /**
     * Highest fall distance the server can legitimately have for this player (sum of the downward
     * moves since the client last said "on ground" or touched water). Used by the mace check.
     */
    public double legitFall;
    public boolean groundSpoofed;
    public double fallDamageTaken;

    public PlayerData(Player player) {
        this.player = player;
        this.uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        this.joinTick = Bukkit.getCurrentTick();
    }

    public UUID uuid() {
        return uuid;
    }

    public Player player() {
        return player;
    }

    public String name() {
        return player.getName();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers

    public static int now() {
        return Bukkit.getCurrentTick();
    }

    /** Server ticks since a stored timestamp. */
    public static int since(int tick) {
        return now() - tick;
    }

    public boolean isBypassing(String checkId) {
        return bypassPermission || bypassChecks.contains(checkId);
    }

    /** Temporary exemptions from /astral exempt (null key = every category). Server tick it ends at. */
    private final Map<CheckCategory, Integer> exemptUntil = new HashMap<>();
    private int exemptAllUntil = NEVER;

    /** Exempts the player for some server ticks. category null = from every check. */
    public void exempt(CheckCategory category, int ticks) {
        int until = now() + ticks;
        if (category == null) {
            exemptAllUntil = until;
        } else {
            exemptUntil.put(category, until);
        }
    }

    public void clearExemptions() {
        exemptAllUntil = NEVER;
        exemptUntil.clear();
    }

    public boolean isExempt(CheckCategory category) {
        int now = now();
        if (now < exemptAllUntil) {
            return true;
        }
        Integer until = exemptUntil.get(category);
        return until != null && now < until;
    }

    /** Server ticks left of the longest running exemption (0 = none). */
    public int exemptTicksLeft() {
        int now = now();
        int max = exemptAllUntil - now;
        for (int until : exemptUntil.values()) {
            max = Math.max(max, until - now);
        }
        return Math.max(0, max);
    }

    public Buffer buffer(String key) {
        return buffers.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> new Buffer(this));
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T> T state(Class<T> type, Supplier<T> creator) {
        return (T) states.computeIfAbsent(type, k -> creator.get());
    }

    public double violation(String check) {
        return violations.getOrDefault(check, 0.0);
    }

    public double addViolation(String check, double amount) {
        double value = violation(check) + amount;
        violations.put(check, value);
        return value;
    }

    public void setViolation(String check, double value) {
        if (value <= 0.0) {
            violations.remove(check);
            punished.remove(check);
        } else {
            violations.put(check, value);
        }
    }

    public Map<String, Double> violations() {
        return violations;
    }

    public double totalViolations() {
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (double v : violations.values()) {
            sum += v;
        }
        return sum;
    }

    public void resetViolations() {
        violations.clear();
        punished.clear();
        buffers.clear();
    }

    public void resetViolations(String check) {
        violations.remove(check);
        punished.remove(check);
    }

    /** Called once per client tick to roll the CPS window. */
    public void rollClickWindow() {
        clickWindowIndex = (clickWindowIndex + 1) % clickWindow.length;
        clickWindow[clickWindowIndex] = clicksThisTick;
    }

    /** Clicks in the last 20 client ticks (= one second). */
    public int cps() {
        int sum = 0;
        for (int c : clickWindow) {
            sum += c;
        }
        return sum;
    }

    public int ping() {
        return player.getPing();
    }

    /** Ping converted to ticks (rounded up). */
    public int pingTicks() {
        return (int) Math.ceil(Math.max(0, ping()) / 50.0);
    }

    public boolean inCombat() {
        return since(lastAttackTick) < 60;
    }

    /** Server ticks after leaving a vehicle during which the client can still be on the seat. */
    public int vehicleExitTicks() {
        return 20 + pingTicks();
    }

    /** Riding, or just got off (the client sees its vehicle late, so its own eyes trail behind). */
    public boolean recentlyRode() {
        return inVehicle || since(lastVehicle) < vehicleExitTicks();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/data/PlayerDataManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.ClientUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class PlayerDataManager {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private final Map<UUID, PlayerData> data = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public PlayerDataManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public PlayerData create(Player player) {
        PlayerData created = new PlayerData(player);
        created.bedrock = ClientUtil.isBedrock(player.getUniqueId());
        created.alerts = player.hasPermission("astral.alerts") && plugin.settings().alertsByDefault;
        data.put(player.getUniqueId(), created);
        refreshBypass(created);
        return created;
    }

    public PlayerData get(Player player) {
        return data.get(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    public PlayerData get(UUID uuid) {
        return data.get(uuid);
    }

    public PlayerData remove(Player player) {
        return data.remove(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    public Collection<PlayerData> all() {
        return data.values();
    }

    /** Re-reads astral.bypass, astral.bypass.<check> and the /astral bypass list. */
    public void refreshBypass(PlayerData playerData) {
        Player player = playerData.player();
        playerData.bypassPermission = player.hasPermission("astral.bypass")
                || plugin.bypass().contains(player.getUniqueId());
        Set<String> checks = new HashSet<>();
        for (Check check : plugin.checks().all()) {
            if (player.hasPermission("astral.bypass." + check.id())) {
                checks.add(check.id());
            }
        }
        playerData.bypassChecks = checks;
    }

    /** Boxes of every player for lag compensation (end of tick, labelled with the next tick). */
    public void recordHistory(int tick) {
        for (PlayerData playerData : data.values()) {
            Player player = playerData.player();
            if (player.isOnline()) {
                playerData.history.add(tick, player.getBoundingBox());
            }
        }
    }

    /** Runs every server tick. */
    public void tick() {
        int tick = Bukkit.getCurrentTick();
        boolean refresh = tick % 20 == 0;
        for (PlayerData playerData : data.values()) {
            Player player = playerData.player();
            if (!player.isOnline()) {
                continue;
            }
            if (refresh) {
                refreshBypass(playerData);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/data/PositionHistory.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data;

import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Ring buffer with the bounding box of a player for the last server ticks. Used for lag
 * compensation: an attacker sees the target where it was "ping" milliseconds ago.
 */
public final class PositionHistory {

    private final int size;
    private final int[] ticks;
    private final double[][] boxes;
    private int head;
    private int count;

    public PositionHistory(int size) {
        this.size = size;
        this.ticks = new int[size];
        this.boxes = new double[size][6];
    }

    public void add(int tick, BoundingBox box) {
        double[] b = boxes[head];
        b[0] = box.getMinX();
        b[1] = box.getMinY();
        b[2] = box.getMinZ();
        b[3] = box.getMaxX();
        b[4] = box.getMaxY();
        b[5] = box.getMaxZ();
        ticks[head] = tick;
        head = (head + 1) % size;
        if (count < size) {
            count++;
        }
    }

    /** Boxes recorded between {@code fromTick} and {@code toTick} (inclusive). */
    public List<BoundingBox> between(int fromTick, int toTick) {
        List<BoundingBox> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            int index = (head - 1 - i + size) % size;
            int tick = ticks[index];
            if (tick < fromTick || tick > toTick) {
                continue;
            }
            double[] b = boxes[index];
            out.add(new BoundingBox(b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3], b[4], b[5]));
        }
        return out;
    }

    public void clear() {
        count = 0;
        head = 0;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/data/VelocityRecord.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data;

/** Knockback / velocity the server sent to a player, waiting to be confirmed by movement. */
public final class VelocityRecord {

    public final double x;
    public final double y;
    public final double z;
    public final double horizontal;
    /** Client tick counter when the velocity was sent. */
    public final int clientTick;
    /** Amount of client ticks the player has to take the velocity. */
    public final int window;
    public final boolean checkVertical;
    public final boolean checkHorizontal;

    public boolean verticalTaken;
    public boolean horizontalTaken;
    public double maxDeltaY;
    public double maxDeltaXZ;
    public boolean invalid;
    public boolean horizontalInvalid;
    public boolean evaluated;

    public VelocityRecord(double x, double y, double z, int clientTick, int window, boolean checkVertical, boolean checkHorizontal) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.z = z;
        this.horizontal = Math.hypot(x, z);
        this.clientTick = clientTick;
        this.window = window;
        this.checkVertical = checkVertical;
        this.checkHorizontal = checkHorizontal;
    }
}
```
