`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/gui/MenuManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.gui;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.ClientUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryDragEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.Inventory;
import org.bukkit.inventory.InventoryHolder;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.SkullMeta;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/** /astral gui - staff menus. */
public final class MenuManager implements Listener {

    private enum Type {
        PLAYERS, PLAYER, CHECKS
    }

    /** Marks Astral inventories. */
    private static final class Menu implements InventoryHolder {
        private final Type type;
        private final UUID target;
        private final List<UUID> slots = new ArrayList<>();
        private final List<Check> checks = new ArrayList<>();
        private Inventory inventory;

        private Menu(Type type, UUID target) {
            this.type = type;
            this.target = target;
        }

        @Override
        public Inventory getInventory() {
            return inventory;
        }
    }

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;

    public MenuManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ menus

    public void openPlayers(Player viewer) {
        Menu menu = new Menu(Type.PLAYERS, null);
        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(menu, 54, Text.color("&8Astral &7» &fPlayers"));
        menu.inventory = inventory;

        List<PlayerData> sorted = new ArrayList<>(plugin.data().all());
        sorted.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble(PlayerData::totalViolations).reversed());
        int slot = 0;
        for (PlayerData data : sorted) {
            if (slot >= 45) {
                break;
            }
            Player player = data.player();
            ItemStack head = new ItemStack(Material.PLAYER_HEAD);
            SkullMeta meta = (SkullMeta) head.getItemMeta();
            meta.setOwningPlayer(player);
            meta.displayName(Text.item((data.totalViolations() > 0 ? "&c" : "&a") + player.getName()));
            meta.lore(Text.lore(List.of(
                    "&7Total VL: &c" + MathUtil.format(data.totalViolations(), 1),
                    "&7Top: " + plugin.violations().topChecks(data, 3),
                    "&7Ping: &f" + data.ping() + "ms &8| &7CPS: &f" + data.cps(),
                    "&7Client: &f" + ClientUtil.describe(player),
                    "&7Bypass: " + (data.bypassPermission ? "&aYes" : "&cNo"),
                    "",
                    "&eClick for actions")));
            head.setItemMeta(meta);
            inventory.setItem(slot, head);
            menu.slots.add(player.getUniqueId());
            slot++;
        }
        inventory.setItem(45, item(Material.BOOK, "&b" + AstralAntiCheat.DISPLAY_NAME + " AntiCheat",
                "&7Mode: " + (plugin.settings().mitigation ? "&cBLOCK" : "&aWATCH (alerts only)"),
                "&7Version: &f" + plugin.getPluginMeta().getVersion(),
                "&7Flags: &f" + plugin.violations().totalFlags(),
                "&7Punishments: &f" + plugin.violations().totalPunishments()));
        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.COMPARATOR, "&bChecks", "&7Enable / disable checks"));
        inventory.setItem(53, item(Material.BARRIER, "&cClose"));
        viewer.openInventory(inventory);
    }

    public void openPlayer(Player viewer, Player target) {
        Menu menu = new Menu(Type.PLAYER, target.getUniqueId());
        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(menu, 27, Text.color("&8Astral &7» &f" + target.getName()));
        menu.inventory = inventory;
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(target);
        boolean bypass = data != null && data.bypassPermission;
        boolean frozen = plugin.freeze().isFrozen(target);

        ItemStack head = new ItemStack(Material.PLAYER_HEAD);
        SkullMeta meta = (SkullMeta) head.getItemMeta();
        meta.setOwningPlayer(target);
        meta.displayName(Text.item("&b" + target.getName()));
        if (data != null) {
            meta.lore(Text.lore(List.of(
                    "&7Total VL: &c" + MathUtil.format(data.totalViolations(), 1),
                    "&7Flags: &f" + data.totalFlags,
                    "&7Ping: &f" + data.ping() + "ms",
                    "&7Client: &f" + ClientUtil.describe(target))));
        }
        head.setItemMeta(meta);
        inventory.setItem(4, head);
        inventory.setItem(10, item(Material.ENDER_PEARL, "&bTeleport", "&7Teleport to the player"));
        inventory.setItem(11, item(Material.BOOK, "&bInfo", "&7Show the full profile in chat"));
        inventory.setItem(12, item(Material.WATER_BUCKET, "&bReset violations", "&7Clear every VL of the player"));
        inventory.setItem(13, item(bypass ? Material.LIME_DYE : Material.GRAY_DYE, bypass ? "&aBypass: ON" : "&cBypass: OFF",
                "&7Toggle /astral bypass"));
        inventory.setItem(14, item(frozen ? Material.PACKED_ICE : Material.ICE, frozen ? "&bFrozen: YES" : "&7Frozen: NO",
                "&7Freeze / unfreeze"));
        inventory.setItem(15, item(Material.CLOCK, "&bWatch CPS", "&7Show the CPS in your action bar"));
        inventory.setItem(16, item(Material.PAPER, "&bViolations", "&7Violation level per check"));
        inventory.setItem(22, item(Material.WRITABLE_BOOK, "&bLogs", "&7Every recent flag of the player"));
        inventory.setItem(18, item(Material.ARROW, "&7Back"));
        viewer.openInventory(inventory);
    }

    public void openChecks(Player viewer) {
        Menu menu = new Menu(Type.CHECKS, null);
        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(menu, 54, Text.color("&8Astral &7» &fChecks"));
        menu.inventory = inventory;
        int slot = 0;
        for (Check check : plugin.checks().all()) {
            if (slot >= 45) {
                break;
            }
            ItemStack item = new ItemStack(check.enabled() ? Material.LIME_DYE : Material.GRAY_DYE);
            ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();
            meta.displayName(Text.item((check.enabled() ? "&a" : "&c") + check.name()));
            meta.lore(Text.lore(List.of(
                    "&7Category: " + check.category().color() + check.category().display(),
                    "&7" + check.description(),
                    "&7Flags since start: &f" + check.totalFlags(),
                    "",
                    check.enabled() ? "&eClick to disable" : "&eClick to enable")));
            item.setItemMeta(meta);
            inventory.setItem(slot, item);
            menu.checks.add(check);
            slot++;
        }
        inventory.setItem(45, item(Material.ARROW, "&7Back"));
        viewer.openInventory(inventory);
    }

    private static ItemStack item(Material material, String name, String... lore) {
        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material);
        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();
        meta.displayName(Text.item(name));
        if (lore.length > 0) {
            meta.lore(Text.lore(List.of(lore)));
        }
        item.setItemMeta(meta);
        return item;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ clicks

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getView().getTopInventory().getHolder(false) instanceof Menu menu)) {
            return;
        }
        event.setCancelled(true);
        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player viewer) || event.getClickedInventory() == null
                || !event.getClickedInventory().equals(event.getView().getTopInventory())) {
            return;
        }
        int slot = event.getRawSlot();
        switch (menu.type) {
            case PLAYERS -> {
                if (slot < menu.slots.size()) {
                    Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(menu.slots.get(slot));
                    if (target != null) {
                        openPlayer(viewer, target);
                    }
                } else if (slot == 49) {
                    openChecks(viewer);
                } else if (slot == 53) {
                    viewer.closeInventory();
                }
            }
            case CHECKS -> {
                if (slot < menu.checks.size()) {
                    Check check = menu.checks.get(slot);
                    viewer.performCommand("astral toggle " + check.id());
                    openChecks(viewer);
                } else if (slot == 45) {
                    openPlayers(viewer);
                }
            }
            case PLAYER -> {
                Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(menu.target);
                if (target == null) {
                    openPlayers(viewer);
                    return;
                }
                String name = target.getName();
                switch (slot) {
                    case 10 -> {
                        viewer.closeInventory();
                        viewer.performCommand("astral tp " + name);
                    }
                    case 11 -> {
                        viewer.closeInventory();
                        viewer.performCommand("astral info " + name);
                    }
                    case 12 -> {
                        viewer.performCommand("astral reset " + name);
                        openPlayer(viewer, target);
                    }
                    case 13 -> {
                        viewer.performCommand("astral bypass " + name);
                        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> openPlayer(viewer, target));
                    }
                    case 14 -> {
                        viewer.performCommand("astral freeze " + name);
                        openPlayer(viewer, target);
                    }
                    case 15 -> {
                        viewer.closeInventory();
                        viewer.performCommand("astral cps " + name);
                    }
                    case 16 -> {
                        viewer.closeInventory();
                        viewer.performCommand("astral violations " + name);
                    }
                    case 22 -> {
                        viewer.closeInventory();
                        viewer.performCommand("astral logs " + name);
                    }
                    case 18 -> openPlayers(viewer);
                    default -> {
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onDrag(InventoryDragEvent event) {
        if (event.getView().getTopInventory().getHolder(false) instanceof Menu) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/listener/DataListener.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener;

import com.destroystokyo.paper.event.player.PlayerElytraBoostEvent;
import com.destroystokyo.paper.event.server.ServerTickEndEvent;
import io.papermc.paper.event.entity.EntityKnockbackEvent;
import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PlayerFailMoveEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.VelocityRecord;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockExplodeEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPistonExtendEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPistonRetractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDismountEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityExplodeEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityMountEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityToggleGlideEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.PlayerDeathEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerChangedWorldEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerGameModeChangeEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRespawnEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRiptideEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerToggleFlightEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerVelocityEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

/**
 * Keeps {@link PlayerData} up to date with everything that can legitimately change how a
 * player moves (teleports, knockback, elytra, pistons ...) so checks can exempt it.
 */
public final class DataListener implements Listener {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;

    public DataListener(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    private PlayerData data(Player player) {
        return plugin.data().get(player);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().create(event.getPlayer());
        data.lastSafe = event.getPlayer().getLocation();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        plugin.data().remove(event.getPlayer());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.lastTeleport = PlayerData.now();
        data.resetPosition = true;
        data.velocities.clear();
        data.fallDistance = 0.0;
        data.groundSpoofed = false;
        Location to = event.getTo();
        data.teleportPending = true;
        data.teleportTick = PlayerData.now();
        data.teleportX = to.getX();
        data.teleportY = to.getY();
        data.teleportZ = to.getZ();
        if (to.getWorld() != null) {
            // where the server put the player is legit, but it is not known to be ground
            // (minigames teleport players into the air - a battle bus, a drop ship ...)
            data.lastSafe = null;
            data.hasLegit = true;
            data.legitX = to.getX();
            data.legitY = to.getY();
            data.legitZ = to.getZ();
            data.legitWorld = to.getWorld().getUID();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.lastRespawn = PlayerData.now();
            data.lastTeleport = PlayerData.now();
            data.resetPosition = true;
            data.lastSafe = null;
            data.hasLegit = false;
            data.velocities.clear();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onDeath(PlayerDeathEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.resetPosition = true;
            data.velocities.clear();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onWorldChange(PlayerChangedWorldEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.lastWorldChange = PlayerData.now();
            data.lastTeleport = PlayerData.now();
            data.resetPosition = true;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onGameMode(PlayerGameModeChangeEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.lastGameMode = PlayerData.now();
            data.lastFlight = PlayerData.now();
        }
    }

    // not ignoreCancelled: the client already started flying before a plugin cancelled it
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onToggleFlight(PlayerToggleFlightEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.lastFlight = PlayerData.now();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVelocity(PlayerVelocityEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        addVelocity(data, event.getVelocity(), true);
    }

    /** Explosion / wind charge / mace knockback (sent to the client inside other packets). */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onKnockback(EntityKnockbackEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player)) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        Vector knockback = event.getKnockback();
        // approximation only: used to exempt movement, never to verify anti-knockback
        addVelocity(data, player.getVelocity().clone().add(knockback), false);
        if (event.getCause() == EntityKnockbackEvent.Cause.EXPLOSION) {
            data.lastExplosion = PlayerData.now();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onEntityExplode(EntityExplodeEvent event) {
        markExplosion(event.getLocation());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBlockExplode(BlockExplodeEvent event) {
        markExplosion(event.getBlock().getLocation());
    }

    private void markExplosion(Location location) {
        if (location.getWorld() == null) {
            return;
        }
        int now = PlayerData.now();
        for (Player player : location.getWorld().getPlayers()) {
            if (player.getLocation().distanceSquared(location) < 16 * 16) {
                PlayerData data = data(player);
                if (data != null) {
                    data.lastExplosion = now;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void addVelocity(PlayerData data, Vector velocity, boolean verify) {
        data.lastVelocity = PlayerData.now();
        data.lastVelocityHorizontal = Math.hypot(velocity.getX(), velocity.getZ());
        data.lastVelocityVertical = velocity.getY();
        data.fallDistance = 0.0;
        int window = Math.min(40, data.pingTicks() + 6);
        boolean vertical = verify && data.onGround && velocity.getY() > 0.15 && velocity.getY() < 1.5;
        boolean horizontal = verify && Math.hypot(velocity.getX(), velocity.getZ()) > 0.25;
        data.velocities.addLast(new VelocityRecord(velocity.getX(), velocity.getY(), velocity.getZ(),
                data.clientTicks, window, vertical, horizontal));
        while (data.velocities.size() > 20) {
            data.velocities.removeFirst();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player)) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.lastDamage = PlayerData.now();
        if (event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.FALL) {
            data.fallDamageTaken += event.getDamage();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onElytraBoost(PlayerElytraBoostEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.lastFirework = PlayerData.now();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onRiptide(PlayerRiptideEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.lastRiptide = PlayerData.now();
        }
    }

    // not ignoreCancelled: the client already started gliding before a plugin cancelled it
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onGlide(EntityToggleGlideEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = data(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.lastGlide = PlayerData.now();
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMount(EntityMountEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = data(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.lastVehicle = PlayerData.now();
                data.lastSafe = null; // the ground before boarding (a bus) is no setback target later
            }
        }
    }

    /** Minigames cancel landing damage (battle royale drops) - NoFall must not hand it out then. */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onFallDamageCancelled(EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (event.isCancelled() && event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.FALL
                && event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = data(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.lastFallCancelled = PlayerData.now();
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Lag compensation history, recorded at the end of the tick: riders are on their seat then
     * (Paper snaps a vehicle driver to the vehicle's feet while handling its packet).
     */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onServerTickEnd(ServerTickEndEvent event) {
        plugin.data().recordHistory(PlayerData.now() + 1);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDismount(EntityDismountEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) {
            PlayerData data = data(player);
            if (data != null) {
                data.lastVehicle = PlayerData.now();
                data.resetPosition = true;
                data.dismountPending = true;
                data.lastSafe = null;
                data.hasLegit = false;
            }
        }
    }

    /** Paper snaps the player back without a teleport event when a move is rejected. */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onFailMove(PlayerFailMoveEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null && !event.isAllowed()) {
            data.lastFailMove = PlayerData.now();
            data.resetPosition = true;
        }
    }

    /** Another plugin cancelled the move (regions, combat tag ...) - the player is snapped back. */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onCancelledMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!event.isCancelled()) {
            return;
        }
        PlayerData data = data(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.lastTeleport = PlayerData.now();
            data.resetPosition = true;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPistonExtend(BlockPistonExtendEvent event) {
        markPiston(event.getBlock());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPistonRetract(BlockPistonRetractEvent event) {
        markPiston(event.getBlock());
    }

    private void markPiston(Block piston) {
        int now = PlayerData.now();
        for (Entity entity : piston.getWorld().getNearbyEntities(piston.getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), 14, 14, 14)) {
            if (entity instanceof Player player) {
                PlayerData data = data(player);
                if (data != null) {
                    data.lastPiston = now;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /** A cancelled place / break next to the player leaves a "ghost block" on the client for a moment. */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onGhostPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (event.isCancelled()) {
            markGhost(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlock());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onGhostBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (event.isCancelled()) {
            markGhost(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlock());
        }
    }

    private void markGhost(Player player, Block block) {
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        Location location = player.getLocation();
        if (Math.abs(block.getX() + 0.5 - location.getX()) < 2.5
                && Math.abs(block.getZ() + 0.5 - location.getZ()) < 2.5
                && block.getY() - location.getY() < 3.0
                && location.getY() - block.getY() < 3.0) {
            data.lastGhostBlock = PlayerData.now();
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/listener/InteractionListener.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PlayerArmSwingEvent;
import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import org.bukkit.FluidCollisionMode;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockDamageAbortEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.EquipmentSlot;

/**
 * Tracks attacks, arm swings (clicks) and digging, shared by many combat and macro checks.
 */
public final class InteractionListener implements Listener {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;

    public InteractionListener(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)
    public void onAttack(PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.attacksThisTick++;
        data.actionsThisTick++;
        data.targetsThisTick.add(event.getAttacked().getEntityId());
        data.lastAttackTick = PlayerData.now();
        data.lastAttackClientTick = data.clientTicks;
        if (MovementProcessor.isSpear(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand())) {
            data.lastSpear = PlayerData.now();
        }
        // a combat check flagged the player recently -> block every hit for a while
        if (PlayerData.now() < data.combatLockUntil && !data.isBypassing("killaura")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    /** Item use, block clicks and dig starts - every one of them is an "action" packet. */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        if (event.getAction() == Action.PHYSICAL || event.getAction() == Action.LEFT_CLICK_AIR) {
            return; // physical = pressure plates, left click air = fired by the arm swing itself
        }
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.actionsThisTick++;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteractEntity(PlayerInteractEntityEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.actionsThisTick++;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onSwing(PlayerArmSwingEvent event) {
        if (event.getHand() != EquipmentSlot.HAND) {
            return;
        }
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.swingsThisTick++;
        if (data.attacksThisTick > 0) {
            data.clicksThisTick++;
            return;
        }
        if (data.digging) {
            return;
        }
        // swinging at a block (mining / block hitting) is not a combat click
        double range = player.getGameMode() == org.bukkit.GameMode.CREATIVE ? 5.0 : 4.5;
        Block target = player.getTargetBlockExact((int) Math.ceil(range), FluidCollisionMode.NEVER);
        if (target != null && !target.getType().isAir()
                && target.getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5).distanceSquared(player.getEyeLocation()) <= (range + 0.5) * (range + 0.5)) {
            return;
        }
        data.clicksThisTick++;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDigStart(BlockDamageEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.digging = !event.getInstaBreak();
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onDigAbort(BlockDamageAbortEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.digging = false;
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(event.getPlayer());
        if (data != null) {
            data.digging = false;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/listener/MovementProcessor.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener;

import io.papermc.paper.event.packet.ClientTickEndEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.TickCheck;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.Tag;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * Heart of Astral. Since Minecraft 1.21.2 every client sends a "tick end" packet once per client
 * tick, right after its movement packet. Paper exposes it as {@link ClientTickEndEvent}, which
 * lets Astral read the exact position of every single client tick and simulate vanilla physics,
 * instead of guessing from PlayerMoveEvent.
 */
public final class MovementProcessor implements Listener {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private boolean errorLogged;

    public MovementProcessor(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onTickEnd(ClientTickEndEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData data = plugin.data().get(player);
        if (data == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.clientTicks++;
        update(player, data);

        for (TickCheck check : plugin.checks().tickChecks()) {
            try {
                check.onTick(data);
            } catch (Throwable throwable) {
                if (!errorLogged) {
                    errorLogged = true;
                    plugin.getLogger().log(Level.WARNING, "Error in check " + check.getClass().getSimpleName(), throwable);
                }
            }
        }
        finish(player, data);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation") // Player#isOnGround is client controlled - that is exactly what NoFall compares
    private void update(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        Location location = player.getLocation();
        World world = location.getWorld();
        int now = PlayerData.now();

        data.lastLastOnGround = data.lastOnGround;
        data.lastOnGround = data.onGround;
        data.lastClientOnGround = data.clientOnGround;
        data.lastLastFriction = data.lastFriction;
        data.lastFriction = data.friction;

        data.lastLastDeltaY = data.lastDeltaY;
        data.lastDeltaX = data.deltaX;
        data.lastDeltaY = data.deltaY;
        data.lastDeltaZ = data.deltaZ;
        data.lastDeltaXZ = data.deltaXZ;
        data.lastDeltaYaw = data.deltaYaw;
        data.lastDeltaPitch = data.deltaPitch;

        data.lastX = data.x;
        data.lastY = data.y;
        data.lastZ = data.z;
        data.lastYaw = data.yaw;
        data.lastPitch = data.pitch;

        data.x = location.getX();
        data.y = location.getY();
        data.z = location.getZ();
        data.yaw = location.getYaw();
        data.pitch = location.getPitch();

        boolean worldChanged = data.world == null || !data.world.equals(world.getUID());
        if (data.teleportPending) {
            boolean atTarget = Math.abs(data.x - data.teleportX) < 1.0E-4 && Math.abs(data.y - data.teleportY) < 1.0E-4
                    && Math.abs(data.z - data.teleportZ) < 1.0E-4;
            if (atTarget && PlayerData.since(data.teleportTick) < 2 * data.pingTicks() + 25) {
                // Paper waits for the client to confirm the teleport: the position is frozen and the
                // ground state is the old one, nothing to check yet
                data.resetPosition = true;
            } else {
                data.teleportPending = false;
                data.resetPosition = true;
                data.lastTeleport = now; // the grace after a teleport starts when the client really is there
            }
        }
        if (data.dismountPending && data.hasPosition && (data.x != data.lastX || data.y != data.lastY || data.z != data.lastZ)) {
            // first position after getting off: may still be the old seat position of the client
            data.dismountPending = false;
            data.resetPosition = true;
        }
        if (!data.hasPosition || data.resetPosition || worldChanged) {
            data.lastX = data.x;
            data.lastY = data.y;
            data.lastZ = data.z;
            data.lastYaw = data.yaw;
            data.lastPitch = data.pitch;
            data.deltaX = data.deltaY = data.deltaZ = data.deltaXZ = 0.0;
            data.lastDeltaX = data.lastDeltaY = data.lastDeltaZ = data.lastDeltaXZ = data.lastLastDeltaY = 0.0;
            data.deltaYaw = data.deltaPitch = data.lastDeltaYaw = data.lastDeltaPitch = 0.0F;
            data.hasPosition = true;
            data.resetPosition = false;
            data.world = world.getUID();
            data.skipMovement = true;
            data.fallDistance = 0.0;
            data.groundSpoofed = false;
        } else {
            data.deltaX = data.x - data.lastX;
            data.deltaY = data.y - data.lastY;
            data.deltaZ = data.z - data.lastZ;
            data.deltaXZ = Math.hypot(data.deltaX, data.deltaZ);
            data.deltaYaw = MathUtil.wrapDegrees(data.yaw - data.lastYaw);
            data.deltaPitch = data.pitch - data.lastPitch;
            data.skipMovement = false;
        }

        data.clientOnGround = player.isOnGround();
        // the server resets the fall distance when the client says "on ground" and in water
        if (data.clientOnGround || player.isInWater() || player.isInLava()) {
            data.legitFall = 0.0;
        } else if (data.deltaY < 0.0) {
            data.legitFall -= data.deltaY;
        }
        data.gliding = player.isGliding();
        data.swimming = player.isSwimming();
        data.riptiding = player.isRiptiding();
        data.inVehicle = player.isInsideVehicle();
        data.flying = player.isFlying();
        data.sneaking = player.isSneaking();
        data.sprinting = player.isSprinting();
        data.eyeHeight = player.getEyeHeight();
        data.climbing = player.isClimbing();

        data.speedAttribute = attribute(player, Attribute.MOVEMENT_SPEED, 0.1);
        data.jumpStrength = attribute(player, Attribute.JUMP_STRENGTH, 0.42);
        data.stepHeight = attribute(player, Attribute.STEP_HEIGHT, 0.6);
        double gravity = player.hasGravity() ? attribute(player, Attribute.GRAVITY, 0.08) : 0.0;
        boolean slowFalling = player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SLOW_FALLING);
        if (gravity != data.gravity || slowFalling != data.slowFalling) {
            data.oldGravity = data.gravity;
            data.oldSlowFalling = data.slowFalling;
            data.gravityChange = now;
        }
        data.gravity = gravity;
        data.slowFalling = slowFalling;

        BoundingBox box = player.getBoundingBox();
        data.chunkLoaded = BlockUtil.isLoaded(world, box);
        if (!data.chunkLoaded) {
            data.skipMovement = true;
            return;
        }
        scan(world, box, data);
        // standing on a boat / shulker / happy ghast is "ground" Astral can not see in the blocks
        data.nearEntityPlatform = (!data.onGround || data.clientOnGround != data.onGround || data.horizontalCollision)
                && BlockUtil.touchesPlatform(player, box);

        // ----- exemption timestamps
        if (data.inLiquid) data.lastLiquid = now;
        if (data.climbing || data.nearClimbable) data.lastClimb = now;
        if (data.inWeb) data.lastWeb = now;
        if (data.nearPowderSnow) data.lastPowderSnow = now;
        if (data.nearHoney) data.lastHoney = now;
        if (data.nearSlime) data.lastSlime = now;
        if (data.nearBed) data.lastBed = now;
        if (data.nearPiston) data.lastPiston = now;
        if (data.nearScaffolding) data.lastScaffolding = now;
        if (data.headBlocked) data.lastHeadBlocked = now;
        if (data.horizontalCollision) data.lastCollision = now;
        if (data.gliding) data.lastGlide = now;
        if (data.riptiding) data.lastRiptide = now;
        if (data.inVehicle) data.lastVehicle = now;
        if (data.nearEntityPlatform) data.lastEntityPlatform = now;
        if (!player.hasGravity()) data.lastNoGravity = now;
        GameMode mode = player.getGameMode();
        if (data.flying || mode == GameMode.CREATIVE || mode == GameMode.SPECTATOR) data.lastFlight = now;
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.LEVITATION)) data.lastLevitation = now;
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SLOW_FALLING)) data.lastSlowFalling = now;
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.DOLPHINS_GRACE)) data.lastDolphin = now;
        if (player.hasActiveItem() && isSpear(player.getActiveItem())) data.lastSpear = now;

        if (data.onGround) {
            data.groundTicks++;
            data.airTicks = 0;
        } else {
            data.airTicks++;
            data.groundTicks = 0;
        }
    }

    private void scan(World world, BoundingBox box, PlayerData data) {
        double minX = box.getMinX();
        double minY = box.getMinY();
        double minZ = box.getMinZ();
        double maxX = box.getMaxX();
        double maxY = box.getMaxY();
        double maxZ = box.getMaxZ();

        data.onGround = BlockUtil.collides(world, minX, minY - 0.03, minZ, maxX, minY, maxZ);
        data.groundLenient = data.onGround
                || BlockUtil.collides(world, minX - 0.05, minY - 0.1, minZ - 0.05, maxX + 0.05, minY, maxZ + 0.05);
        data.headBlocked = BlockUtil.collides(world, minX, maxY, minZ, maxX, maxY + 0.1, maxZ);
        data.horizontalCollision = BlockUtil.collides(world, minX - 0.04, minY + 0.01, minZ - 0.04, maxX + 0.04, maxY - 0.01, maxZ + 0.04);

        boolean inLiquid = false;
        boolean nearLiquid = false;
        boolean liquidBelow = false;
        double liquidDepth = 0.0;
        boolean nearClimbable = false;
        boolean inWeb = false;
        boolean nearPowderSnow = false;
        boolean nearHoney = false;
        boolean nearSlime = false;
        boolean nearBed = false;
        boolean nearPiston = false;
        boolean nearScaffolding = false;

        int x0 = (int) Math.floor(minX - 0.3);
        int x1 = (int) Math.floor(maxX + 0.3);
        int y0 = (int) Math.floor(minY - 0.6);
        int y1 = (int) Math.floor(maxY + 0.2);
        int z0 = (int) Math.floor(minZ - 0.3);
        int z1 = (int) Math.floor(maxZ + 0.3);
        for (int x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
            boolean xIn = x + 1 > minX && x < maxX;
            for (int z = z0; z <= z1; z++) {
                boolean xzIn = xIn && z + 1 > minZ && z < maxZ;
                for (int y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, y, z);
                    Material type = block.getType();
                    if (type.isAir()) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    boolean inBox = xzIn && y + 1 > minY && y < maxY;
                    double fluid = BlockUtil.fluidHeight(block);
                    if (fluid > 0.0) {
                        double surface = y + fluid;
                        nearLiquid = true;
                        if (xzIn && y < maxY && surface > minY + 0.001) {
                            inLiquid = true;
                            liquidDepth = Math.max(liquidDepth, surface - minY);
                        }
                        if (xzIn && surface <= minY + 0.05 && surface >= minY - 0.55) {
                            liquidBelow = true;
                        }
                    }
                    if (BlockUtil.isClimbable(type)) {
                        nearClimbable = true;
                        if (type == Material.SCAFFOLDING) {
                            nearScaffolding = true;
                        }
                    }
                    switch (type) {
                        case COBWEB, SWEET_BERRY_BUSH -> inWeb |= inBox;
                        case POWDER_SNOW -> nearPowderSnow = true;
                        case HONEY_BLOCK -> nearHoney = true;
                        case SLIME_BLOCK -> nearSlime = true;
                        case MOVING_PISTON, PISTON_HEAD -> nearPiston = true;
                        default -> {
                            if (Tag.BEDS.isTagged(type)) {
                                nearBed = true;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // friction of the block under the feet (max of the four corners - lenient)
        int frictionY = (int) Math.floor(minY - 0.5000001);
        double friction = -1.0;
        double[][] corners = {{minX, minZ}, {minX, maxZ}, {maxX, minZ}, {maxX, maxZ}};
        for (double[] corner : corners) {
            Material type = world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(corner[0]), frictionY, (int) Math.floor(corner[1])).getType();
            if (!type.isAir()) {
                friction = Math.max(friction, BlockUtil.friction(type));
            }
        }
        data.friction = friction < 0.0 ? 0.6 : friction;

        data.inLiquid = inLiquid;
        data.nearLiquid = nearLiquid;
        data.liquidBelow = liquidBelow && !inLiquid;
        data.liquidDepth = liquidDepth;
        data.nearClimbable = nearClimbable;
        data.inWeb = inWeb;
        data.nearPowderSnow = nearPowderSnow;
        data.nearHoney = nearHoney;
        data.nearSlime = nearSlime;
        data.nearBed = nearBed;
        data.nearPiston = nearPiston;
        data.nearScaffolding = nearScaffolding;
    }

    private void finish(Player player, PlayerData data) {
        int clientTicks = data.clientTicks;
        data.velocities.removeIf(record -> clientTicks - record.clientTick > record.window + 2);
        data.rollClickWindow();
        data.attacksThisTick = 0;
        data.swingsThisTick = 0;
        data.clicksThisTick = 0;
        data.targetsThisTick.clear();
        data.placedThisTick = 0;
        data.brokenThisTick = 0;
        data.inventoryClicksThisTick = 0;
        data.slotChangesThisTick = 0;
        data.actionsThisTick = 0;
        data.movesThisTick = 0;
        data.attackedBeforeSlotChange = false;

        Location location = player.getLocation();
        data.tickEndX = location.getX();
        data.tickEndY = location.getY();
        data.tickEndZ = location.getZ();
        data.tickEndYaw = location.getYaw();
        data.tickEndPitch = location.getPitch();
        data.hasTickSnapshot = true;

        // right after a teleport / setback the target stays the legit position, so the few unchecked
        // ticks after it can not be used to move on step by step
        if (!data.skipMovement && !data.movementFlagged && !data.movementSuspicious && data.chunkLoaded && !data.inVehicle
                && PlayerData.since(data.lastSetback) >= 5 && PlayerData.since(data.lastTeleport) >= 5) {
            data.hasLegit = true;
            data.legitX = location.getX();
            data.legitY = location.getY();
            data.legitZ = location.getZ();
            data.legitWorld = location.getWorld().getUID();
            if (data.onGround && data.clientOnGround && !data.inLiquid && !data.gliding) {
                data.lastSafe = location;
            }
        }
        data.movementFlagged = false;
        data.movementSuspicious = false;
    }

    private static double attribute(Player player, Attribute attribute, double def) {
        AttributeInstance instance = player.getAttribute(attribute);
        return instance == null ? def : instance.getValue();
    }

    public static boolean isSpear(ItemStack item) {
        return item != null && item.getType().name().endsWith("_SPEAR");
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/manager/AlertDigest.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Collects the flags of one player and decides when staff get one compact alert line for them,
 * so a flying cheater produces a few lines instead of hundreds.
 * <ul>
 *   <li>The first flags are gathered for {@code gatherMs}, then sent as one line.</li>
 *   <li>A cheat that was not reported yet is sent at most once per {@code intervalMs}.</li>
 *   <li>The same cheats again are only reminded every {@code repeatMs} (with the new count).</li>
 *   <li>After {@code quietMs} without flags everything counts as new again.</li>
 * </ul>
 * Pure logic (no Bukkit), so it is unit tested directly.
 */
public final class AlertDigest {

    /** One likely cheat in the next alert line. */
    public static final class Entry {
        public final String name;
        /** "Check(Type)" -> flags, in the order they were first seen. */
        public final Map<String, Integer> types = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        public int count;
        public double vl;
        public String lastDebug = "";

        Entry(String name) {
            this.name = name;
        }
    }

    private final Map<String, Entry> pending = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final Set<String> reported = new HashSet<>();
    private long lastSent = Long.MIN_VALUE / 4;
    private long firstPending;
    private long lastFlag = Long.MIN_VALUE / 4;

    public void add(String name, String checkType, double vl, String debug, long now, long quietMs) {
        if (now - lastFlag > quietMs) {
            reported.clear();
        }
        lastFlag = now;
        if (pending.isEmpty()) {
            firstPending = now;
        }
        Entry entry = pending.computeIfAbsent(name, Entry::new);
        entry.count++;
        entry.types.merge(checkType, 1, Integer::sum);
        entry.vl = Math.max(entry.vl, vl);
        entry.lastDebug = debug;
    }

    /** True when an alert line should be sent now. */
    public boolean due(long now, long gatherMs, long intervalMs, long repeatMs) {
        if (pending.isEmpty() || now - firstPending < gatherMs) {
            return false;
        }
        boolean fresh = false;
        for (String name : pending.keySet()) {
            if (!reported.contains(name)) {
                fresh = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        return now - lastSent >= (fresh ? intervalMs : repeatMs);
    }

    /** Takes everything gathered for the alert line that is being sent. Newly reported cheats first. */
    public List<Entry> drain(long now) {
        List<Entry> fresh = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Entry> known = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Entry entry : pending.values()) {
            (reported.contains(entry.name) ? known : fresh).add(entry);
        }
        fresh.addAll(known);
        for (Entry entry : fresh) {
            reported.add(entry.name);
        }
        pending.clear();
        lastSent = now;
        return fresh;
    }

    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return pending.isEmpty();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/manager/AlertManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config.Settings;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.ClientUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.ClickEvent;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.event.HoverEvent;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Sends flag alerts to staff (chat with hover + click) and to the console.
 * <p>
 * Compact mode (default): flags are grouped per player into one short line that says what the
 * player is most likely using ("Steve may be using KillAura (silent rotations) x6, Silent swap x2"),
 * with every detail in the hover. See {@link AlertDigest} for when lines are sent.
 * Staff with /astral verbose (or /astral debug) still see every single flag.
 */
public final class AlertManager {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private final CheatNames names = new CheatNames();

    public AlertManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    /** (Re)loads the cheat names from messages.yml. */
    public void load() {
        names.load(plugin.messages().section("cheat-names"));
    }

    public CheatNames names() {
        return names;
    }

    public void alert(Check check, PlayerData data, String type, double vl, String debug) {
        Player player = data.player();
        String[] placeholders = flagPlaceholders(check, data, type, vl, debug);
        sendVerbose(check, placeholders, player);

        if (vl < check.alertVl()) {
            return;
        }
        Settings settings = plugin.settings();
        if (!settings.alertCompact) {
            legacyAlert(check, data, placeholders, player, debug);
            return;
        }
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        String name = names.name(check.id(), check.name(), type);
        data.alertDigest.add(name, check.name() + " " + type, vl, debug, now, settings.alertQuietMs);
        flush(data, now);
    }

    /** Sends the alert lines that became due (runs every 10 ticks). */
    public void tick() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        for (PlayerData data : plugin.data().all()) {
            if (!data.alertDigest.isEmpty()) {
                flush(data, now);
            }
        }
    }

    private void flush(PlayerData data, long now) {
        Settings settings = plugin.settings();
        AlertDigest digest = data.alertDigest;
        if (!digest.due(now, settings.alertGatherMs, settings.alertIntervalMs, settings.alertRepeatMs)) {
            return;
        }
        List<AlertDigest.Entry> entries = digest.drain(now);
        if (entries.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Player player = data.player();

        StringBuilder cheats = new StringBuilder();
        StringBuilder plainCheats = new StringBuilder();
        String separator = plugin.messages().raw("compact-alert-separator");
        int flags = 0;
        for (AlertDigest.Entry entry : entries) {
            if (!cheats.isEmpty()) {
                cheats.append(separator);
                plainCheats.append(", ");
            }
            cheats.append(plugin.messages().raw("compact-alert-cheat",
                    "cheat", entry.name, "count", String.valueOf(entry.count)));
            plainCheats.append(entry.name).append(" x").append(entry.count);
            flags += entry.count;
        }
        String[] placeholders = {
                "player", player.getName(),
                "cheats", cheats.toString(),
                "flags", String.valueOf(flags),
                "vl", MathUtil.format(data.totalViolations(), 1),
                "ping", String.valueOf(data.ping()),
                "tps", MathUtil.format(plugin.violations().currentTps(), 1),
                "client", ClientUtil.describe(player),
                "mode", plugin.messages().raw(settings.mitigation ? "mode-blocking" : "mode-watching")
        };

        List<String> hover = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String line : plugin.messages().rawList("compact-alert-hover", placeholders)) {
            if (!line.contains("%details%")) {
                hover.add(line);
                continue;
            }
            for (AlertDigest.Entry entry : entries) {
                hover.add(plugin.messages().raw("compact-alert-detail",
                        "cheat", entry.name, "count", String.valueOf(entry.count),
                        "checks", types(entry.types), "vl", MathUtil.format(entry.vl, 1)));
                if (entry.lastDebug != null && !entry.lastDebug.isEmpty()) {
                    hover.add(plugin.messages().raw("compact-alert-detail-info", "debug", entry.lastDebug));
                }
            }
        }

        Component line = plugin.messages().get("compact-alert", placeholders)
                .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(Text.lines(hover)))
                .clickEvent(ClickEvent.runCommand(clickCommand(player)));
        for (PlayerData staffData : plugin.data().all()) {
            Player staff = staffData.player();
            if (staffData.alerts && !staffData.verbose && staff.hasPermission("astral.alerts")) {
                staff.sendMessage(line);
            }
        }
        if (settings.alertConsole) {
            StringBuilder details = new StringBuilder();
            for (AlertDigest.Entry entry : entries) {
                if (!details.isEmpty()) {
                    details.append("; ");
                }
                details.append(types(entry.types));
                if (entry.lastDebug != null && !entry.lastDebug.isEmpty()) {
                    details.append(" - ").append(entry.lastDebug);
                }
            }
            Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color(plugin.messages().raw("compact-alert", placeholders)
                    + " &8(" + details + ")"));
        }
        plugin.logs().log("ALERT " + player.getName() + " may be using " + plainCheats
                + " vl=" + MathUtil.format(data.totalViolations(), 1));
    }

    private static String types(Map<String, Integer> types) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> type : types.entrySet()) {
            if (!sb.isEmpty()) {
                sb.append(", ");
            }
            sb.append(type.getKey()).append(" x").append(type.getValue());
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ every flag (verbose / debug)

    private String[] flagPlaceholders(Check check, PlayerData data, String type, double vl, String debug) {
        Player player = data.player();
        return new String[]{
                "player", player.getName(),
                "check", check.name(),
                "type", type,
                "cheat", names.name(check.id(), check.name(), type),
                "vl", MathUtil.format(vl, 1),
                "ping", String.valueOf(data.ping()),
                "tps", MathUtil.format(plugin.violations().currentTps(), 1),
                "debug", debug,
                "category", check.category().display(),
                "description", check.description(),
                "client", ClientUtil.describe(player)
        };
    }

    private void sendVerbose(Check check, String[] placeholders, Player player) {
        Component verbose = null;
        for (PlayerData staffData : plugin.data().all()) {
            boolean wantsVerbose = staffData.verbose || staffData.debugChecks.contains(check.id());
            if (!wantsVerbose) {
                continue;
            }
            Player staff = staffData.player();
            if (!staff.hasPermission("astral.verbose")) {
                continue;
            }
            if (verbose == null) {
                verbose = plugin.messages().get("verbose", placeholders)
                        .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(plugin.messages().lines("alert-hover", placeholders)))
                        .clickEvent(ClickEvent.runCommand(clickCommand(player)));
            }
            staff.sendMessage(verbose);
        }
    }

    /** alerts.compact: false - one line per flag (with a cooldown per check). */
    private void legacyAlert(Check check, PlayerData data, String[] placeholders, Player player, String debug) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        Long last = data.lastAlert.get(check.id());
        if (last != null && now - last < plugin.settings().alertCooldownMs) {
            return;
        }
        data.lastAlert.put(check.id(), now);
        Component alert = null;
        for (PlayerData staffData : plugin.data().all()) {
            Player staff = staffData.player();
            if (staffData.alerts && !staffData.verbose && staff.hasPermission("astral.alerts")) {
                if (alert == null) {
                    alert = plugin.messages().get("alert", placeholders)
                            .hoverEvent(HoverEvent.showText(plugin.messages().lines("alert-hover", placeholders)))
                            .clickEvent(ClickEvent.runCommand(clickCommand(player)));
                }
                staff.sendMessage(alert);
            }
        }
        if (plugin.settings().alertConsole) {
            Bukkit.getConsoleSender().sendMessage(Text.color(
                    plugin.messages().raw("alert", placeholders) + " &8(" + debug + ")"));
        }
    }

    private String clickCommand(Player player) {
        return plugin.settings().alertClickCommand.replace("%player%", player.getName());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/manager/BypassManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/**
 * Players added with /astral bypass. They are treated exactly like players with the
 * astral.bypass permission. Saved in bypass.yml so it survives restarts.
 */
public final class BypassManager {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private final File file;
    private final Set<UUID> bypassing = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public BypassManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.file = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "bypass.yml");
    }

    public void load() {
        bypassing.clear();
        if (!file.exists()) {
            return;
        }
        YamlConfiguration config = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(file);
        for (String raw : config.getStringList("players")) {
            try {
                bypassing.add(UUID.fromString(raw));
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                // invalid entry
            }
        }
    }

    public void save() {
        YamlConfiguration config = new YamlConfiguration();
        List<String> list = new ArrayList<>();
        for (UUID uuid : bypassing) {
            list.add(uuid.toString());
        }
        config.set("players", list);
        try {
            if (!plugin.getDataFolder().exists()) {
                plugin.getDataFolder().mkdirs();
            }
            config.save(file);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.WARNING, "Could not save bypass.yml", e);
        }
    }

    public boolean contains(UUID uuid) {
        return bypassing.contains(uuid);
    }

    /** Toggles the bypass. Returns the new state. */
    public boolean toggle(UUID uuid) {
        boolean now;
        if (bypassing.remove(uuid)) {
            now = false;
        } else {
            bypassing.add(uuid);
            now = true;
        }
        save();
        return now;
    }

    public Set<UUID> all() {
        return bypassing;
    }
}
```

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager;

import org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Turns a check + type into what the player is most likely using ("KillAura (silent rotations)",
 * "Auto attribute swap" ...). Shown in the compact staff alerts. Every name can be changed in
 * messages.yml under "cheat-names".
 */
public final class CheatNames {

    private static final Map<String, String> DEFAULTS = new HashMap<>();

    static {
        put("reach", "Reach");
        put("hitbox", "KillAura / Hitbox");
        put("killaura", "KillAura");
        put("killaura.post", "KillAura (silent rotations)");
        put("killaura.multi", "Multi aura");
        put("killaura.switch", "Switch aura");
        put("killaura.noswing", "KillAura (no swing)");
        put("killaura.walls", "KillAura (through walls)");
        put("aim", "Aimbot");
        put("aim.silentaim", "Silent aim");
        put("aim.gcd", "Aimbot / aim assist");
        put("aim.robotic", "Aimbot (robotic rotations)");
        put("crystalaura", "Crystal aura");
        put("autoblock", "AutoBlock");
        put("criticals", "Criticals");
        put("velocity", "Anti knockback");
        put("mace", "Mace exploit");
        put("mace.fakefall", "Mace kill (fake fall)");

        put("flight", "Fly");
        put("flight.hover", "Fly (hover)");
        put("flight.glide", "Glide / slow fall");
        put("flight.spider", "Spider");
        put("flight.highjump", "High jump");
        put("flight.minijump", "Criticals / low hop");
        put("flight.fastfall", "Fast fall");
        put("speed", "Speed");
        put("speed.strafe", "Air strafe");
        put("nofall", "NoFall");
        put("timer", "Timer");
        put("step", "Step");
        put("jesus", "Jesus");
        put("fastclimb", "Fast ladder");
        put("elytrafly", "Elytra fly");
        put("noslow", "NoSlow");
        put("sprint", "Omni sprint");
        put("sprint.hunger", "Sprint while starving");
        put("phase", "Phase / NoClip");
        put("boatfly", "Boat fly");

        put("inventorymove", "Inventory move");
        put("inventorymove.action", "Inventory macro (auto armor / auto totem)");
        put("inventorymove.post", "Inventory macro (auto armor / auto totem)");
        put("inventorymove.combo", "Inventory macro (auto armor / auto totem)");
        put("badpackets", "Bad packets");
        put("badpackets.postuse", "Auto pot / silent rotations");
        put("badpackets.postdig", "Silent rotations");
        put("badpackets.postinteract", "Silent rotations");
        put("badpackets.multi", "Blink / fake rotations");
        put("fastbow", "Fast bow");
        put("clientbrand", "Hacked client");

        put("fastbreak", "Fast break");
        put("nuker", "Nuker");
        put("blockreach", "Block reach");
        put("interact", "Ghost hand");
        put("fastplace", "Fast place");
        put("scaffold", "Scaffold");
        put("xray", "X-Ray (suspected)");

        put("autoclicker", "Auto clicker");
        put("autototem", "Auto totem");
        put("inventorymacro", "Chest stealer");
        put("hotbarmacro", "Silent swap");
        put("hotbarmacro.spam", "Hotbar macro");
        put("hotbarmacro.attributeswap", "Auto attribute swap");
        put("hotbarmacro.stunslam", "Auto stun slam");
        put("autofish", "Auto fish");
        put("chat", "Chat spam / macro");
    }

    private static void put(String key, String name) {
        DEFAULTS.put(key, name);
    }

    private final Map<String, String> overrides = new HashMap<>();

    public void load(ConfigurationSection section) {
        overrides.clear();
        if (section == null) {
            return;
        }
        // reach: "Reach"   or   killaura: {default: "KillAura", post: "KillAura (silent rotations)"}
        for (String check : section.getKeys(false)) {
            String id = check.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            ConfigurationSection types = section.getConfigurationSection(check);
            if (types == null) {
                String value = section.getString(check);
                if (value != null) {
                    overrides.put(id, value);
                }
                continue;
            }
            for (String type : types.getKeys(false)) {
                String value = types.getString(type);
                if (value == null) {
                    continue;
                }
                String key = type.equalsIgnoreCase("default") ? id : id + "." + type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
                overrides.put(key, value);
            }
        }
    }

    /** What the player is most likely using, e.g. name("killaura", "KillAura", "Post"). */
    public String name(String checkId, String checkName, String type) {
        String id = checkId.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String full = id + "." + type.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String name = overrides.get(full);
        if (name == null) {
            name = DEFAULTS.get(full);
        }
        if (name == null) {
            name = overrides.get(id);
        }
        if (name == null) {
            name = DEFAULTS.get(id);
        }
        return name == null ? checkName : name;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/manager/FreezeManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import net.kyori.adventure.title.Title;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerDropItemEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/** /astral freeze - lock a player in place for a screen share / check. */
public final class FreezeManager implements Listener {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private final Set<UUID> frozen = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    public FreezeManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public boolean isFrozen(Player player) {
        return frozen.contains(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    /** Toggles freeze, returns the new state. */
    public boolean toggle(Player player) {
        if (frozen.remove(player.getUniqueId())) {
            player.clearTitle();
            return false;
        }
        frozen.add(player.getUniqueId());
        remind(player);
        return true;
    }

    public void tick() {
        for (UUID uuid : frozen) {
            Player player = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);
            if (player != null) {
                remind(player);
            }
        }
    }

    private void remind(Player player) {
        player.sendMessage(plugin.messages().lines("freeze-message"));
        player.showTitle(Title.title(
                plugin.messages().get("prefix"),
                me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text.color("&cYou are frozen - do not log out"),
                Title.Times.times(Duration.ZERO, Duration.ofSeconds(3), Duration.ofMillis(500))));
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!isFrozen(event.getPlayer())) {
            return;
        }
        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();
        if (from.getX() != to.getX() || from.getY() != to.getY() || from.getZ() != to.getZ()) {
            Location back = from.clone();
            back.setYaw(to.getYaw());
            back.setPitch(to.getPitch());
            event.setTo(back);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        if (isFrozen(event.getPlayer())) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        if (isFrozen(event.getPlayer())) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (isFrozen(event.getPlayer())) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDrop(PlayerDropItemEvent event) {
        if (isFrozen(event.getPlayer())) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onInventory(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player && isFrozen(player)) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player player && isFrozen(player)) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
        if (event instanceof EntityDamageByEntityEvent byEntity && byEntity.getDamager() instanceof Player damager && isFrozen(damager)) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onCommand(PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent event) {
        if (!isFrozen(event.getPlayer())) {
            return;
        }
        String label = event.getMessage().split(" ")[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        for (String allowed : plugin.settings().freezeAllowedCommands) {
            if (label.equalsIgnoreCase(allowed)) {
                return;
            }
        }
        event.setCancelled(true);
        plugin.messages().send(event.getPlayer(), "freeze-blocked-command");
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (!frozen.remove(player.getUniqueId())) {
            return;
        }
        for (Player staff : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if (staff.hasPermission("astral.alerts")) {
                plugin.messages().send(staff, "freeze-quit", "player", player.getName());
            }
        }
        plugin.logs().log("FREEZE-QUIT " + player.getName() + " logged out while frozen");
        for (String command : plugin.settings().freezeQuitCommands) {
            String parsed = command.replace("%player%", player.getName());
            if (parsed.startsWith("/")) {
                parsed = parsed.substring(1);
            }
            String finalCommand = parsed;
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), finalCommand));
        }
    }
}
```
