`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/manager/LogManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.logging.Level;

/** Writes every flag and punishment to plugins/Astral/logs/&lt;date&gt;.log (async). */
public final class LogManager {

    private static final DateTimeFormatter TIME = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm:ss");

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private final File folder;
    private final ExecutorService executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(runnable -> {
        Thread thread = new Thread(runnable, "Astral-Logger");
        thread.setDaemon(true);
        return thread;
    });

    public LogManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.folder = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "logs");
    }

    public void log(String line) {
        if (!plugin.settings().logFile) {
            return;
        }
        String entry = "[" + LocalTime.now().format(TIME) + "] " + line + System.lineSeparator();
        LocalDate date = LocalDate.now();
        executor.execute(() -> write(date, entry));
    }

    private void write(LocalDate date, String entry) {
        try {
            if (!folder.exists() && !folder.mkdirs()) {
                return;
            }
            File file = new File(folder, date + ".log");
            try (BufferedWriter writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(file.toPath(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8,
                    StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND)) {
                writer.write(entry);
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            plugin.getLogger().log(Level.WARNING, "Could not write the violation log", e);
        }
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        executor.shutdown();
        try {
            executor.awaitTermination(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/manager/ViolationManager.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.FlagRecord;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Text;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Handles everything that happens after a check detected something: violation levels,
 * alerts, logging, mitigation (setbacks) and punishments.
 */
public final class ViolationManager {

    private final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    private long totalFlags;
    private long totalPunishments;

    public ViolationManager(AstralAntiCheat plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    /**
     * Adds violations for a check. Returns true when the check should block the cheat.
     */
    public boolean flag(Check check, PlayerData data, String type, double amount, String debug) {
        if (data == null || data.isBypassing(check.id())) {
            return false;
        }
        if (!Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()) {
            // async events (chat) - process the flag on the main thread
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> flag(check, data, type, amount, debug));
            return check.mitigate() && data.violation(check.id()) + amount >= check.mitigateVl();
        }
        if (isLagging()) {
            return false;
        }
        double vl = data.addViolation(check.id(), amount);
        if (check.category() == me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckCategory.MOVEMENT) {
            data.movementFlagged = true;
        }
        data.totalFlags++;
        check.incrementFlags();
        totalFlags++;

        data.flags.addFirst(new FlagRecord(System.currentTimeMillis(), check.name(), type, vl, debug));
        while (data.flags.size() > plugin.settings().logMemorySize) {
            data.flags.removeLast();
        }

        plugin.logs().log(data.name() + " failed " + check.name() + " (" + type + ") vl=" + MathUtil.format(vl, 2)
                + " ping=" + data.ping() + " | " + debug);
        plugin.alerts().alert(check, data, type, vl, debug);

        if (plugin.settings().punishEnabled && check.punish() && vl >= check.punishVl()
                && !data.punished.contains(check.id())) {
            data.punished.add(check.id());
            punish(check, data, type, vl);
        }
        return check.mitigate() && vl >= check.mitigateVl();
    }

    private void punish(Check check, PlayerData data, String type, double vl) {
        totalPunishments++;
        Player player = data.player();
        String[] placeholders = {
                "player", player.getName(),
                "uuid", player.getUniqueId().toString(),
                "check", check.name(),
                "type", type,
                "vl", MathUtil.format(vl, 1),
                "ping", String.valueOf(data.ping())
        };
        plugin.logs().log("PUNISH " + player.getName() + " for " + check.name() + " (" + type + ") vl=" + MathUtil.format(vl, 2));

        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> {
            if (plugin.settings().punishBroadcast) {
                Bukkit.getServer().sendMessage(plugin.messages().lines("punish-broadcast", placeholders));
            }
            for (Player staff : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                if (staff.hasPermission("astral.notify.punish")) {
                    plugin.messages().send(staff, "punish-staff", placeholders);
                }
            }
            for (String command : check.punishCommands()) {
                String parsed = command;
                for (int i = 0; i + 1 < placeholders.length; i += 2) {
                    parsed = parsed.replace("%" + placeholders[i] + "%", placeholders[i + 1]);
                }
                if (parsed.startsWith("/")) {
                    parsed = parsed.substring(1);
                }
                Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), parsed);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Movement mitigation: teleports the player back to the last position on the ground that
     * passed every check. When that is far away (falling from a battle bus, long glides, a
     * teleport into the air ...) only the illegal move is undone: back to the last checked
     * position. A setback never moves a player more than settings.max-setback-distance.
     */
    public void setback(PlayerData data) {
        if (!plugin.settings().mitigation) {
            return;
        }
        if (PlayerData.since(data.lastSetback) < 1) {
            return;
        }
        Player player = data.player();
        if (player.isInsideVehicle() || PlayerData.since(data.lastVehicle) < 2) {
            return; // a teleport would pull the player off the vehicle (battle bus)
        }
        Location current = player.getLocation();
        double max = plugin.settings().maxSetbackDistance;
        Location target = null;
        Location safe = data.lastSafe;
        if (safe != null && safe.getWorld() != null && safe.getWorld().equals(current.getWorld())
                && safe.distanceSquared(current) <= max * max) {
            target = safe.clone();
            player.setFallDistance(0.0F);
        } else if (data.hasLegit && current.getWorld().getUID().equals(data.legitWorld)) {
            Location legit = new Location(current.getWorld(), data.legitX, data.legitY, data.legitZ);
            if (legit.distanceSquared(current) <= max * max) {
                target = legit;
            }
        }
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }
        data.lastSetback = PlayerData.now();
        target.setYaw(current.getYaw());
        target.setPitch(current.getPitch());
        player.teleport(target, PlayerTeleportEvent.TeleportCause.PLUGIN);
    }

    /** Lowers every violation level a bit. Runs every "violations.decay-interval-seconds". */
    public void decay() {
        for (PlayerData data : plugin.data().all()) {
            List<String> ids = new ArrayList<>(data.violations().keySet());
            for (String id : ids) {
                Check check = plugin.checks().get(id);
                double decay = check == null ? 1.0 : check.decay();
                if (decay <= 0.0) {
                    continue;
                }
                double value = data.violation(id) - decay;
                data.setViolation(id, value);
            }
        }
    }

    public boolean isLagging() {
        if (!plugin.settings().lagProtection) {
            return false;
        }
        return currentTps() < plugin.settings().minTps;
    }

    /** TPS of the last ~5 seconds. */
    public double currentTps() {
        double mspt = Bukkit.getAverageTickTime();
        if (mspt <= 0.0) {
            return 20.0;
        }
        return Math.min(20.0, 1000.0 / mspt);
    }

    public long totalFlags() {
        return totalFlags;
    }

    public long totalPunishments() {
        return totalPunishments;
    }

    public String topChecks(PlayerData data, int limit) {
        List<Map.Entry<String, Double>> entries = new ArrayList<>(data.violations().entrySet());
        entries.sort((a, b) -> Double.compare(b.getValue(), a.getValue()));
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(limit, entries.size()); i++) {
            Check check = plugin.checks().get(entries.get(i).getKey());
            if (!sb.isEmpty()) {
                sb.append("&7, ");
            }
            sb.append("&f").append(check == null ? entries.get(i).getKey() : check.name())
                    .append(" &c").append(MathUtil.format(entries.get(i).getValue(), 1));
        }
        return sb.isEmpty() ? "&anone" : sb.toString();
    }

    public String stripTop(PlayerData data, int limit) {
        return Text.strip(topChecks(data, limit));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/util/BlockUtil.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util;

import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.Tag;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.data.BlockData;
import org.bukkit.block.data.Levelled;
import org.bukkit.block.data.Waterlogged;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.EnumSet;
import java.util.Set;

public final class BlockUtil {

    private static final Set<Material> WATERLOGGABLE = EnumSet.noneOf(Material.class);
    private static final Set<Material> ALWAYS_WATER = EnumSet.of(
            Material.KELP, Material.KELP_PLANT, Material.SEAGRASS, Material.TALL_SEAGRASS, Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN);

    static {
        for (Material material : Material.values()) {
            if (material.isLegacy() || !material.isBlock()) {
                continue;
            }
            try {
                if (material.createBlockData() instanceof Waterlogged) {
                    WATERLOGGABLE.add(material);
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                // some technical blocks cannot create block data
            }
        }
    }

    private BlockUtil() {
    }

    /** Vanilla block friction ("slipperiness"). */
    public static double friction(Material material) {
        return switch (material) {
            case ICE, PACKED_ICE, FROSTED_ICE -> 0.98;
            case BLUE_ICE -> 0.989;
            case SLIME_BLOCK -> 0.8;
            default -> 0.6;
        };
    }

    public static boolean isClimbable(Material material) {
        return Tag.CLIMBABLE.isTagged(material);
    }

    /**
     * Height of the fluid inside the block (0 - 1), or -1 if the block has no fluid.
     */
    public static double fluidHeight(Block block) {
        Material type = block.getType();
        boolean fluid;
        double height = 8.0 / 9.0;
        if (type == Material.WATER || type == Material.LAVA) {
            fluid = true;
            BlockData data = block.getBlockData();
            if (data instanceof Levelled levelled) {
                int level = levelled.getLevel();
                if (level > 0 && level < 8) {
                    height = (8 - level) / 9.0;
                }
            }
        } else if (ALWAYS_WATER.contains(type)) {
            fluid = true;
            if (type == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN) {
                height = 1.0;
            }
        } else if (WATERLOGGABLE.contains(type)) {
            fluid = block.getBlockData() instanceof Waterlogged w && w.isWaterlogged();
        } else {
            fluid = false;
        }
        if (!fluid) {
            return -1.0;
        }
        Block above = block.getRelative(0, 1, 0);
        Material aboveType = above.getType();
        if (aboveType == Material.WATER || aboveType == Material.LAVA || ALWAYS_WATER.contains(aboveType)) {
            return 1.0;
        }
        if (WATERLOGGABLE.contains(aboveType) && above.getBlockData() instanceof Waterlogged w && w.isWaterlogged()) {
            return 1.0;
        }
        return height;
    }

    public static boolean isLiquid(Block block) {
        return fluidHeight(block) > 0.0;
    }

    /** True when every chunk touched by the box (plus a margin) is loaded. */
    public static boolean isLoaded(World world, BoundingBox box) {
        int minCX = ((int) Math.floor(box.getMinX() - 2)) >> 4;
        int maxCX = ((int) Math.floor(box.getMaxX() + 2)) >> 4;
        int minCZ = ((int) Math.floor(box.getMinZ() - 2)) >> 4;
        int maxCZ = ((int) Math.floor(box.getMaxZ() + 2)) >> 4;
        for (int cx = minCX; cx <= maxCX; cx++) {
            for (int cz = minCZ; cz <= maxCZ; cz++) {
                if (!world.isChunkLoaded(cx, cz)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    public static boolean collides(World world, double minX, double minY, double minZ, double maxX, double maxY, double maxZ) {
        return world.hasCollisionsIn(new BoundingBox(minX, minY, minZ, maxX, maxY, maxZ));
    }

    /** Center of a block. */
    public static Vector center(Block block) {
        return new Vector(block.getX() + 0.5, block.getY() + 0.5, block.getZ() + 0.5);
    }

    /** Box of a block (falls back to a full cube for blocks without a shape). */
    public static BoundingBox box(Block block) {
        BoundingBox box = block.getBoundingBox();
        if (box.getVolume() <= 0.0) {
            return BoundingBox.of(block);
        }
        return box;
    }

    public static boolean isFullCube(Block block) {
        BoundingBox box = block.getBoundingBox();
        return box.getWidthX() >= 0.999 && box.getHeight() >= 0.999 && box.getWidthZ() >= 0.999;
    }

    public static boolean isOre(Material material) {
        return switch (material) {
            case DIAMOND_ORE, DEEPSLATE_DIAMOND_ORE, ANCIENT_DEBRIS, EMERALD_ORE, DEEPSLATE_EMERALD_ORE -> true;
            default -> false;
        };
    }

    public static boolean isStoneLike(Material material) {
        return switch (material) {
            case STONE, DEEPSLATE, TUFF, NETHERRACK, BASALT, BLACKSTONE, ANDESITE, DIORITE, GRANITE,
                 COBBLED_DEEPSLATE, CALCITE, SMOOTH_BASALT -> true;
            default -> false;
        };
    }

    /**
     * Boats, shulkers, minecarts and happy ghasts have collision boxes players can stand on,
     * which makes their movement look strange.
     */
    public static boolean nearCollidable(Player player) {
        for (Entity entity : player.getNearbyEntities(1.5, 2.5, 1.5)) {
            if (isPlatform(entity)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Entities players can stand on or get pushed by like a block: boats / rafts, minecarts,
     * shulkers and happy ghasts. Minigames often build moving platforms (battle bus, airships,
     * elevators) out of invisible shulkers or boats.
     */
    public static boolean isPlatform(Entity entity) {
        String type = entity.getType().name();
        return type.contains("BOAT") || type.contains("RAFT") || type.contains("MINECART")
                || type.equals("SHULKER") || type.equals("HAPPY_GHAST");
    }

    /** True when a platform entity touches the player's box (below, around or above). */
    public static boolean touchesPlatform(Player player, BoundingBox box) {
        BoundingBox area = new BoundingBox(box.getMinX() - 0.6, box.getMinY() - 1.2, box.getMinZ() - 0.6,
                box.getMaxX() + 0.6, box.getMaxY() + 0.6, box.getMaxZ() + 0.6);
        return !player.getWorld().getNearbyEntities(area, entity -> entity != player && isPlatform(entity)).isEmpty();
    }
}
```

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.TreeMap;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Detects the real Minecraft version / brand / platform of a client.
 * ViaVersion and Floodgate are used through reflection when they are installed.
 */
public final class ClientUtil {

    private static final Map<Integer, String> VERSIONS = new TreeMap<>();

    static {
        VERSIONS.put(774, "1.21.11");
        VERSIONS.put(773, "1.21.9 - 1.21.10");
        VERSIONS.put(772, "1.21.7 - 1.21.8");
        VERSIONS.put(771, "1.21.6");
        VERSIONS.put(770, "1.21.5");
        VERSIONS.put(769, "1.21.4");
        VERSIONS.put(768, "1.21.2 - 1.21.3");
        VERSIONS.put(767, "1.21 - 1.21.1");
        VERSIONS.put(766, "1.20.5 - 1.20.6");
        VERSIONS.put(765, "1.20.3 - 1.20.4");
        VERSIONS.put(764, "1.20.2");
        VERSIONS.put(763, "1.20 - 1.20.1");
        VERSIONS.put(762, "1.19.4");
        VERSIONS.put(761, "1.19.3");
        VERSIONS.put(760, "1.19.1 - 1.19.2");
        VERSIONS.put(759, "1.19");
        VERSIONS.put(758, "1.18.2");
        VERSIONS.put(757, "1.18 - 1.18.1");
        VERSIONS.put(756, "1.17.1");
        VERSIONS.put(755, "1.17");
        VERSIONS.put(754, "1.16.4 - 1.16.5");
        VERSIONS.put(753, "1.16.3");
        VERSIONS.put(751, "1.16.2");
        VERSIONS.put(736, "1.16.1");
        VERSIONS.put(735, "1.16");
        VERSIONS.put(578, "1.15.2");
        VERSIONS.put(575, "1.15.1");
        VERSIONS.put(573, "1.15");
        VERSIONS.put(498, "1.14.4");
        VERSIONS.put(490, "1.14.3");
        VERSIONS.put(485, "1.14.2");
        VERSIONS.put(480, "1.14.1");
        VERSIONS.put(477, "1.14");
        VERSIONS.put(404, "1.13.2");
        VERSIONS.put(401, "1.13.1");
        VERSIONS.put(393, "1.13");
        VERSIONS.put(340, "1.12.2");
        VERSIONS.put(338, "1.12.1");
        VERSIONS.put(335, "1.12");
        VERSIONS.put(316, "1.11.1 - 1.11.2");
        VERSIONS.put(315, "1.11");
        VERSIONS.put(210, "1.10.x");
        VERSIONS.put(110, "1.9.3 - 1.9.4");
        VERSIONS.put(109, "1.9.2");
        VERSIONS.put(108, "1.9.1");
        VERSIONS.put(107, "1.9");
        VERSIONS.put(47, "1.8.x");
        VERSIONS.put(5, "1.7.6 - 1.7.10");
        VERSIONS.put(4, "1.7.2 - 1.7.5");
    }

    private static Boolean viaPresent;
    private static Method viaGetApi;
    private static Method viaGetPlayerVersion;

    private static Boolean floodgatePresent;
    private static Object floodgateApi;
    private static Method floodgateIsPlayer;

    private ClientUtil() {
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    public static int serverProtocol() {
        try {
            return Bukkit.getUnsafe().getProtocolVersion();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return -1;
        }
    }

    /** Real protocol version of the player (uses ViaVersion when installed). */
    public static int protocol(Player player) {
        int via = viaProtocol(player.getUniqueId());
        if (via > 0) {
            return via;
        }
        return player.getProtocolVersion();
    }

    public static String versionName(int protocol) {
        if (protocol <= 0) {
            return "Unknown";
        }
        if (protocol == serverProtocol()) {
            return Bukkit.getMinecraftVersion();
        }
        String name = VERSIONS.get(protocol);
        if (name != null) {
            return name;
        }
        if (protocol > 774) {
            return "Newer than 1.21.11";
        }
        return "Unknown";
    }

    public static String version(Player player) {
        int protocol = protocol(player);
        return versionName(protocol) + " (protocol " + protocol + ")";
    }

    public static String brand(Player player) {
        String brand = player.getClientBrandName();
        return brand == null || brand.isBlank() ? "unknown" : brand;
    }

    public static String describe(Player player) {
        return versionName(protocol(player)) + " / " + brand(player) + (isBedrock(player.getUniqueId()) ? " / Bedrock" : "");
    }

    private static int viaProtocol(UUID uuid) {
        try {
            if (viaPresent == null) {
                viaPresent = Bukkit.getPluginManager().getPlugin("ViaVersion") != null;
                if (viaPresent) {
                    Class<?> via = Class.forName("com.viaversion.viaversion.api.Via");
                    viaGetApi = via.getMethod("getAPI");
                    Object api = viaGetApi.invoke(null);
                    viaGetPlayerVersion = api.getClass().getMethod("getPlayerVersion", UUID.class);
                }
            }
            if (!viaPresent) {
                return -1;
            }
            Object api = viaGetApi.invoke(null);
            Object result = viaGetPlayerVersion.invoke(api, uuid);
            return result instanceof Integer i ? i : -1;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            viaPresent = false;
            return -1;
        }
    }

    /** Bedrock player joined through Geyser / Floodgate. */
    public static boolean isBedrock(UUID uuid) {
        try {
            if (floodgatePresent == null) {
                floodgatePresent = Bukkit.getPluginManager().getPlugin("floodgate") != null;
                if (floodgatePresent) {
                    Class<?> apiClass = Class.forName("org.geysermc.floodgate.api.FloodgateApi");
                    floodgateApi = apiClass.getMethod("getInstance").invoke(null);
                    floodgateIsPlayer = apiClass.getMethod("isFloodgatePlayer", UUID.class);
                }
            }
            if (floodgatePresent) {
                Object result = floodgateIsPlayer.invoke(floodgateApi, uuid);
                if (result instanceof Boolean b && b) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            floodgatePresent = false;
        }
        // Floodgate default UUID format: 00000000-0000-0000-xxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx
        return uuid.getMostSignificantBits() == 0L;
    }
}
```

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util;

import java.util.function.LongSupplier;

/** Time source used by timing based checks (replaceable in tests). */
public final class Clock {

    private static LongSupplier nanos = System::nanoTime;

    private Clock() {
    }

    public static long nanos() {
        return nanos.getAsLong();
    }

    public static long millis() {
        return nanos.getAsLong() / 1_000_000L;
    }

    /** Only for tests: replace the time source. */
    public static void use(LongSupplier source) {
        nanos = source;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/util/DurationUtil.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

public final class DurationUtil {

    private static final Pattern PART = Pattern.compile("(\\d+)\\s*(y|mo|w|d|h|m|s)");

    private DurationUtil() {
    }

    /**
     * Parses "30d", "1d12h", "45m", "perm" ... Returns null for permanent, or throws when invalid.
     */
    public static Duration parse(String input) {
        String text = input.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).trim();
        if (text.equals("perm") || text.equals("permanent") || text.equals("forever") || text.equals("-1")) {
            return null;
        }
        Matcher matcher = PART.matcher(text);
        Duration total = Duration.ZERO;
        int end = 0;
        boolean found = false;
        while (matcher.find()) {
            if (matcher.start() != end) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(input);
            }
            end = matcher.end();
            found = true;
            long amount = Long.parseLong(matcher.group(1));
            total = total.plus(switch (matcher.group(2)) {
                case "y" -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 365);
                case "mo" -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 30);
                case "w" -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 7);
                case "d" -> Duration.ofDays(amount);
                case "h" -> Duration.ofHours(amount);
                case "m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(amount);
                default -> Duration.ofSeconds(amount);
            });
        }
        if (!found || end != text.length() || total.isZero()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(input);
        }
        return total;
    }

    public static boolean isDuration(String input) {
        try {
            parse(input);
            return true;
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static String format(Duration duration) {
        if (duration == null) {
            return "Permanent";
        }
        long seconds = duration.getSeconds();
        long days = seconds / 86400;
        long hours = (seconds % 86400) / 3600;
        long minutes = (seconds % 3600) / 60;
        long secs = seconds % 60;
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        if (days > 0) {
            sb.append(days).append("d ");
        }
        if (hours > 0) {
            sb.append(hours).append("h ");
        }
        if (minutes > 0) {
            sb.append(minutes).append("m ");
        }
        if (secs > 0 || sb.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append(secs).append("s");
        }
        return sb.toString().trim();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/util/MathUtil.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util;

import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Locale;

public final class MathUtil {

    /** Smallest possible rotation step of a real mouse (0% sensitivity), in degrees. */
    public static final double MIN_SENSITIVITY_STEP = 0.0096;

    private MathUtil() {
    }

    public static float wrapDegrees(float value) {
        value %= 360.0F;
        if (value >= 180.0F) {
            value -= 360.0F;
        }
        if (value < -180.0F) {
            value += 360.0F;
        }
        return value;
    }

    /**
     * Greatest common divisor of two rotation deltas with a small tolerance, so float rounding
     * errors do not break it. Legit mouse rotations always share a divisor that comes from
     * the mouse sensitivity.
     */
    public static double gcd(double a, double b) {
        a = Math.abs(a);
        b = Math.abs(b);
        if (a < b) {
            double t = a;
            a = b;
            b = t;
        }
        int guard = 0;
        while (b > 0.0015 && guard++ < 64) {
            double t = a % b;
            a = b;
            b = t;
        }
        return a;
    }

    public static double mean(Collection<? extends Number> values) {
        if (values.isEmpty()) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (Number n : values) {
            sum += n.doubleValue();
        }
        return sum / values.size();
    }

    public static double deviation(Collection<? extends Number> values) {
        if (values.size() < 2) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double mean = mean(values);
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (Number n : values) {
            double d = n.doubleValue() - mean;
            sum += d * d;
        }
        return Math.sqrt(sum / (values.size() - 1));
    }

    /** Distance from a point to the closest point of a box (0 when inside). */
    public static double distanceToBox(Vector point, BoundingBox box) {
        double dx = Math.max(Math.max(box.getMinX() - point.getX(), 0.0), point.getX() - box.getMaxX());
        double dy = Math.max(Math.max(box.getMinY() - point.getY(), 0.0), point.getY() - box.getMaxY());
        double dz = Math.max(Math.max(box.getMinZ() - point.getZ(), 0.0), point.getZ() - box.getMaxZ());
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }

    public static double horizontalDistanceToBox(Vector point, BoundingBox box) {
        double dx = Math.max(Math.max(box.getMinX() - point.getX(), 0.0), point.getX() - box.getMaxX());
        double dz = Math.max(Math.max(box.getMinZ() - point.getZ(), 0.0), point.getZ() - box.getMaxZ());
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
    }

    /** Look direction for a yaw / pitch pair (same math as the vanilla client). */
    public static Vector direction(float yaw, float pitch) {
        double rotX = Math.toRadians(yaw);
        double rotY = Math.toRadians(pitch);
        double xz = Math.cos(rotY);
        return new Vector(-xz * Math.sin(rotX), -Math.sin(rotY), xz * Math.cos(rotX));
    }

    /** Angle in degrees between two vectors. */
    public static double angle(Vector a, Vector b) {
        double la = a.length();
        double lb = b.length();
        if (la == 0.0 || lb == 0.0) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double cos = a.dot(b) / (la * lb);
        cos = Math.max(-1.0, Math.min(1.0, cos));
        return Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(cos));
    }

    public static float lerpAngle(float from, float to, float t) {
        return from + wrapDegrees(to - from) * t;
    }

    public static String format(double value) {
        return format(value, 3);
    }

    public static String format(double value, int decimals) {
        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%." + decimals + "f", value);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/util/Text.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util;

import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.format.TextDecoration;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.serializer.legacy.LegacyComponentSerializer;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.serializer.plain.PlainTextComponentSerializer;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Colour helpers. Supports legacy '&' colour codes and '&#RRGGBB' hex colours.
 */
public final class Text {

    private static final LegacyComponentSerializer LEGACY = LegacyComponentSerializer.builder()
            .character('&')
            .hexColors()
            .build();

    private Text() {
    }

    public static Component color(String text) {
        return LEGACY.deserialize(text == null ? "" : text);
    }

    /** Component without the default italic style of item names / lore. */
    public static Component item(String text) {
        return color(text).decoration(TextDecoration.ITALIC, false);
    }

    public static List<Component> lore(List<String> lines) {
        List<Component> out = new ArrayList<>(lines.size());
        for (String line : lines) {
            out.add(item(line));
        }
        return out;
    }

    public static Component lines(List<String> lines) {
        Component out = Component.empty();
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
            if (i > 0) {
                out = out.append(Component.newline());
            }
            out = out.append(color(lines.get(i)));
        }
        return out;
    }

    public static String plain(Component component) {
        return PlainTextComponentSerializer.plainText().serialize(component);
    }

    /** Removes colour codes from a legacy string (used for console / log files). */
    public static String strip(String text) {
        return plain(color(text));
    }
}

```

`src/main/resources/config.yml`:

```yml
# =====================================================================
#                     Astral AntiCheat - Paper 1.21.11
# =====================================================================
#  BLOCK MODE (default)
#  Astral blocks cheats: hits of combat cheaters do not register, flyers /
#  speeders are pulled back, silent swaps and macros are cancelled.
#  Nobody is kicked or banned automatically. Staff with astral.alerts get ONE
#  short alert line per player that says what they might be using, and every
#  flag is written to plugins/Astral/logs/<date>.log.
#
#  Only want to watch? Set settings.mitigation: false.
#  Want automatic punishments? Set punishments.enabled: true and give a
#  check "punish: true" + punish-commands.
#
#  Minigames that move players themselves (battle bus, launch pads, parkour
#  boosters, cutscenes): riding / standing on entities, plugin teleports and
#  plugin velocity are handled automatically. For anything else use
#  settings.exempt-worlds or "/astral exempt <player> <seconds>".
#
#  Every check has:
#    enabled          - turn the check on / off  (also: /astral toggle <check>)
#    alert-vl         - staff only get an alert once the violation level (VL) reaches this
#    mitigate         - block this cheat (only used when settings.mitigation is true)
#    mitigate-vl      - VL needed before blocking starts
#    punish           - run punish-commands at punish-vl (only when punishments.enabled is true)
#    punish-vl        - VL needed to punish
#    punish-commands  - console commands. Placeholders: %player% %uuid% %check% %type% %vl% %ping%
#    decay            - VL removed from the player every "violations.decay-interval-seconds"
#
#  Players with the permission astral.bypass are NEVER checked or flagged.
#  astral.bypass.<check> bypasses one check (example: astral.bypass.reach).
#  /astral bypass <player> toggles it in-game.
# =====================================================================

# Do not change - used to update old config files
config-version: 2

settings:
  # true = block cheats (cancel hits, setbacks, cancel blocks...). false = WATCH ONLY (alerts + logs).
  mitigation: true
  # A setback never teleports a player further than this (blocks). Further away = the illegal
  # move is undone instead (back to the last checked position).
  max-setback-distance: 12.0
  # Worlds where Astral checks nobody (e.g. a lobby or a minigame world)
  exempt-worlds: []
  # Skip checks while the server is lagging (prevents false flags)
  lag-protection:
    enabled: true
    min-tps: 17.5
  # Combat checks are skipped for players with a higher ping than this (ms)
  max-combat-ping: 800
  # Highest ping (ms) used for lag compensation in reach / hitbox checks
  max-ping-compensation: 600
  # Ticks a player is ignored after joining
  join-exempt-ticks: 60
  # Skip Bedrock players (Geyser / Floodgate) - their movement is translated
  exempt-bedrock-players: true

alerts:
  # Staff with astral.alerts have alerts turned on when they join
  enabled-by-default: true
  # true = ONE line per player that says what they might be using (details in the hover).
  # false = old style, one line per flag.
  compact: true
  # Flags are collected this long before the first line about a player is sent
  gather-seconds: 1.0
  # A new cheat of the same player is reported at most once every ... seconds
  interval-seconds: 5
  # The same cheats are only reminded every ... seconds (with the new count)
  repeat-seconds: 30
  # After this long without flags, a player's cheats count as new again
  reset-after-seconds: 120
  # (compact: false only) minimum time between two alerts for the same player + check (milliseconds)
  cooldown-ms: 400
  # Also print alerts to the console
  console: true
  # Command run when a staff member clicks an alert
  click-command: "/astral tp %player%"

violations:
  # How often (seconds) VL decays
  decay-interval-seconds: 30

punishments:
  # Automatic punishments. OFF: Astral never kicks or bans anyone by itself.
  enabled: false
  # Broadcast the punish message (messages.yml -> punish-broadcast) to everyone
  broadcast: false
  # Default ban length used by the manual /astral ban command (e.g. 7d, 12h, 30m, perm)
  default-ban-duration: "30d"

freeze:
  # Commands run when a frozen player disconnects (%player% placeholder). Empty = nothing.
  quit-commands: []
  # Commands frozen players are still allowed to use
  allowed-commands: ["/msg", "/r", "/tell", "/w", "/reply", "/helpop"]

logging:
  # Write every flag to plugins/Astral/logs/<date>.log
  file: true
  # Amount of flags remembered per player for /astral logs
  memory-size: 150

# =====================================================================
#                               CHECKS
# =====================================================================
checks:

  # ------------------------------ COMBAT ------------------------------
  reach:
    enabled: true
    alert-vl: 2.0
    mitigate: true
    mitigate-vl: 0.0
    punish: false
    punish-vl: 25
    punish-commands: []
    decay: 2.0
    # Extra blocks allowed on top of the vanilla attack range
    tolerance: 0.1
    # Hits needed in a row before a flag (filters jitter)
    buffer: 2.0

  hitbox:
    enabled: true
    alert-vl: 2.0
    mitigate: true
    mitigate-vl: 0.0
    punish: false
    punish-vl: 25
    punish-commands: []
    decay: 2.0
    # Extra hitbox expansion allowed (blocks)
    margin: 0.1
    buffer: 3.0

  killaura:
    enabled: true
    alert-vl: 2.0
    mitigate: true
    mitigate-vl: 0.0
    punish: false
    punish-vl: 20
    punish-commands: []
    decay: 1.5
    multi: true       # hits 2+ entities in the same tick
    post: true        # attack sent after the movement packet (silent rotation auras)
    no-swing: true    # attacks without swinging the arm
    walls: true       # attacks through solid walls
    switch: true      # switching between targets that are far apart instantly
    # When blocking is on: attacks of a flagged player are cancelled for this many ticks

  autoblock:
```

`src/main/resources/messages.yml`:

```yml
# =====================================================================
#                    Astral AntiCheat - messages
#   Colors: legacy & codes (&a, &l ...) and hex colors (&#00E5FF)
# =====================================================================

prefix: "&8[&#00E5FF&lAstral&8] &r"

no-permission: "%prefix%&cYou do not have permission to do that."
player-only: "%prefix%&cOnly players can use this command."
player-not-found: "%prefix%&cPlayer &f%player% &cwas not found."
unknown-check: "%prefix%&cUnknown check &f%check%&c. Use &f/astral checks&c."
unknown-subcommand: "%prefix%&cUnknown sub command. Use &f/astral help&c."
reloaded: "%prefix%&aConfiguration reloaded in &f%time%ms&a."

alerts-enabled: "%prefix%&aAlerts enabled. &7You will see every flag."
alerts-disabled: "%prefix%&cAlerts disabled."
verbose-enabled: "%prefix%&aVerbose enabled. &7You will see EVERY flag (no cooldown, no minimum VL)."
verbose-disabled: "%prefix%&cVerbose disabled."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Compact staff alert (alerts.compact: true). Flags of a player are grouped
# into ONE line that says what they are most likely using. Details are in the
# hover. How often a line is sent: config.yml -> alerts.
#   %player% %cheats% %flags% %vl% %ping% %tps% %client% %mode%
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
compact-alert: "%prefix%&f%player% &7may be using %cheats% &8[&7VL &c%vl% &8| &7%ping%ms &8| %mode%&8]"
# one cheat inside %cheats%  (%cheat% %count%)
compact-alert-cheat: "&c%cheat% &7x%count%"
compact-alert-separator: "&7, "
compact-alert-hover:
  - "&#00E5FF&l%player% &7- possible cheats"
  - "%details%"
  - ""
  - "&7Ping: &f%ping%ms &8| &7TPS: &f%tps%"
  - "&7Client: &f%client%"
  - "&7Astral: %mode%"
  - ""
  - "&eClick to teleport"
# one line per cheat in the hover (%cheat% %count% %checks% %vl%) + the last detail (%debug%)
compact-alert-detail: "&c%cheat% &7x%count% &8- &f%checks% &8(&7vl %vl%&8)"
compact-alert-detail-info: "&8   %debug%"
mode-blocking: "&cblocked"
mode-watching: "&ewatching"

# Old style alert, one line per flag (alerts.compact: false).  %player% %check% %type% %cheat% %vl% %ping% %tps% %debug% %category%
alert: "%prefix%&f%player% &7failed &#00E5FF%check% &8(&7%type%&8) &7x&c%vl% &8[&7%ping%ms&8]"
alert-hover:
  - "&#00E5FF&l%check% &8(&7%type%&8)"
  - "&7Might be: &c%cheat%"
  - "&7%description%"
  - ""
  - "&7Category: &f%category%"
  - "&7Violations: &c%vl%"
  - "&7Ping: &f%ping%ms &8| &7TPS: &f%tps%"
  - "&7Client: &f%client%"
  - "&7Info: &f%debug%"
  - ""
  - "&eClick to teleport"
verbose: "&8[&7V&8] &7%player% &8» &f%check% &8(&7%type%&8) &c%cheat% &7%debug% &8(&7vl %vl%&8)"

# Sent to everyone when a player is punished (punishments.broadcast)
punish-broadcast:
  - "&8&m                                                  "
  - " &#00E5FF&lAstral AntiCheat"
  - " &f%player% &7was removed for &cUnfair Advantage&7."
  - "&8&m                                                  "
punish-staff: "%prefix%&c%player% &7was punished for &f%check% &8(&7vl %vl%&8)"

kick-screen:
  - "&#00E5FF&lAstral AntiCheat"
  - ""
  - "&cYou have been kicked."
  - "&7Reason: &f%reason%"
  - ""
  - "&8by MrTahaDarvish"
ban-screen:
  - "&#00E5FF&lAstral AntiCheat"
  - ""
  - "&cYou are banned from this server."
  - "&7Reason: &f%reason%"
  - "&7Expires: &f%expires%"
  - ""
  - "&8by MrTahaDarvish"

kicked: "%prefix%&aKicked &f%player%&a. &7(%reason%)"
banned: "%prefix%&aBanned &f%player% &afor &f%duration%&a. &7(%reason%)"
unbanned: "%prefix%&aUnbanned &f%player%&a."
not-banned: "%prefix%&c%player% is not banned."

bypass-enabled: "%prefix%&f%player% &anow bypasses the anticheat. &7(not flagged, not blocked)"

debug-disabled: "%prefix%&cDebug for &f%check% &cdisabled."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# What a check + type is shown as in the alerts ("might be using ...").
# <check>: "name"   or   <check>: {default: "name", <type>: "name"}
# (check ids are the section names in config.yml, types are shown in the alert hover)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
cheat-names:
  reach: "Reach"
  hitbox: "KillAura / Hitbox"
  killaura:
    default: "KillAura"
    post: "KillAura (silent rotations)"

    silentaim: "Silent aim"

    robotic: "Aimbot (robotic rotations)"
  crystalaura: "Crystal aura"
  autoblock: "AutoBlock"
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/AlertDigestTest.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.AlertDigest;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/** Compact alerts: a cheater flagging all the time must produce a handful of lines, not hundreds. */
class AlertDigestTest {

    private static final long GATHER = 1_000;
    private static final long INTERVAL = 5_000;
    private static final long REPEAT = 30_000;
    private static final long QUIET = 120_000;

    /** Runs the alert task every 500 ms like the plugin, returns the lines that were sent. */
    private static List<List<AlertDigest.Entry>> run(AlertDigest digest, long from, long to, long flagEvery, String... cheats) {
        List<List<AlertDigest.Entry>> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        for (long now = from; now < to; now += 50) {
            if ((now - from) % flagEvery == 0) {
                for (String cheat : cheats) {
                    digest.add(cheat, cheat + " Type", 5.0, "debug", now, QUIET);
                }
            }
            if (now % 500 == 0 && digest.due(now, GATHER, INTERVAL, REPEAT)) {
                lines.add(digest.drain(now));
            }
        }
        return lines;
    }

    @Test
    void flyingCheaterGivesAFewLinesNotHundreds() {
        AlertDigest digest = new AlertDigest();
        // a flag every 100 ms for one minute = 600 flags
        List<List<AlertDigest.Entry>> lines = run(digest, 0, 60_000, 100, "Fly");
        int flags = lines.stream().mapToInt(line -> line.getFirst().count).sum();
        Report.add("ALERTS", "600 Fly flags in one minute -> " + lines.size() + " alert lines",
                "lines=" + lines.size() + " flags shown=" + flags, lines.size() <= 3);
        assertTrue(lines.size() >= 2 && lines.size() <= 3, "lines=" + lines.size());
        assertTrue(lines.getFirst().getFirst().count >= 9, "the first line gathers the first second of flags");
    }

    @Test
    void aNewCheatIsReportedQuicklyAndFirst() {
        AlertDigest digest = new AlertDigest();
        List<List<AlertDigest.Entry>> first = run(digest, 0, 10_000, 100, "Fly");
        assertEquals(1, first.size());
        // he starts using a kill aura too: reported within interval-seconds, listed first
        List<List<AlertDigest.Entry>> next = run(digest, 10_000, 12_000, 100, "KillAura (silent rotations)", "Fly");
        assertEquals(1, next.size(), "new cheat -> one new line");
        assertEquals("KillAura (silent rotations)", next.getFirst().getFirst().name);
        Report.add("ALERTS", "New cheat while flagging: reported at once, listed first",
                next.getFirst().getFirst().name + " x" + next.getFirst().getFirst().count, true);
    }

    @Test
    void quietPlayerStartsFresh() {
        AlertDigest digest = new AlertDigest();
        run(digest, 0, 5_000, 200, "Speed");
        List<List<AlertDigest.Entry>> later = run(digest, 200_000, 202_000, 200, "Speed");
        assertEquals(1, later.size(), "after a quiet time the same cheat is reported again");
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/BattleRoyaleTest.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.Sim;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.SimEntity;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.SimPlayer;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.ArmorStand;
import org.bukkit.entity.Boat;
import org.bukkit.entity.EntityType;
import org.bukkit.entity.Shulker;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Battle royale / minigame situations where a PLUGIN moves the players: a battle bus they ride,
 * stand on, get teleported or pushed along with, then the drop (free fall, elytra, slow falling
 * parachute) and the landing. Blocking is on (the default). Legit players must get no flags and
 * no setbacks - at 50, 150 and 300 ms ping.
 */
class BattleRoyaleTest {

    private static final int BUS_Y = 150;

    private static Sim map() {
        return new Sim().floor(90);
    }

    private static void assertLegit(Sim sim, SimPlayer player, String scenario) {
        String summary = sim.summary(player);
        boolean ok = sim.flags(player).isEmpty() && player.astralTeleports == 0;
        Report.add("LEGIT", scenario + " (" + player.ping + "ms)",
                summary + (player.astralTeleports > 0 ? " setbacks=" + player.astralTeleports : ""), ok);
        assertTrue(sim.flags(player).isEmpty(), scenario + " -> " + summary);
        assertTrue(player.astralTeleports == 0, scenario + " -> Astral teleported the player " + player.astralTeleports + " times");
    }

    /** Ticks until the player is on the floor again (and a few more), gliding ends on landing. */
    private static void untilLanded(Sim sim, SimPlayer player) {
        int landed = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 2000 && landed < 40; i++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == player && pl.gliding && pl.onGround) {
                    sim.glide(pl, false);
                }
            });
            if (player.onGround && player.sy < 65) {
                landed++;
            }
        }
        assertTrue(landed >= 40, player.name + " should have landed (y=" + player.sy + ")");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "bus seat {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 150, 300, 500, 800})
    void ridingTheBusThenFreeFall(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("BusRider" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        // an invisible armor stand seat the plugin moves along the bus route
        SimEntity seat = new SimEntity(sim.world, ArmorStand.class, EntityType.ARMOR_STAND, -60, BUS_Y, 0, 0.5, 1.975);
        sim.teleport(p, new Location(sim.world.world, seat.x, seat.y, seat.z));
        sim.mount(p, seat);
        for (int t = 0; t < 140; t++) {
            seat.move(0.7, t < 70 ? 0.05 : -0.05, 0.1);
            p.ride(seat.x, seat.y + 0.5, seat.z);
            sim.tick(null);
        }
        // jump off: Paper puts the player next to the seat, then he falls ~85 blocks
        sim.dismount(p, new Location(sim.world.world, seat.x, seat.y, seat.z));
        untilLanded(sim, p);
        sim.tick(pl -> pl.forward = 1);
        sim.idle(20);
        assertLegit(sim, p, "riding the battle bus, jumping off, free fall");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "teleport stream {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 150, 300, 500, 800})
    void teleportedAlongTheBusRouteThenElytra(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("BusTeleport" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        // no vehicle: the plugin teleports the player every tick (Paper freezes him until he confirms)
        for (int t = 0; t < 120; t++) {
            sim.teleport(p, new Location(sim.world.world, -48 + t * 0.4, BUS_Y, 0.5));
            sim.tick(null);
        }
        // drop: falls a bit, opens the elytra the plugin gave him, glides down
        sim.idle(15);
        sim.glide(p, true);
        for (int t = 0; t < 400 && p.gliding; t++) {
            int tick = t;
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                pl.pitch = tick < 200 ? 15 : 30;
                pl.yaw += 3.0F; // circles down over the map
                if (pl.gliding && pl.onGround) {
                    sim.glide(pl, false);
                }
            });
        }
        untilLanded(sim, p);
        assertLegit(sim, p, "teleported along the bus route, elytra drop");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "velocity bus {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 150, 300, 500, 800})
    void carriedByVelocityThenSlowFallingParachute(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("BusVelocity" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        sim.teleport(p, new Location(sim.world.world, -60, BUS_Y, 0.5));
        sim.idle(3);
        // the plugin carries the player with setVelocity every tick
        for (int t = 0; t < 120; t++) {
            sim.push(p, new Vector(0.8, 0.0, 0.0));
            sim.tick(null);
        }
        // drop with a slow falling "parachute" that runs out in the air
        sim.effect(p, "slow_falling", true);
        sim.idle(150);
        sim.effect(p, "slow_falling", false);
        untilLanded(sim, p);
        assertLegit(sim, p, "carried by plugin velocity, slow falling parachute");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "no gravity {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 150, 300, 500, 800})
    void floatingWithGravityOffThenFall(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("BusFloat" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        sim.teleport(p, new Location(sim.world.world, -40, BUS_Y, 0.5));
        sim.idle(3);
        sim.gravity(p, false);
        sim.push(p, new Vector(0.6, 0.0, 0.0));
        sim.idle(100);
        sim.gravity(p, true);
        untilLanded(sim, p);
        assertLegit(sim, p, "plugin turned gravity off, then on again mid-air");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "shulker floor {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 150, 300, 500, 800})
    void standingOnAMovingShulkerFloor(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("BusFloor" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        // the bus floor is an invisible shulker the plugin moves; the player stands on it
        SimEntity floor = new SimEntity(sim.world, Shulker.class, EntityType.SHULKER, -40.0, BUS_Y, 0.5, 1.0, 1.0);
        sim.teleport(p, new Location(sim.world.world, floor.x, floor.y + 1.0, floor.z));
        for (int t = 0; t < 8; t++) {
            // waits on the floor until the teleport is confirmed
            p.physicsTick();
            p.x = floor.x;
            p.y = floor.y + 1.0;
            p.z = floor.z;
            p.my = 0.0;
            p.onGround = true;
            p.sendMovement();
            sim.tickEnd(p);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        for (int t = 0; t < 120; t++) {
            floor.move(0.5, 0.0, 0.0);
            // the client stands on the shulker: carried along, on ground
            p.x = floor.x;
            p.y = floor.y + 1.0;
            p.z = floor.z;
            p.mx = p.my = p.mz = 0.0;
            p.onGround = true;
            p.sendMovement();
            sim.tickEnd(p);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        // walks off the edge
        for (int t = 0; t < 10; t++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> pl.forward = 1);
        }
        untilLanded(sim, p);
        assertLegit(sim, p, "standing on a moving shulker bus floor, walking off");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "spectator {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 150, 300, 500, 800})
    void spectatingTheBusThenSurvival(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("BusSpectator" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        sim.gameMode(p, GameMode.SPECTATOR);
        for (int t = 0; t < 80; t++) {
            sim.teleport(p, new Location(sim.world.world, -40 + t, BUS_Y, 0.5));
            sim.tick(null);
        }
        sim.gameMode(p, GameMode.SURVIVAL);
        untilLanded(sim, p);
        assertLegit(sim, p, "spectating the bus, survival mid-air");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "launch {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 150, 300, 500, 800})
    void cancelledDoubleJumpLaunch(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Launcher" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        p.allowFlight = true;
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int launch = 0; launch < 3; launch++) {
            // jump, then double jump: the client starts flying, the plugin cancels it and launches
            sim.tick(pl -> pl.jump = true);
            sim.tick(pl -> pl.jump = false);
            p.flying = true;
            assertFalse(sim.toggleFlight(p, true, true));
            p.later(p.halfRtt(), () -> p.flying = false);
            sim.push(p, new Vector(0.0, 1.2, 1.4));
            untilLanded(sim, p);
        }
        assertLegit(sim, p, "double jump launch pad (flight toggle cancelled by the plugin)");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "boat ride {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 300, 800})
    void drivingABoatThePluginLifts(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("BoatRide" + ping, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        SimEntity boat = new SimEntity(sim.world, Boat.class, EntityType.OAK_BOAT, 0.5, 64, 3.5, 1.375, 0.5625);
        boat.gravity = false; // a ride / bus plugin moves the boat
        sim.mount(p, boat);
        moveBoat(sim, p, boat, 60, 0.15);
        assertFalse(sim.flagged(p, "BoatFly"), "a boat a plugin moves is not BoatFly -> " + sim.summary(p));
        assertLegit(sim, p, "driving a boat a plugin lifts into the air");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ cheaters on the bus

    private static SimPlayer dropFromBus(Sim sim, String name, int ping) {
        SimPlayer p = sim.join(name, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.ping = ping;
        sim.idle(5);
        SimEntity seat = new SimEntity(sim.world, ArmorStand.class, EntityType.ARMOR_STAND, -30, BUS_Y, 0, 0.5, 1.975);
        sim.teleport(p, new Location(sim.world.world, seat.x, seat.y, seat.z));
        sim.mount(p, seat);
        for (int t = 0; t < 60; t++) {
            seat.move(0.7, 0.0, 0.0);
            p.ride(seat.x, seat.y + 0.5, seat.z);
            sim.tick(null);
        }
        sim.dismount(p, new Location(sim.world.world, seat.x, seat.y, seat.z));
        return p;
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "fly after the drop {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 300})
    void cheaterFlyingAfterTheDropIsCaught(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = dropFromBus(sim, "DropFlyer" + ping, ping);
        sim.idle(50); // falls for a while
        double before = p.sy;
        for (int t = 0; t < 80; t++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> pl.cheatFlyY = 0.0); // hovers in the air
        }
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(p, "Flight");
        Report.add("CHEAT", "Fly / hover after jumping off the battle bus (" + ping + "ms)", sim.summary(p), caught);
        assertTrue(caught, sim.summary(p) + " y " + before + " -> " + p.sy);
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "nofall after the drop {0}ms")
    @ValueSource(ints = {50, 300})
    void noFallCheaterLandingFromTheBusIsCaught(int ping) {
        Sim sim = map();
        SimPlayer p = dropFromBus(sim, "DropNoFall" + ping, ping);
        p.cheatGround = true; // always tells the server "on ground"
        untilLanded(sim, p);
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(p, "NoFall");
        Report.add("CHEAT", "NoFall landing from the battle bus (" + ping + "ms)", sim.summary(p), caught);
        assertTrue(caught, sim.summary(p));
    }

    /** The client drives the boat: Paper fires PlayerMoveEvent for the vehicle packet. */
    static void moveBoat(Sim sim, SimPlayer p, SimEntity boat, int ticks, double rise) {
        for (int t = 0; t < ticks; t++) {
            Location from = new Location(sim.world.world, boat.x, boat.y, boat.z);
            boat.move(0.2, rise, 0.0);
            Location to = new Location(sim.world.world, boat.x, boat.y, boat.z);
            p.ride(boat.x, boat.y + 0.5, boat.z);
            sim.call(new PlayerMoveEvent(p.player, from, to));
            sim.tick(null);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/CheatClientTest.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;

import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.Human;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.Sim;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.SimPlayer;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;
import org.bukkit.damage.DamageSource;
import org.bukkit.entity.Arrow;
import org.bukkit.entity.EnderCrystal;
import org.bukkit.entity.FishHook;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityShootBowEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.ClickType;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryAction;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryType;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerFishEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRegisterChannelEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.EquipmentSlot;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Random;
import com.google.common.base.Function;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Simulated hacked clients. Every cheat has to be detected by the right check.
 */
@SuppressWarnings({"deprecation", "removal"})
class CheatClientTest {

    private static void assertCaughtType(Sim sim, SimPlayer cheater, String scenario, String check, String type) {
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(cheater, check, type);
        Report.add("CHEAT", scenario, sim.summary(cheater), caught);
        assertTrue(caught, scenario + " should be caught by " + check + "(" + type + ") -> " + sim.summary(cheater));
    }

    /** Caught by one of the checks (with blocking on, the first check to catch a cheat stops it). */
    private static void assertCaught(Sim sim, SimPlayer cheater, String scenario, String... checks) {
        boolean caught = false;
        for (String check : checks) {
            caught |= sim.flagged(cheater, check);
        }
        Report.add("CHEAT", scenario, sim.summary(cheater), caught);
        assertTrue(caught, scenario + " should be caught by " + String.join(" / ", checks) + " -> " + sim.summary(cheater));
    }

    private static Sim arena() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(60);
        return sim;
    }

    private static void face(SimPlayer player, Vector point) {
        double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(player.eyeClient(), point);
        player.yaw = (float) r[0];
        player.pitch = (float) r[1];
    }

    private static Vector body(SimPlayer target) {
        return new Vector(target.sx, target.sy + 1.2, target.sz);
    }

    @Test
    void flyHover() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("FlyHover", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(3);
        sim.tick(pl -> pl.jump = true);
        sim.tick(pl -> pl.jump = false);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                pl.cheatFlyY = 0.0;
                pl.forward = 1;
            });
        }
        assertCaught(sim, p, "Fly (hover)", "Flight");
    }

    @Test
    void timer() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Timer", 0.5, 64, -40.5);
        sim.idle(3);
        // 5 client ticks for every 4 server ticks = game speed 125%
        for (int i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
            sim.clientTick(p, pl -> pl.forward = 1, null);
            if (i % 5 != 4) {
                sim.serverTick();
            }
        }
        assertCaught(sim, p, "Timer 1.25x", "Timer");
    }

            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                face(pl, body(b));

                sim.swing(pl);
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);

        assertCaught(sim, a, "Multi aura (2 targets per tick)", "KillAura");
    }

    @Test
    void silentRotationAuraPost() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("PostAura", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("VictimP", 2.5, 64, 1.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> pl.yaw = 0, t % 5 == 0 ? pl -> {
                // rotate in the movement packet, then attack after it (silent rotations)
                double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(pl.eyeServer(), body(b));
                pl.sendRawPosition(pl.sx, pl.sy, pl.sz, (float) r[0], (float) r[1], true);
                sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                sim.swing(pl);
            } : null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaught(sim, a, "Silent rotation aura (attack after movement)", "KillAura", "Aim");
    }

    @Test
    void silentRotationAuraInMovePacket() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("PostAura2", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("VictimP2", 2.5, 64, 1.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
            boolean hit = i % 5 == 0;
            a.yaw = 0;
            if (hit) {
                double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(a.eyeClient(), body(b));
                a.silentYaw = (float) r[0];
                a.silentPitch = (float) r[1];
            } else {
                a.silentYaw = null;
                a.silentPitch = null;
            }
            sim.clientTick(a, null, hit ? pl -> {
                // the movement packet carried the aim, the attack comes after it
                sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                sim.swing(pl);
            } : null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaught(sim, a, "Silent rotation aura (aim inside the movement packet)", "KillAura", "Aim");
    }

    @Test
    void blink() {
        // movement packets are held back for 9 ticks and released at once (blink / fake lag)
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Blink", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        List<double[]> held = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
            p.yaw = 0;
            p.forward = 1;
            p.sprinting = true;
            p.physicsTick();
            if (i % 10 < 9) {
                held.add(new double[]{p.x, p.y, p.z});
            } else {
                for (double[] pos : held) {
                    p.sendRawPosition(pos[0], pos[1], pos[2], p.yaw, p.pitch, true);
                }
                held.clear();
                p.sendMovement();
            }
            sim.tickEnd(p);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaughtType(sim, p, "Blink (movement held back, then sent at once)", "BadPackets", "Multi");
    }

    @Test
    void silentAim() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("SilentAim", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("VictimS", 3.0, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
            int t = i;
            boolean hitTick = t % 8 == 0;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                pl.yaw = 0;
                pl.pitch = 10;
                if (hitTick) {
                    // correct packet order, but the rotation only points at the target for this one tick
                    sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                    face(pl, body(b));
                }
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaught(sim, a, "Silent aim (flick to target and back)", "Aim");
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotGcd() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("Aimbot", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("VictimG", 0.5, 64, 3.0);
        Random random = new Random(4);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 500; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(pl.eyeClient(), body(b));
                // smooth aim with randomisation: perfect floats, no mouse steps
                pl.yaw += (float) ((r[0] - pl.yaw) * 0.6 + (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 3.0);
                pl.pitch += (float) ((r[1] - pl.pitch) * 0.6 + (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 4.0);
                if (t % 4 == 0) {
                    sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, pl -> {
                pl.strafe = (t / 15) % 2 == 0 ? 1 : -1;
                pl.jump = true;
            }, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaught(sim, a, "Aimbot / aim assist (rotations without mouse steps)", "Aim");
    }

            int t = i;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                SimPlayer target = t % 2 == 0 ? b : c;

            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);

    @Test
    void criticals() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("Crits", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("VictimCr", 0.5, 64, 2.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                face(pl, body(b));
                if (t % 5 == 0) {
                    // packet criticals: tiny hop packets, then the hit counts as critical while standing
                    pl.sendRawPosition(pl.sx, pl.sy + 0.0625, pl.sz, pl.yaw, pl.pitch, false);
                    pl.sendRawPosition(pl.sx, 64.0, pl.sz, pl.yaw, pl.pitch, false);
                    sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                    crit(sim, pl, b);
                }
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaught(sim, a, "Packet criticals", "Criticals");
    }

    private static void crit(Sim sim, SimPlayer attacker, SimPlayer victim) {
        Map<EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier, Double> modifiers = new EnumMap<>(EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier.class);
        modifiers.put(EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier.BASE, 7.0);
        Map<EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier, Function<? super Double, Double>> functions = new EnumMap<>(EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier.class);
        functions.put(EntityDamageEvent.DamageModifier.BASE, d -> 0.0);
        sim.call(new EntityDamageByEntityEvent(attacker.player, victim.player, EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.ENTITY_ATTACK,
                Sim.mock(DamageSource.class), modifiers, functions, true));
    }

    @Test
    void antiKnockback() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("AntiKB", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int hit = 0; hit < 5; hit++) {
            sim.velocity(p, new Vector(0.3, 0.4, 0.0)); // the client ignores it
            sim.idle(30);
        }
        assertCaught(sim, p, "Velocity / anti knockback", "Velocity");
    }

    @Test
    void crystalAuraPredict() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("CrystalAura", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.world.set(0, 64, 3, Material.OBSIDIAN);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int round = 0; round < 5; round++) {
            Block obsidian = sim.world.block(0, 64, 3);
            EnderCrystal crystal = sim.crystal(obsidian);
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                face(pl, new Vector(0.5, 65.5, 3.5));
                sim.placeCrystal(pl, obsidian, crystal);
                sim.attack(pl, crystal); // hits the crystal before the server even sent it
                sim.swing(pl);
            });
            sim.idle(4);
        }
        assertCaught(sim, p, "Crystal aura (ID predict / instant break)", "CrystalAura");
    }

    @Test
    void silentSwap() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("SilentSwap", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("VictimSw", 0.5, 64, 2.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                face(pl, body(b));
                if (t % 5 == 0) {
                    sim.slot(pl, 3);           // swap to the axe / mace
                    sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                    sim.slot(pl, 0);           // and back in the same tick
                }
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaught(sim, a, "Silent swap (switch, hit, switch back)", "SilentSwap");
    }

    @Test
    void autoClickerPattern() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("Clicker12", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("VictimC12", 0.5, 64, 2.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        double[] next = {0.0};
        for (int i = 0; i < 700; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                face(pl, body(b));
                while (next[0] <= t * 50.0) {
                    next[0] += 80.0; // 12.5 CPS, fixed delay
                    sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertCaught(sim, a, "Auto clicker 12.5 CPS fixed delay", "AutoClicker");
    }

    @Test
    void scaffoldAirPlace() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("AirPlace", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {

                sim.place(pl, sim.world.block(0, 66 + t, 3), BlockFace.UP, Material.STONE);

    /** The floor ends at z = 3: from there a bridge has to be built over the void. */
    private static Sim voidBridge() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        sim.world.fill(-60, 63, 3, 60, 63, 60, Material.AIR);
        return sim;
    }

    /** Center of the south face of the bridge block the next block is placed against. */
    private static Vector bridgeFace(int nextZ) {
        return new Vector(0.5, 63.5, nextZ);
    }

    @Test
    void scaffoldSilentRotations() {
        // Meteor / LiquidBounce style: the regular movement packet carries a rotation looking down
        // at the bridge while the player looks ahead, and the block is placed after that packet
        Sim sim = voidBridge();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Scaffold", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        int nextZ = 3;
        for (int i = 0; i < 80 && nextZ < 12; i++) {
            p.yaw = 0;
            p.pitch = 10;
            p.forward = 1;
            p.physicsTick();
            double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(p.eyeClient(), bridgeFace(nextZ));
            p.silentYaw = (float) r[0];
            p.silentPitch = (float) r[1];
            p.sendMovement();
            if (p.sz > nextZ + 0.05) {
                sim.place(p, sim.world.block(0, 63, nextZ - 1), BlockFace.SOUTH, Material.STONE);
                sim.swing(p);
                nextZ++;
            }
            sim.tickEnd(p);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertTrue(nextZ >= 10, "the bridge should have been built (nextZ=" + nextZ + ")");
        assertCaughtType(sim, p, "Scaffold with silent rotations (place after the movement packet)", "Scaffold", "Post");
    }

    @Test
    void scaffoldRotateBack() {
        // "instant" rotations: an extra rotation packet, the place, then the regular movement
        // packet turns back to where the player really looks
        Sim sim = voidBridge();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("ScaffoldRB", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        int nextZ = 3;
        for (int i = 0; i < 80 && nextZ < 12; i++) {
            p.yaw = 0;
            p.pitch = 10;
            p.forward = 1;
            if (p.sz > nextZ + 0.05) {
                double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(p.eyeServer(), bridgeFace(nextZ));
                p.sendRawPosition(p.sx, p.sy, p.sz, (float) r[0], (float) r[1], true);
                sim.place(p, sim.world.block(0, 63, nextZ - 1), BlockFace.SOUTH, Material.STONE);
                sim.swing(p);
                nextZ++;
            }
            p.physicsTick();
            p.sendMovement();
            sim.tickEnd(p);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertTrue(nextZ >= 10, "the bridge should have been built (nextZ=" + nextZ + ")");
        assertCaughtType(sim, p, "Scaffold rotate-place-rotate back (extra rotation packet)", "BadPackets", "Multi");
    }

    @Test
    void fastPlace() {

    @Test
```
