```java
    // ================================================================ watch mode vs block mode

    @Test
    void watchModeNeverBlocks() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        sim.settings.mitigation = false;
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("WatchReach", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("WatchVictim", 0.5, 64, 5.0);
        sim.idle(5);
        boolean[] cancelled = {false};
        for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                face(pl, body(b));
                PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event = sim.call(new PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent(pl.player, b.player, true));
                cancelled[0] |= event.isCancelled();
                sim.swing(pl);
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        boolean flagged = sim.flagged(a, "Reach");
        Report.add("MODE", "Watch mode: reach is flagged but the hits are NOT cancelled", sim.summary(a), flagged && !cancelled[0]);
        assertTrue(flagged);
        assertFalse(cancelled[0], "watch mode must not cancel anything");
    }

    @Test
    void blockModeCancelsAndSetsBack() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        sim.settings.mitigation = true;
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("BlockReach2", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("BlockVictim", 0.5, 64, 5.0);
        sim.idle(5);
        boolean[] cancelled = {false};
        for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                face(pl, body(b));
                PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event = sim.call(new PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent(pl.player, b.player, true));
                cancelled[0] |= event.isCancelled();
                sim.swing(pl);
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        SimPlayer f = sim.join("BlockFly", 10.5, 64, 10.5);
        sim.idle(5);
        double highest = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == f) {
                    pl.cheatFlyY = 0.3;
                }
            });
            highest = Math.max(highest, f.sy);
        }
        boolean setBack = f.sy < 70;
        Report.add("MODE", "Block mode (mitigation: true): reach hits cancelled, flyer pulled back down", sim.summary(f),
                cancelled[0] && setBack);
        assertTrue(cancelled[0], "block mode should cancel reach hits");
        assertTrue(setBack, "block mode should set the flyer back (y=" + f.sy + ", highest " + highest + ")");
    }

    @Test
    void legitHumanBeatsAuraFight() {
        // a human vs a silent-rotation aura player in the same fight: only the aura user is flagged
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer human = sim.join("HonestPlayer", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer cheater = sim.join("AuraUser", 0.5, 64, 3.5);
        Human h = new Human(human, 10, 99);
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
            int t = i;
            h.record(cheater);
            sim.clientTick(human, pl -> {
                h.applyVelocity();
                h.aim(cheater);
                h.move(cheater);
                for (int c = h.clicksThisTick(); c > 0; c--) {
                    h.click(sim, cheater);
                }
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(cheater, pl -> {
                pl.yaw = 180 + (t % 40);
                pl.forward = 0;
            }, t % 3 == 0 ? pl -> {
                double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(pl.eyeServer(), body(human));
                pl.sendRawPosition(pl.sx, pl.sy, pl.sz, (float) r[0], (float) r[1], true);
                sim.attack(pl, human.player);
                sim.swing(pl);
            } : null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        boolean ok = sim.flags(human).isEmpty() && sim.flagged(cheater, "KillAura");
        Report.add("MIXED", "Human vs aura user in one fight: only the aura user flagged",
                "human: " + sim.summary(human) + " / aura: " + sim.summary(cheater), ok);
        assertTrue(sim.flags(human).isEmpty(), "human flagged: " + sim.summary(human));
        assertTrue(sim.flagged(cheater, "KillAura"), sim.summary(cheater));
        // keep the location import used
        assertTrue(new Location(sim.world.world, 0, 0, 0).getWorld() != null);
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/CombatMacroTest.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.Sim;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.SimPlayer;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.Random;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Weapon swap macros and mace exploits of 1.21 cheat clients, with blocking on (default): the
 * cheater must be flagged with the right name AND his hits must stop registering. Legit fast
 * players doing the same tricks by hand must not be flagged or blocked.
 */
class CombatMacroTest {

    private static final int SWORD = 0;
    private static final int MACE = 1;
    private static final int AXE = 2;

    private static Sim arena() {
        return new Sim().floor(30);
    }

    private static SimPlayer fighter(Sim sim, String name) {
        SimPlayer p = sim.join(name, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.hotbar[SWORD] = Material.NETHERITE_SWORD;
        p.hotbar[MACE] = Material.MACE;
        p.hotbar[AXE] = Material.NETHERITE_AXE;
        p.slot = SWORD;
        return p;
    }

    /** Looks straight at the victim standing 2.2 blocks in front (yaw 0 = +z). */
    private static SimPlayer victim(Sim sim, String name) {
        return sim.join(name, 0.5, 64, 2.7);
    }

    private static void aim(SimPlayer attacker, SimPlayer victim) {
        double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(attacker.eyeClient(), new Vector(victim.sx, victim.sy + 1.2, victim.sz));
        attacker.yaw = (float) r[0];
        attacker.pitch = (float) r[1];
    }

    private static void report(Sim sim, SimPlayer cheater, String scenario, boolean ok) {
        Report.add("CHEAT", scenario, sim.summary(cheater), ok);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ cheats

    @Test
    void autoAttributeSwapIsCaughtAndHitsStopCounting() {
        // Meteor style: swap to the mace in the same tick as the hit, swap back 2 ticks later
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = fighter(sim, "AttrSwap");
        SimPlayer v = victim(sim, "AttrVictim");
        sim.idle(5);
        int[] registered = {0, 0};
        int flaggedAt = -1;
        for (int hit = 0; hit < 40; hit++) {
            int h = hit;
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    sim.slot(pl, MACE);
                    if (sim.attack(pl, v.player)) {
                        registered[h < 20 ? 0 : 1]++;
                    }
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
            sim.idle(2);
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    sim.slot(pl, SWORD);
                }
            });
            sim.idle(9);
            if (flaggedAt < 0 && sim.flagged(a, "SilentSwap", "AttributeSwap")) {
                flaggedAt = hit;
            }
        }
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(a, "SilentSwap", "AttributeSwap");
        report(sim, a, "Auto attribute swap (swap in the hit tick, swap back 2 ticks later), hits blocked after "
                + flaggedAt + " hits, last 20 hits registered " + registered[1], caught && registered[1] == 0);
        assertTrue(caught, sim.summary(a));
        assertEquals(0, registered[1], "hits after the detection must not register");
    }

    @Test
    void switchHitSwitchBackInOneTickIsBlockedAtOnce() {
        // swap to the mace, hit, swap back - all in one tick (impossible for vanilla)
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = fighter(sim, "SameTickSwap");
        SimPlayer v = victim(sim, "SameTickVictim");
        sim.idle(5);
        int cancelledSwaps = 0;
        int secondHits = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            int[] result = {0, 0};
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    sim.attack(pl, v.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                    if (!sim.slot(pl, MACE)) {
                        result[0]++;
                    }
                    if (sim.attack(pl, v.player)) {
                        result[1]++;
                    }
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
            cancelledSwaps += result[0];
            secondHits += result[1];
            sim.idle(10);
        }
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(a, "SilentSwap");
        report(sim, a, "Silent swap in one tick: swaps cancelled " + cancelledSwaps + "/10, mace hits registered " + secondHits,
                caught && cancelledSwaps == 10 && secondHits == 0);
        assertTrue(caught, sim.summary(a));
        assertEquals(10, cancelledSwaps, "the impossible swap is cancelled");
        assertEquals(0, secondHits, "hits after the impossible swap do not register");
    }

    @Test
    void autoStunSlamSameTickIsBlocked() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = fighter(sim, "StunSlammer");
        SimPlayer v = victim(sim, "ShieldUser");
        v.blocking = true;
        sim.idle(5);
        int maceHits = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            int[] result = {0};
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    sim.slot(pl, AXE);
                    sim.attack(pl, v.player); // breaks the shield
                    sim.swing(pl);
                    sim.slot(pl, MACE);
                    if (sim.attack(pl, v.player)) {
                        result[0]++;
                    }
                    sim.swing(pl);
                    sim.slot(pl, SWORD);
                }
            });
            maceHits += result[0];
            a.slot = SWORD;
            sim.idle(30);
        }
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(a, "SilentSwap", "StunSlam");
        report(sim, a, "Auto stun slam (axe + mace in one tick), mace hits registered " + maceHits + "/6", caught && maceHits == 0);
        assertTrue(caught, sim.summary(a));
        assertEquals(0, maceHits, "the mace smash after the impossible swap must not register");
    }

    @Test
    void autoStunSlamOneTickLaterIsAlerted() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = fighter(sim, "StunSlam1t");
        SimPlayer v = victim(sim, "ShieldUser2");
        v.blocking = true;
        sim.idle(5);
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    sim.slot(pl, AXE);
                    sim.attack(pl, v.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    sim.slot(pl, MACE);
                    sim.attack(pl, v.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
            sim.idle(2);
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    sim.slot(pl, SWORD);
                }
            });
            sim.idle(25);
        }
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(a, "SilentSwap", "StunSlam");
        report(sim, a, "Auto stun slam (mace exactly 1 tick after the axe, every time)", caught);
        assertTrue(caught, sim.summary(a));
    }

    @Test
    void maceKillFakeFallIsCancelled() {
        // up and down position packets right before the hit: the server thinks he fell 10 blocks
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = fighter(sim, "MaceKill");
        SimPlayer v = victim(sim, "MaceVictim");
        a.slot = MACE;
        sim.idle(5);
        int registered = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            int[] result = {0};
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    pl.sendRawPosition(pl.sx, pl.sy + 10.0, pl.sz, pl.yaw, pl.pitch, false);
                    pl.sendRawPosition(pl.sx, pl.sy - 10.0, pl.sz, pl.yaw, pl.pitch, false);
                    if (sim.attack(pl, v.player)) {
                        result[0]++;
                    }
                    sim.swing(pl);
                    pl.sFall = 0.0; // the server resets it when the smash lands
                }
            });
            registered += result[0];
            sim.idle(20);
        }
        boolean caught = sim.flagged(a, "Mace", "FakeFall");
        report(sim, a, "Mace kill (fake 10 block fall before the hit), smashes registered " + registered + "/6",
                caught && registered == 0);
        assertTrue(caught, sim.summary(a));
        assertEquals(0, registered, "fake fall smashes must not register");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ legit

    @Test
    void legitMaceSmashFromAPillar() {
        Sim sim = arena();
        sim.world.fill(0, 64, 0, 0, 72, 0, Material.STONE);
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("MaceLegit", 0.5, 73, 0.5);
        a.hotbar[MACE] = Material.MACE;
        a.slot = MACE;
        SimPlayer v = sim.join("MaceTarget", 0.5, 64, 3.2);
        sim.idle(5);
        boolean[] hit = {false};
        boolean[] registered = {false};
        for (int t = 0; t < 120 && !hit[0]; t++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl != a) {
                    return;
                }
                pl.forward = 1; // walks off the pillar towards the target
                aim(pl, v);
                pl.pitch = Math.max(pl.pitch, 10.0F);
                if (!pl.onGround && pl.sFall > 3.0 && pl.eyeServer().distance(new Vector(v.sx, v.sy + 1.0, v.sz)) < 3.0) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    hit[0] = true;
                    registered[0] = sim.attack(pl, v.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
        }
        sim.idle(20);
        String summary = sim.summary(a);
        boolean ok = hit[0] && registered[0] && sim.flags(a).isEmpty();
        Report.add("LEGIT", "mace smash after falling off a 9 block pillar", summary + (hit[0] ? "" : " (no hit)"), ok);
        assertTrue(hit[0], "the attacker should have reached the target");
        assertTrue(registered[0], "a real smash must register -> " + summary);
        assertTrue(sim.flags(a).isEmpty(), summary);
    }

    @Test
    void legitManualAttributeSwapping() {
        // a human: the swap lands in the same tick as the click about half of the time and the
        // swap back comes after 2-7 ticks
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = fighter(sim, "HandSwapper");
        SimPlayer v = victim(sim, "HandVictim");
        Random random = new Random(7);
        sim.idle(5);
        int cancelled = 0;
        for (int hit = 0; hit < 60; hit++) {
            boolean sameTick = random.nextDouble() < 0.5;
            if (!sameTick) {
                sim.tick(pl -> {
                    if (pl == a) {
                        sim.slot(pl, MACE);
                    }
                });
            }
            int[] result = {0};
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    if (sameTick) {
                        sim.slot(pl, MACE);
                    }
                    if (!sim.attack(pl, v.player)) {
                        result[0]++;
                    }
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
            cancelled += result[0];
            sim.idle(2 + random.nextInt(6));
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    sim.slot(pl, SWORD);
                }
            });
            sim.idle(6 + random.nextInt(8));
        }
        boolean ok = sim.flags(a).isEmpty() && cancelled == 0;
        Report.add("LEGIT", "attribute swapping by hand (60 hits)", sim.summary(a) + " cancelled=" + cancelled, ok);
        assertTrue(sim.flags(a).isEmpty(), sim.summary(a));
        assertEquals(0, cancelled);
    }

    @Test
    void legitFastStunSlams() {
        // a very fast player: mace hit 1-3 ticks after the axe broke the shield
        Sim sim = arena();
        SimPlayer a = fighter(sim, "FastSlammer");
        SimPlayer v = victim(sim, "ShieldUser3");
        v.blocking = true;
        Random random = new Random(11);
        sim.idle(5);
        int cancelled = 0;
        for (int combo = 0; combo < 30; combo++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    aim(pl, v);
                    sim.slot(pl, AXE);
                    sim.attack(pl, v.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
            double r = random.nextDouble();
            int delay = r < 0.4 ? 1 : r < 0.8 ? 2 : 3;
            sim.idle(delay - 1);
            int[] result = {0};
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    sim.slot(pl, MACE);
                    if (!sim.attack(pl, v.player)) {
                        result[0]++;
                    }
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
            cancelled += result[0];
            sim.idle(3 + random.nextInt(4));
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (pl == a) {
                    sim.slot(pl, SWORD);
                }
            });
            sim.idle(20 + random.nextInt(20));
        }
        boolean ok = sim.flags(a).isEmpty() && cancelled == 0;
        Report.add("LEGIT", "fast stun slams by hand (30 combos, 1-3 ticks)", sim.summary(a) + " cancelled=" + cancelled, ok);
        assertTrue(sim.flags(a).isEmpty(), sim.summary(a));
        assertEquals(0, cancelled);
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/LegitPlayerTest.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.Human;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.Sim;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim.SimPlayer;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;
import org.bukkit.entity.EnderCrystal;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.ClickType;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryAction;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;

import java.util.Random;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Normal players doing normal things. Astral must not flag any of them.
 */
class LegitPlayerTest {

    private static void assertClean(Sim sim, SimPlayer player, String scenario) {
        String summary = sim.summary(player);
        Report.add("LEGIT", scenario, summary, sim.flags(player).isEmpty());
        assertTrue(sim.flags(player).isEmpty(), scenario + " -> " + summary);
    }

    /** A world with slabs, 1 and 2 block walls, a pit and an ice tunnel. */
    private static Sim terrain() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(40);
        var w = sim.world;
        // a fence around the arena so nobody walks off the edge
        w.fill(-40, 64, -40, 40, 66, -40, Material.STONE);
        w.fill(-40, 64, 40, 40, 66, 40, Material.STONE);
        w.fill(-40, 64, -40, -40, 66, 40, Material.STONE);
        w.fill(40, 64, -40, 40, 66, 40, Material.STONE);
        w.fill(4, 64, -6, 8, 64, -2, Material.STONE_SLAB);          // slabs to step on
        w.fill(-8, 64, 4, -8, 64, 12, Material.STONE);              // 1 block wall
        w.fill(10, 64, 10, 14, 65, 10, Material.STONE);             // 2 block wall
        w.fill(-14, 63, -14, -10, 63, -10, Material.AIR);           // pit
        w.fill(-14, 59, -14, -10, 59, -10, Material.STONE);         // pit floor (3 deep)
        w.fill(15, 63, -10, 25, 63, -6, Material.PACKED_ICE);       // ice tunnel floor
        w.fill(15, 66, -10, 25, 66, -6, Material.STONE);            // ice tunnel ceiling (2 high)
        w.fill(0, 64, 15, 2, 66, 17, Material.STONE);               // pillar
        return sim;
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "random wandering seed {0}")
    @ValueSource(longs = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5})
    void wanderingOnTerrain(long seed) {
        Sim sim = terrain();
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Wanderer" + seed, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        Random random = new Random(seed);
        int[] change = {0};
        sim.idle(3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 4000; i++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (--change[0] <= 0) {
                    change[0] = 5 + random.nextInt(40);
                    pl.forward = random.nextInt(4) == 0 ? 0 : (random.nextInt(6) == 0 ? -1 : 1);
                    pl.strafe = random.nextInt(3) - 1;
                    pl.sneaking = random.nextInt(10) == 0;
                    pl.sprinting = pl.forward > 0 && !pl.sneaking && random.nextBoolean();
                    pl.jump = random.nextInt(3) == 0;
                }
                if (random.nextInt(4) == 0) {
                    pl.mouseTurn(random.nextInt(81) - 40, random.nextInt(21) - 10);
                }
                // walk back to the middle when far away
                if (Math.hypot(pl.x, pl.z) > 32) {
                    double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(new Vector(pl.x, 0, pl.z), new Vector(0, 0, 0));
                    pl.yaw = (float) r[0];
                    pl.forward = 1;
                }
            });
        }
        assertClean(sim, p, "random wandering (seed " + seed + ")");
    }

    @Test
    void bunnyHopping() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(60);
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Bhop", 0.5, 64, -50.5);
        sim.idle(3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 600; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                pl.forward = 1;
                pl.sprinting = true;
                pl.jump = true;
                if (t % 40 == 0) {
                    pl.mouseTurn(30, 0);
                }
            });
        }
        assertClean(sim, p, "sprint jumping non stop");
    }

    @Test
    void iceTunnelBoosting() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(60);
        sim.world.fill(-2, 63, -50, 2, 63, 50, Material.PACKED_ICE);
        sim.world.fill(-2, 66, -50, 2, 66, 50, Material.STONE);
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("IceRunner", 0.5, 64, -45.5);
        sim.idle(3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                pl.forward = 1;
                pl.sprinting = true;
                pl.jump = true;
            });
        }
        assertClean(sim, p, "ice tunnel jump boosting (" + String.format("%.2f", p.z + 45.5) + " blocks)");
    }

    @Test
    void longFall() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(20);
        sim.world.fill(-1, 90, -1, 1, 90, 1, Material.STONE);
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Faller", 0.5, 91, 0.5);
        sim.idle(3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
            sim.tick(pl -> pl.forward = 1);
        }
        assertClean(sim, p, "walking off a 26 block tower");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "pvp fight seed {0}")
    @ValueSource(longs = {11, 12, 13})
    void humanPvpFight(long seed) {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(60);
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("FighterA" + seed, 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("FighterB" + seed, 0.5, 64, 3.5);
        a.yaw = 0;
        b.yaw = 180;
        Human ha = new Human(a, 9 + seed % 4, seed);
        Human hb = new Human(b, 8 + seed % 3, seed * 7);
        sim.idle(3);
        fight(sim, ha, hb, 1500);
        Report.note("pvp seed " + seed + ": A hits=" + ha.hits + " misses=" + ha.misses + ", B hits=" + hb.hits + " misses=" + hb.misses);
        assertClean(sim, a, "human PvP fight, attacker (seed " + seed + ")");
        assertClean(sim, b, "human PvP fight, defender (seed " + seed + ")");
    }

    /** Two humans fighting: mouse aim, human clicks, strafing, jumps, knockback, hotbar switches. */
    static void fight(Sim sim, Human ha, Human hb, int ticks) {
        Human[] humans = {ha, hb};
        for (int i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
            for (Human h : humans) {
                Human other = h == ha ? hb : ha;
                h.record(other.self);
            }
            for (Human h : humans) {
                Human other = h == ha ? hb : ha;
                SimPlayer self = h.self;
                sim.clientTick(self, pl -> {
                    h.applyVelocity();
                    if (h.random().nextDouble() < 0.01) {
                        sim.slot(pl, h.random().nextInt(9));
                    }
                    h.aim(other.self);
                    h.move(other.self);
                    int clicks = h.clicksThisTick();
                    for (int c = 0; c < clicks; c++) {
                        if (h.click(sim, other.self) && other.hurtTime <= 0) {
                            other.hurtTime = 10;
                            Vector direction = new Vector(other.self.sx - pl.sx, 0, other.self.sz - pl.sz).normalize().multiply(0.4);
                            Vector kb = new Vector(other.self.mx / 2 + direction.getX(), other.self.sOnGround ? 0.4 : Math.min(0.0, other.self.my),
                                    other.self.mz / 2 + direction.getZ());
                            sim.velocity(other.self, kb);
                            other.queueVelocity(kb, 2);
                        }
                    }
                }, null);
            }
            for (Human h : humans) {
                h.hurtTime--;
            }
            sim.serverTick();
        }
    }

    @Test
    void inventoryUseStandingStill() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(10);
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Organizer", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.idle(3);
        p.releaseKeys();
        for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (t % 7 == 0) {
                    sim.click(pl, 9 + t % 27, ClickType.LEFT, InventoryAction.PICKUP_ALL, SimPlayer.item(Material.DIRT), null);
                }
            });
        }
        // totem pop, then the human opens the inventory and moves a totem after ~0.6s
        sim.totemPop(p);
        sim.idle(12);
        sim.click(p, 20, ClickType.SWAP_OFFHAND, InventoryAction.HOTBAR_SWAP, SimPlayer.item(Material.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING), null);
        sim.idle(5);
        assertClean(sim, p, "inventory sorting + manual totem");
    }

    @Test
    void buildingAndMining() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(20);
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Builder", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        p.pitch = 60;
        sim.idle(3);
        // hold right click: vanilla places every 4 ticks. A small wall in front (top faces below the eyes)
        for (int i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                if (t % 4 == 0 && t / 4 < 10) {
                    int column = t / 4 % 5 - 2;
                    int level = t / 4 / 5;
                    Block against = sim.world.block(column, 63 + level, 2);
                    double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(pl.eyeClient(), new Vector(column + 0.5, 64 + level, 2.5));
                    pl.mouseAim(new Vector(column + 0.5, 64 + level, 2.5), 1.0, 0);
                    if (against.getType().isAir()) {
                        return;
                    }
                    sim.place(pl, against, BlockFace.UP, Material.OAK_PLANKS);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            });
        }
        // mine the wall back down at vanilla speed (20 ticks per block)
        for (int index = 9; index >= 0; index--) {
            Block block = sim.world.block(index % 5 - 2, 64 + index / 5, 2);
            if (block.getType().isAir()) {
                continue;
            }
            int ticks = 0;
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                pl.mouseAim(new Vector(block.getX() + 0.5, block.getY() + 0.5, 2.5), 1.0, 0);
                sim.startDig(pl, block);
                sim.swing(pl);
            });
            while (++ticks < 20) {
                sim.tick(pl -> sim.swing(pl));
            }
            sim.tick(pl -> sim.breakBlock(pl, block));
            sim.idle(5);
        }
        assertClean(sim, p, "building a wall and mining it");
    }

    @Test
    void crystalPvpLegit() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(20);
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("CrystalLegit", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        sim.world.set(0, 64, 3, Material.OBSIDIAN);
        sim.idle(3);
        for (int round = 0; round < 10; round++) {
            Block obsidian = sim.world.block(0, 64, 3);
            EnderCrystal crystal = sim.crystal(obsidian);
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(pl.eyeClient(), new Vector(0.5, 65.5, 3.5));
                pl.yaw = (float) r[0];
                pl.pitch = (float) r[1];
                sim.placeCrystal(pl, obsidian, crystal);
                sim.swing(pl);
            });
            sim.idle(3); // the crystal arrives at the client after its ping
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                sim.attack(pl, crystal);
                sim.swing(pl);
            });
            sim.idle(8);
        }
        assertClean(sim, p, "crystal PvP at human speed");
    }

    @Test
    void eatingWhileWalking() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(60);
        SimPlayer p = sim.join("Eater", 0.5, 64, -40.5);
        p.mainHand = Material.COOKED_BEEF;
        sim.idle(3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.tick(pl -> {
                pl.forward = 1;
                if (t % 60 == 0) {
                    pl.usingItem = true;
                    pl.useTicks = 0;
                    pl.sprinting = false;
                    sim.useItem(pl);
                } else if (pl.usingItem && pl.useTicks >= 32) {
                    pl.usingItem = false;
                } else if (!pl.usingItem) {
                    pl.sprinting = true;
                }
            });
        }
        assertClean(sim, p, "eating while walking");
    }

    @Test
    void scrollingHotbarAndHitting() {
        Sim sim = new Sim().floor(20);
        SimPlayer a = sim.join("Scroller", 0.5, 64, 0.5);
        SimPlayer b = sim.join("Dummy", 0.5, 64, 2.5);
        sim.idle(3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
            int t = i;
            sim.clientTick(a, pl -> {
                if (t % 3 == 0) {
                    sim.slot(pl, (pl.slot + 1) % 9); // vanilla sends the slot before the attack
                }
                double[] r = SimPlayer.rotationTo(pl.eyeClient(), new Vector(0.5, 65.2, 2.5));
                pl.mouseAim(new Vector(0.5, 65.2, 2.5), 1.0, 0);
                if (t % 3 == 0) {
                    sim.attack(pl, b.player);
                    sim.swing(pl);
                }
            }, null);
            sim.clientTick(b, null, null);
            sim.serverTick();
        }
        assertClean(sim, a, "switching hotbar slots then hitting");
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/Report.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;

/** Collects the result of every scenario into target/astral-test-report.md. */
final class Report {

    private static final Path FILE = Path.of("target", "astral-test-report.md");

    private Report() {
    }

    static synchronized void add(String kind, String scenario, String result, boolean passed) {
        write("| " + kind + " | " + scenario + " | " + (passed ? "PASS" : "FAIL") + " | " + result.replace("|", "/") + " |");
    }

    static synchronized void note(String text) {
        write("<!-- " + text + " -->");
    }

    private static void write(String line) {
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(FILE.getParent());
            if (!Files.exists(FILE)) {
                Files.writeString(FILE, "| Type | Scenario | Result | Astral output |\n|---|---|---|---|\n", StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            }
            Files.writeString(FILE, line + "\n", StandardCharsets.UTF_8, StandardOpenOption.APPEND);
        } catch (IOException ignored) {
            // report only
        }
        System.out.println(line);
    }
}
```

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral;
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/Human.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.Random;

/**
 * Human-like behaviour for a simulated player: mouse aiming with whole mouse counts, clicking
 * with human timing noise, strafing, jumping, taking knockback after its ping.
 */
public final class Human {

    public final SimPlayer self;
    private final Random random;
    private double nextClickMs;
    private double timeMs;
    private int missTime;
    private int strafeTicks;
    private final Deque<Vector[]> history = new ArrayDeque<>();
    private final Deque<Object[]> pendingVelocity = new ArrayDeque<>();
    public int hurtTime;
    public double clickMean;
    public double clickDeviation;
    public int hits;
    public int misses;

    public Human(SimPlayer self, double cps, long seed) {
        this.self = self;
        this.random = new Random(seed);
        this.clickMean = 1000.0 / cps;
        this.clickDeviation = this.clickMean * 0.22;
        this.nextClickMs = random.nextDouble() * clickMean;
    }

    /** Remembers the server box of a player every tick (what others see, a few ticks late). */
    public void record(SimPlayer target) {
        history.addLast(new Vector[]{target.serverBox().getMin(), target.serverBox().getMax()});
        while (history.size() > 6) {
            history.removeFirst();
        }
    }

    /** The target as this client sees it (3 ticks old: ping + entity interpolation). */
    public BoundingBox seen(SimPlayer target) {
        Vector[] old = history.peekFirst();
        if (old == null) {
            return target.serverBox();
        }
        return BoundingBox.of(old[0], old[1]);
    }

    public void queueVelocity(Vector velocity, int delayTicks) {
        pendingVelocity.addLast(new Object[]{velocity, delayTicks});
    }

    /** Applies knockback that reached the client. */
    public void applyVelocity() {
        for (Object[] entry : pendingVelocity) {
            entry[1] = (Integer) entry[1] - 1;
        }
        while (!pendingVelocity.isEmpty() && (Integer) pendingVelocity.peekFirst()[1] <= 0) {
            Vector v = (Vector) pendingVelocity.removeFirst()[0];
            self.mx = v.getX();
            self.my = v.getY();
            self.mz = v.getZ();
        }
    }

    /** Fighting movement: keep ~2.6 blocks distance, strafe, sometimes jump. */
    public void move(SimPlayer target) {
        double dx = target.x - self.x;
        double dz = target.z - self.z;
        double distance = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        self.forward = distance > 2.9 ? 1 : distance < 2.0 ? -1 : (random.nextDouble() < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
        if (--strafeTicks <= 0) {
            strafeTicks = 8 + random.nextInt(25);
            self.strafe = random.nextInt(3) - 1;
        }
        self.sprinting = self.forward > 0 && !self.sneaking && !self.usingItem;
        self.jump = self.onGround && random.nextDouble() < 0.04;
    }

    /** Aims with the mouse at a point on the target's (seen) body. */
    public void aim(SimPlayer target) {
        BoundingBox box = seen(target);
        Vector point = new Vector(box.getCenterX() + (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.3,
                box.getMinY() + 1.0 + random.nextDouble() * 0.6, box.getCenterZ() + (random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.3);
        self.mouseAim(point, 0.35 + random.nextDouble() * 0.35, 3);
    }

    /** True when the crosshair is on the target (what the client's pick would see). */
    public boolean crosshairOn(SimPlayer target) {
        Vector eye = self.eyeClient();
        Vector direction = me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil.direction(self.yaw, self.pitch);
        BoundingBox box = seen(target);
        return box.rayTrace(eye, direction, 3.0) != null || box.contains(eye);
    }

    /**
     * Number of clicks that happened since the last tick (human timing). Clicks during the miss
     * penalty are swallowed like in vanilla.
     */
    public int clicksThisTick() {
        timeMs += 50.0;
        int clicks = 0;
        while (nextClickMs <= timeMs) {
            clicks++;
            double interval = clickMean + random.nextGaussian() * clickDeviation;
            if (random.nextDouble() < 0.06) {
                interval += 150 + random.nextDouble() * 400; // short pauses
            }
            nextClickMs += Math.max(25.0, interval);
        }
        if (missTime > 0) {
            missTime--;
            return 0;
        }
        return clicks;
    }

    /** Vanilla handleKeybinds for the attack key. Returns true if the target got hit. */
    public boolean click(Sim sim, SimPlayer target) {
        if (missTime > 0) {
            return false;
        }
        if (crosshairOn(target)) {
            boolean hit = sim.attack(self, target.player);
            sim.swing(self);
            hits++;
            return hit;
        }
        sim.swing(self);
        misses++;
        missTime = 10;
        return false;
    }

    public Random random() {
        return random;
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/Sim.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import com.destroystokyo.paper.event.server.ServerTickEndEvent;
import io.papermc.paper.event.packet.ClientTickEndEvent;
import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PlayerArmSwingEvent;
import io.papermc.paper.event.player.PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.AstralAntiCheat;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.Check;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.check.CheckManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config.Messages;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.config.Settings;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.FlagRecord;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerData;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.data.PlayerDataManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.DataListener;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.InteractionListener;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.listener.MovementProcessor;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.AlertManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.BypassManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.FreezeManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.LogManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.manager.ViolationManager;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.Clock;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockState;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.entity.EnderCrystal;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.EntityType;
import org.bukkit.event.Cancellable;
import org.bukkit.event.Event;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDismountEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityMountEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityResurrectEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityToggleGlideEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.ClickType;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryAction;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryType;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemHeldEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerVelocityEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.EquipmentSlot;
import org.bukkit.inventory.Inventory;
import org.bukkit.inventory.InventoryView;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.PlayerInventory;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.mockito.Mockito;
import org.mockito.stubbing.Answer;

import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/**
 * Runs the real Astral code (data, listeners, every check, violation manager) against simulated
 * players. Events are dispatched like Bukkit does (priority order, ignoreCancelled).
 */
public final class Sim {

    private static Sim current;

    public final AstralAntiCheat plugin;
    public final Settings settings = new Settings();
    public final SimWorld world;
    public final List<SimPlayer> players = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<Handler> handlers = new ArrayList<>();
    private long nanos = 1_000_000_000L;

    private record Handler(Object listener, Method method, Class<?> type, EventPriority priority, boolean ignoreCancelled, int order) {
    }

    public static Sim current() {
        return current;
    }

    /**
     * Creates a stub-only mock owned by the current simulation. Inline mocks keep a strong
     * reference to themselves (last invocation), so they are released explicitly in
     * {@link #close()} instead of piling up across tests.
     */
    public static <T> T mock(Class<T> type) {
        return track(Mockito.mock(type, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly()));
    }

    public static <T> T mock(Class<T> type, Answer<Object> answer) {
        return track(Mockito.mock(type, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly().defaultAnswer(answer)));
    }

    private static final List<Object> MOCKS = new ArrayList<>();

    static synchronized <T> T track(T mock) {
        MOCKS.add(mock);
        return mock;
    }

    /** Releases every mock created for this simulation so the whole world can be collected. */
    public void close() {
        synchronized (Sim.class) {
            for (Object mock : MOCKS) {
                Mockito.framework().clearInlineMock(mock);
            }
            MOCKS.clear();
        }
        handlers.clear();
        players.clear();
        world.clear();
        SimServer.ONLINE.clear();
        if (current == this) {
            current = null;
        }
    }

    public Sim() {
        if (current != null) {
            current.close();
        }
        world = new SimWorld();
        SimServer.init();
        SimServer.ONLINE.clear();
        // initialise registry backed classes before any Mockito stubbing uses them
        InventoryType.values();
        InventoryType.SlotType.values();
        EntityType.values();
        ClickType.values();
        InventoryAction.values();
        Material.STONE.isAir();
        current = this;
        Clock.use(() -> nanos);

        YamlConfiguration config = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(new InputStreamReader(
                Sim.class.getResourceAsStream("/config.yml"), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        settings.load(config);
        settings.joinExemptTicks = 0;
        settings.logFile = false;

        plugin = mock(AstralAntiCheat.class);
        when(plugin.settings()).thenReturn(settings);
        when(plugin.getConfig()).thenReturn(config);
        when(plugin.getLogger()).thenReturn(Logger.getLogger("Astral-Sim"));
        when(plugin.messages()).thenReturn(mock(Messages.class));
        when(plugin.logs()).thenReturn(mock(LogManager.class));
        when(plugin.alerts()).thenReturn(mock(AlertManager.class));
        when(plugin.bypass()).thenReturn(mock(BypassManager.class));
        when(plugin.freeze()).thenReturn(mock(FreezeManager.class));
        PlayerDataManager data = new PlayerDataManager(plugin);
        when(plugin.data()).thenReturn(data);
        ViolationManager violations = new ViolationManager(plugin);
        when(plugin.violations()).thenReturn(violations);
        CheckManager checks = new CheckManager(plugin);
        when(plugin.checks()).thenReturn(checks);
        checks.registerAll();

        // same registration order as the plugin: checks first, then the listeners
        for (Check check : checks.all()) {
            register(check);
        }
        register(new DataListener(plugin));
        register(new MovementProcessor(plugin));
        register(new InteractionListener(plugin));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ events

    private void register(Listener listener) {
        for (Method method : listener.getClass().getMethods()) {
            EventHandler annotation = method.getAnnotation(EventHandler.class);
            if (annotation == null || method.getParameterCount() != 1) {
                continue;
            }
            method.setAccessible(true);
            handlers.add(new Handler(listener, method, method.getParameterTypes()[0], annotation.priority(),
                    annotation.ignoreCancelled(), handlers.size()));
        }
        handlers.sort(Comparator.comparingInt((Handler h) -> h.priority().getSlot()).thenComparingInt(Handler::order));
    }

    public <T extends Event> T call(T event) {
        for (Handler handler : handlers) {
            if (!handler.type().isInstance(event)) {
                continue;
            }
            if (handler.ignoreCancelled() && event instanceof Cancellable cancellable && cancellable.isCancelled()) {
                continue;
            }
            try {
                handler.method().invoke(handler.listener(), event);
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Handler " + handler.method() + " failed", e.getCause() != null ? e.getCause() : e);
            }
        }
        return event;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ world / players

    /** Flat stone floor, top surface at y = 64. */
    public Sim floor(int radius) {
        world.fill(-radius, 63, -radius, radius, 63, radius, Material.STONE);
        return this;
    }

    public SimPlayer join(String name, double x, double y, double z) {
        SimPlayer player = new SimPlayer(world, name, x, y, z, name.hashCode());
        players.add(player);
        SimServer.ONLINE.add(player.player);
        call(new PlayerJoinEvent(player.player, Component.empty()));
        return player;
    }

    public PlayerData data(SimPlayer player) {
        return plugin.data().get(player.player);
    }

    /**
     * A movement packet was received: fires PlayerMoveEvent exactly when Paper does (moved more
     * than 1/16 block or turned more than 10 degrees since the last fired event).
     */
    void moved(SimPlayer player) {
        double dx = player.sx - player.eventX;
        double dy = player.sy - player.eventY;
        double dz = player.sz - player.eventZ;
        float angle = Math.abs(player.syaw - player.eventYaw) + Math.abs(player.spitch - player.eventPitch);
        if (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz <= 1.0 / 256.0 && angle <= 10.0F) {
            return;
        }
        Location from = new Location(world.world, player.eventX, player.eventY, player.eventZ, player.eventYaw, player.eventPitch);
        Location to = new Location(world.world, player.sx, player.sy, player.sz, player.syaw, player.spitch);
        player.eventX = player.sx;
        player.eventY = player.sy;
        player.eventZ = player.sz;
        player.eventYaw = player.syaw;
        player.eventPitch = player.spitch;
        call(new PlayerMoveEvent(player.player, from, to));
    }

    public void teleport(SimPlayer player, Location to) {
        Location from = new Location(world.world, player.sx, player.sy, player.sz);
        PlayerTeleportEvent event = new PlayerTeleportEvent(player.player, from, to, PlayerTeleportEvent.TeleportCause.PLUGIN);
        call(event);
        player.teleportTo(to);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ ticking

    /**
     * One tick for everybody: each client runs its tick (actions -> movement -> tick end), then
     * the server ticks.
     */
    public void tick(Consumer<SimPlayer> beforeMove) {
        tick(beforeMove, null);
    }

    public void tick(Consumer<SimPlayer> beforeMove, Consumer<SimPlayer> afterMove) {
        for (SimPlayer player : players) {
            clientTick(player, beforeMove, afterMove);
        }
        serverTick();
    }

    public void clientTick(SimPlayer player, Consumer<SimPlayer> beforeMove, Consumer<SimPlayer> afterMove) {
        if (beforeMove != null) {
            beforeMove.accept(player);
        }
        player.physicsTick();
        player.sendMovement();
        if (afterMove != null) {
            afterMove.accept(player);
        }
        tickEnd(player);
    }

    public void tickEnd(SimPlayer player) {
        call(new ClientTickEndEvent(player.player));
    }

    public void serverTick() {
        call(new ServerTickEndEvent(SimServer.tick, 50.0, 0L));
        SimServer.tick++;
        nanos += 50_000_000L;
        plugin.data().tick();
    }

    /** Advances only the real time clock (for timer tests). */
    public void advanceNanos(long amount) {
        nanos += amount;
    }

    public void idle(int ticks) {
        for (int i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
            tick(null);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ actions (packets)

    public boolean attack(SimPlayer attacker, Entity target) {
        PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent event = call(new PrePlayerAttackEntityEvent(attacker.player, target, true));
        return !event.isCancelled();
    }

    public void swing(SimPlayer player) {
        call(new PlayerArmSwingEvent(player.player, EquipmentSlot.HAND));
    }

    /** Hotbar slot change. Returns false when Astral cancelled it (the server keeps the old slot). */
    public boolean slot(SimPlayer player, int slot) {
        PlayerItemHeldEvent event = call(new PlayerItemHeldEvent(player.player, player.slot, slot));
        if (!event.isCancelled()) {
            player.slot = slot;
        }
        return !event.isCancelled();
    }

    public void useItem(SimPlayer player) {
        call(new PlayerInteractEvent(player.player, Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR, SimPlayer.item(player.mainHand), null, BlockFace.SELF, EquipmentSlot.HAND));
    }

    public void velocity(SimPlayer player, Vector velocity) {
        call(new PlayerVelocityEvent(player.player, velocity));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ minigame plugin actions

    /** A plugin puts the player on a vehicle (bus seat, boat ...). */
    public void mount(SimPlayer player, SimEntity vehicle) {
        call(new EntityMountEvent(player.player, vehicle.entity));
        player.vehicle = vehicle;
        vehicle.passengers.add(player.player);
        player.ride(vehicle.x, vehicle.y + vehicle.height, vehicle.z);
    }

    /** The player leaves the vehicle: Paper teleports him to the dismount position. */
    public void dismount(SimPlayer player, Location to) {
        SimEntity vehicle = player.vehicle;
        call(new EntityDismountEvent(player.player, vehicle.entity));
        vehicle.passengers.remove(player.player);
        player.vehicle = null;
        teleport(player, to);
    }

    public void glide(SimPlayer player, boolean gliding) {
        call(new EntityToggleGlideEvent(player.player, gliding));
        player.gliding = gliding;
    }

    /** A plugin adds / removes a potion effect: the client gets it half a round trip later. */
    public void effect(SimPlayer player, String key, boolean on) {
        if (on) {
            player.effects.add(key);
        } else {
            player.effects.remove(key);
        }
        player.later(player.halfRtt(), () -> {
            if (on) {
                player.clientEffects.add(key);
            } else {
                player.clientEffects.remove(key);
            }
        });
    }

    /** Entity#setGravity from a plugin (also reaches the client later). */
    public void gravity(SimPlayer player, boolean on) {
        player.gravity = on;
        player.later(player.halfRtt(), () -> player.clientGravity = on);
    }

    /** Player#setVelocity from a plugin: event now, the client applies it later. */
    public void push(SimPlayer player, Vector velocity) {
        call(new PlayerVelocityEvent(player.player, velocity.clone()));
        Vector v = velocity.clone();
        player.later(player.halfRtt(), () -> {
            player.mx = v.getX();
            player.my = v.getY();
            player.mz = v.getZ();
        });
    }

    public void gameMode(SimPlayer player, org.bukkit.GameMode mode) {
        call(new org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerGameModeChangeEvent(player.player, mode));
        player.gameMode = mode;
    }

    /** The client asks to start / stop flying (double jump). Returns false when a plugin cancelled it. */
    public boolean toggleFlight(SimPlayer player, boolean flying, boolean pluginCancels) {
        org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerToggleFlightEvent event = new org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerToggleFlightEvent(player.player, flying);
        event.setCancelled(pluginCancels); // the minigame's own listener runs before Astral's MONITOR one
        call(event);
        if (!event.isCancelled()) {
            player.sFlying = flying;
        }
        return !event.isCancelled();
    }

    public void totemPop(SimPlayer player) {
        call(new EntityResurrectEvent(player.player, EquipmentSlot.OFF_HAND));
    }

    public boolean place(SimPlayer player, Block against, BlockFace face, Material material) {
        Block placed = against.getRelative(face);
        call(new PlayerInteractEvent(player.player, Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK, SimPlayer.item(material), against, face, EquipmentSlot.HAND));
        BlockPlaceEvent event = new BlockPlaceEvent(placed, mock(BlockState.class), against, SimPlayer.item(material),
                player.player, true, EquipmentSlot.HAND);
        call(event);
        if (!event.isCancelled()) {
            world.set(placed.getX(), placed.getY(), placed.getZ(), material);
        }
        return !event.isCancelled();
    }

    public void startDig(SimPlayer player, Block block) {
        call(new PlayerInteractEvent(player.player, Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK, SimPlayer.item(player.mainHand), block, BlockFace.UP, EquipmentSlot.HAND));
        call(new BlockDamageEvent(player.player, block, SimPlayer.item(player.mainHand), false));
    }

    public boolean breakBlock(SimPlayer player, Block block) {
        BlockBreakEvent event = call(new BlockBreakEvent(block, player.player));
        if (!event.isCancelled()) {
            world.set(block.getX(), block.getY(), block.getZ(), Material.AIR);
        }
        return !event.isCancelled();
    }

    /** A click in the player's own inventory (or a container when top != null). */
    public InventoryClickEvent click(SimPlayer player, int slot, ClickType click, InventoryAction action, ItemStack current,
                                     InventoryType top) {
        InventoryView view = mock(InventoryView.class);
        Inventory topInventory = mock(Inventory.class);
        when(topInventory.getType()).thenReturn(top == null ? InventoryType.CRAFTING : top);
        PlayerInventory bottom = mock(PlayerInventory.class);
        when(bottom.getType()).thenReturn(InventoryType.PLAYER);
        when(view.getTopInventory()).thenReturn(topInventory);
        when(view.getBottomInventory()).thenReturn(bottom);
        when(view.getPlayer()).thenReturn(player.player);
        when(view.getItem(Mockito.anyInt())).thenReturn(current);
        boolean inTop = top != null && slot < 27;
        when(view.getInventory(Mockito.anyInt())).thenReturn(inTop ? topInventory : bottom);
        when(view.convertSlot(Mockito.anyInt())).thenReturn(slot);
        when(view.getSlotType(Mockito.anyInt())).thenReturn(InventoryType.SlotType.CONTAINER);
        return call(new InventoryClickEvent(view, InventoryType.SlotType.CONTAINER, slot, click, action));
    }

    public EnderCrystal crystal(Block on) {
        EnderCrystal crystal = mock(EnderCrystal.class);
        int id = 50_000 + world.entities.size();
        int bx = on.getX();
        int by = on.getY();
        int bz = on.getZ();
        BoundingBox box = new BoundingBox(bx - 0.5, by + 1, bz - 0.5, bx + 1.5, by + 3, bz + 1.5);
        Location location = new Location(world.world, bx + 0.5, by + 1, bz + 0.5);
        when(crystal.getEntityId()).thenReturn(id);
        when(crystal.getBoundingBox()).thenReturn(box);
        when(crystal.getWorld()).thenReturn(world.world);
        when(crystal.getType()).thenReturn(EntityType.END_CRYSTAL);
        when(crystal.getVelocity()).thenReturn(new Vector());
        when(crystal.getLocation()).thenReturn(location);
        world.entities.add(crystal);
        return crystal;
    }

    public void placeCrystal(SimPlayer player, Block on, EnderCrystal crystal) {
        call(new PlayerInteractEvent(player.player, Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK, SimPlayer.item(Material.END_CRYSTAL), on, BlockFace.UP, EquipmentSlot.HAND));
        call(new EntityPlaceEvent(crystal, player.player, on, BlockFace.UP, EquipmentSlot.HAND));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ results

    public List<FlagRecord> flags(SimPlayer player) {
        PlayerData data = data(player);
        return data == null ? List.of() : new ArrayList<>(data.flags);
    }

    public boolean flagged(SimPlayer player, String check) {
        return flags(player).stream().anyMatch(f -> f.check().equalsIgnoreCase(check));
    }

    public boolean flagged(SimPlayer player, String check, String type) {
        return flags(player).stream().anyMatch(f -> f.check().equalsIgnoreCase(check) && f.type().equalsIgnoreCase(type));
    }

    public String summary(SimPlayer player) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        List<FlagRecord> list = flags(player);
        java.util.Map<String, Integer> counts = new java.util.TreeMap<>();
        for (FlagRecord f : list) {
            counts.merge(f.check() + "(" + f.type() + ")", 1, Integer::sum);
        }
        counts.forEach((k, v) -> sb.append(k).append(" x").append(v).append("  "));
        if (!list.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append("| first: ").append(list.getLast().check()).append(" ").append(list.getLast().debug());
        }
        return sb.isEmpty() ? "no flags" : sb.toString();
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/SimBlocks.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import org.bukkit.Material;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;

/** Block shapes / properties used by the simulated world. */
public final class SimBlocks {

    public enum Shape {
        NONE, FULL, SLAB
    }

    private static final Set<String> AIR = Set.of("air", "cave_air", "void_air");
    private static final Set<String> SLABS = Set.of("stone_slab", "oak_slab", "smooth_stone_slab");
    private static final Set<String> NON_SOLID = Set.of("water", "lava", "ladder", "vine", "cobweb", "short_grass",
            "bubble_column", "kelp", "seagrass", "scaffolding");
    private static final Set<String> TRANSPARENT = Set.of("glass", "ice", "slime_block", "honey_block");

    private SimBlocks() {
    }

    private static String path(Material material) {
        return material.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }

    public static boolean isAir(String path) {
        return AIR.contains(path);
    }

    public static Shape shape(String path) {
        if (AIR.contains(path) || NON_SOLID.contains(path)) {
            return Shape.NONE;
        }
        if (SLABS.contains(path)) {
            return Shape.SLAB;
        }
        return Shape.FULL;
    }

    public static Shape shape(Material material) {
        return shape(path(material));
    }

    public static boolean isSolid(String path) {
        return shape(path) != Shape.NONE;
    }

    public static boolean isOccluding(String path) {
        return shape(path) == Shape.FULL && !TRANSPARENT.contains(path);
    }

    /** Collision height of the block (0 = no collision). */
    public static double height(Material material) {
        return switch (shape(material)) {
            case FULL -> 1.0;
            case SLAB -> 0.5;
            case NONE -> 0.0;
        };
    }
}
```
