`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/SimBuildInfo.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import io.papermc.paper.ServerBuildInfo;
import net.kyori.adventure.key.Key;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.OptionalInt;

/** Build info of the simulated server (loaded with ServiceLoader). */
public final class SimBuildInfo implements ServerBuildInfo {

    @Override
    public Key brandId() {
        return ServerBuildInfo.BRAND_PAPER_ID;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isBrandCompatible(Key brandId) {
        return brandId.equals(ServerBuildInfo.BRAND_PAPER_ID);
    }

    @Override
    public String brandName() {
        return "Paper";
    }

    @Override
    public String minecraftVersionId() {
        return "1.21.11";
    }

    @Override
    public String minecraftVersionName() {
        return "1.21.11";
    }

    @Override
    public OptionalInt buildNumber() {
        return OptionalInt.empty();
    }

    @Override
    public Instant buildTime() {
        return Instant.EPOCH;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> gitBranch() {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<String> gitCommit() {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    @Override
    public String asString(StringRepresentation representation) {
        return "Paper 1.21.11 (simulated)";
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/SimEntity.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.EntityType;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.mockito.Mockito;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * A non player entity the server moves: a battle bus seat (armor stand), a shulker or boat used
 * as a moving platform, a boat driven by a player ... Its box is width x height around x/y/z.
 */
public final class SimEntity {

    private static int nextId = 70_000;

    public final Entity entity;
    public final EntityType type;
    public final int id = nextId++;
    private final UUID uuid = UUID.randomUUID();
    private final SimWorld world;
    public double x, y, z;
    public final double width, height;
    public boolean gravity = true;
    public Vector velocity = new Vector();
    public final List<Entity> passengers = new ArrayList<>();
    public final List<Location> teleports = new ArrayList<>();

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T extends Entity> SimEntity(SimWorld world, Class<T> api, EntityType type, double x, double y, double z,
                                        double width, double height) {
        this.world = world;
        this.type = type;
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.z = z;
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
        this.entity = Sim.mock(api, invocation -> {
            Object[] a = invocation.getArguments();
            return switch (invocation.getMethod().getName()) {
                case "getType" -> type;
                case "getEntityId" -> id;
                case "getUniqueId" -> uuid;
                case "getWorld" -> world.world;
                case "getLocation" -> a.length == 0 ? new Location(world.world, this.x, this.y, this.z) : null;
                case "getBoundingBox" -> box();
                case "getVelocity" -> velocity.clone();
                case "setVelocity" -> {
                    velocity = ((Vector) a[0]).clone();
                    yield null;
                }
                case "hasGravity" -> gravity;
                case "getPassengers" -> new ArrayList<>(passengers);
                case "isValid", "isEmpty" -> invocation.getMethod().getName().equals("isValid") || passengers.isEmpty();
                case "teleport" -> {
                    Location to = (Location) a[0];
                    teleports.add(to.clone());
                    this.x = to.getX();
                    this.y = to.getY();
                    this.z = to.getZ();
                    yield true;
                }
                case "eject" -> {
                    passengers.clear();
                    yield true;
                }
                case "hashCode" -> System.identityHashCode(invocation.getMock());
                case "equals" -> invocation.getMock() == a[0];
                case "toString" -> "SimEntity[" + type + "]";
                default -> Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
            };
        });
        world.entities.add(entity);
    }

    public BoundingBox box() {
        double half = width / 2.0;
        return new BoundingBox(x - half, y, z - half, x + half, y + height, z + half);
    }

    public void move(double dx, double dy, double dz) {
        x += dx;
        y += dy;
        z += dz;
    }

    public void remove() {
        world.entities.remove(entity);
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/SimPlayer.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.BlockUtil;
import me.mrtahadarvish.astral.util.MathUtil;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Input;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.EntityType;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.PlayerInventory;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.mockito.Mockito;
import org.mockito.stubbing.Answer;

import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * A simulated player. The "client" half runs vanilla movement physics and sends its position
 * like a vanilla client does; the "server" half is a Player mock that returns what the server
 * knows from the received packets.
 */
public final class SimPlayer {

    private static int nextEntityId = 100;

    public final SimWorld world;
    public final Player player;
    public final UUID uuid = UUID.randomUUID();
    public final String name;
    public final int entityId = nextEntityId++;
    public final Random random;

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- server side (received)
    public double sx, sy, sz;
    public float syaw, spitch;
    public boolean sOnGround = true;
    public boolean sSprinting;

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- client side
    public double x, y, z;
    public double mx, my, mz;
    public float yaw, pitch;
    public boolean onGround = true;
    public boolean sprinting, sneaking, jump;
    public float forward, strafe;
    public boolean usingItem, blocking;
    public int useTicks;
    public int slot;
    public int ping = 50;
    public GameMode gameMode = GameMode.SURVIVAL;
    public Material mainHand = Material.DIAMOND_SWORD;
    /** Hotbar contents (null = use mainHand for that slot). */
    public final Material[] hotbar = new Material[9];
    public String brand = "vanilla";
    public boolean gliding;
    public final Set<String> permissions = new HashSet<>();

    private int jumpDelay;
    private int positionReminder;
    private double lastSentX, lastSentY, lastSentZ;
    private float lastSentYaw, lastSentPitch;
    private boolean lastSentOnGround = true;
    private boolean lastSentSprint;
    /** Last position / rotation a PlayerMoveEvent was fired for (Paper's lastPosX / lastYaw). */
    double eventX, eventY, eventZ;
    float eventYaw, eventPitch;

    /**
     * Silent rotations: when set, the regular movement packet carries this rotation instead of
     * the one the client is looking with (movement still follows the client's own yaw).
     */
    public Float silentYaw, silentPitch;

    /** Server side fall distance, added up from every movement packet like the server does. */
    public double sFall;
    /** Entity#setGravity - a plugin can turn gravity off (server side / what the client applied). */
    public boolean gravity = true;
    public boolean clientGravity = true;

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- minigames (battle bus ...)
    /** Vehicle the player rides, null = none. The server moves passengers, the client follows. */
    public SimEntity vehicle;
    /** Potion effects ("slow_falling", "levitation") the server knows / the client already applies. */
    public final Set<String> effects = new HashSet<>();
    public final Set<String> clientEffects = new HashSet<>();
    /** flying = client side flight, sFlying = what the server allows / knows. */
    public boolean flying, sFlying, allowFlight;
    /** Teleports Astral itself did (setbacks). */
    public int astralTeleports;
    /** Paper ignores movement until the client confirms the newest teleport. */
    private Location pendingTeleport;
    private int confirmIn;
    private int clientTick;
    private final List<Object[]> scheduled = new java.util.ArrayList<>();

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- cheats
    /** Multiplies the horizontal movement (speed hack). */
    public double cheatSpeed = 1.0;
    /** Fly: replaces gravity, vertical motion is set to this every tick. */
    public Double cheatFlyY;
    /** NoFall: always tell the server "on ground". */
    public boolean cheatGround;
    /** Step height (vanilla 0.6). */
    public double stepHeight = 0.6;
    /** NoSlow: ignore the item use slow down. */
    public boolean cheatNoSlow;
    /** Air strafe: in the air the motion instantly follows the input direction. */
    public boolean cheatStrafe;
    /** Mouse sensitivity (vanilla option, 0.5 = 100%). */
    public double sensitivity = 0.5;

    public SimPlayer(SimWorld world, String name, double x, double y, double z, long seed) {
        this.world = world;
        this.name = name;
        this.random = new Random(seed);
        this.x = this.sx = this.lastSentX = x;
        this.y = this.sy = this.lastSentY = y;
        this.z = this.sz = this.lastSentZ = z;
        this.eventX = x;
        this.eventY = y;
        this.eventZ = z;
        this.player = Sim.mock(Player.class,
                invocation -> answer(invocation.getMethod().getName(), invocation.getArguments(), invocation));
        world.players.add(this);
    }

    // ================================================================ client physics

    public double speedAttribute(boolean sprint) {
        return 0.1 * (sprint ? 1.3 : 1.0);
    }

    public BoundingBox clientBox() {
        double h = sneaking ? 1.5 : 1.8;
        return new BoundingBox(x - 0.3, y, z - 0.3, x + 0.3, y + h, z + 0.3);
    }

    /** Runs a change on the client after some client ticks (it travels through the network). */
    public void later(int ticks, Runnable change) {
        if (ticks <= 0) {
            change.run();
        } else {
            scheduled.add(new Object[]{clientTick + ticks, change});
        }
    }

    /** Item in the selected hotbar slot. */
    public Material held() {
        return slot >= 0 && slot < 9 && hotbar[slot] != null ? hotbar[slot] : mainHand;
    }

    /** One way network delay in client ticks. */
    public int halfRtt() {
        return (int) Math.ceil(ping / 100.0);
    }

    /** One vanilla client tick of movement (LivingEntity#aiStep + travel). */
    public void physicsTick() {
        clientTick++;
        for (java.util.Iterator<Object[]> it = scheduled.iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
            Object[] task = it.next();
            if ((int) task[0] <= clientTick) {
                it.remove();
                ((Runnable) task[1]).run();
            }
        }
        if (pendingTeleport != null && --confirmIn <= 0) {
            // the teleport arrived: snap there and confirm it (the confirm carries this position)
            x = lastSentX = pendingTeleport.getX();
            y = lastSentY = pendingTeleport.getY();
            z = lastSentZ = pendingTeleport.getZ();
            mx = my = mz = 0.0;
            pendingTeleport = null;
        }
        if (vehicle != null) {
            return; // passengers are moved by their vehicle
        }
        if (gliding) {
            glideTick();
            return;
        }
        if (flying) {
            my = jump ? 0.375 : sneaking ? -0.375 : my * 0.6;
        }
        if (Math.abs(mx) < 0.003) {
            mx = 0.0;
        }
        if (Math.abs(my) < 0.003) {
            my = 0.0;
        }
        if (Math.abs(mz) < 0.003) {
            mz = 0.0;
        }
        if (jumpDelay > 0) {
            jumpDelay--;
        }
        if (jump) {
            if (onGround && jumpDelay == 0) {
                my = Math.max(0.42F, my);
                if (sprinting) {
                    double r = Math.toRadians(yaw);
                    mx += -Math.sin(r) * 0.2;
                    mz += Math.cos(r) * 0.2;
                }
                jumpDelay = 10;
            }
        } else {
            jumpDelay = 0;
        }

        double fw = forward * 0.98;
        double st = strafe * 0.98;
        if (sneaking) {
            fw *= 0.3;
            st *= 0.3;
        }
        if (usingItem && !cheatNoSlow) {
            fw *= 0.2;
            st *= 0.2;
        }
        double friction = onGround ? BlockUtil.friction(world.type((int) Math.floor(x), (int) Math.floor(y - 0.5000001), (int) Math.floor(z))) : 1.0;
        double speed = onGround ? speedAttribute(sprinting) * (0.21600002 / (friction * friction * friction)) : (sprinting ? 0.026 : 0.02);
        double length = st * st + fw * fw;
        if (length > 1.0E-7) {
            if (length > 1.0) {
                double n = Math.sqrt(length);
                st /= n;
                fw /= n;
            }
            double r = Math.toRadians(yaw);
            double sin = Math.sin(r);
            double cos = Math.cos(r);
            if (cheatStrafe && !onGround) {
                double h = Math.hypot(mx, mz);
                mx = (st * cos - fw * sin) * h;
                mz = (fw * cos + st * sin) * h;
            } else {
                mx += (st * cos - fw * sin) * speed;
                mz += (fw * cos + st * sin) * speed;
            }
        }
        if (cheatFlyY != null) {
            my = cheatFlyY;
        }
        Vector wanted = new Vector(mx * cheatSpeed, my, mz * cheatSpeed);
        Vector moved = world.collide(clientBox(), wanted, onGround, stepHeight);
        boolean collX = moved.getX() != wanted.getX();
        boolean collZ = moved.getZ() != wanted.getZ();
        boolean collY = moved.getY() != wanted.getY();
        x += moved.getX();
        y += moved.getY();
        z += moved.getZ();
        onGround = collY && wanted.getY() < 0.0;
        if (collX) {
            mx = 0.0;
        }
        if (collZ) {
            mz = 0.0;
        }
        if (collY) {
            my = 0.0;
        }
        if (cheatFlyY == null && !flying) {
            if (clientEffects.contains("levitation")) {
                my += (0.05 - my) * 0.2;
            } else if (clientGravity) {
                my -= clientEffects.contains("slow_falling") && my <= 0.0 ? 0.01 : 0.08;
            }
            my *= 0.98;
        }
        mx *= friction * 0.91;
        mz *= friction * 0.91;
        if (usingItem) {
            useTicks++;
        }
    }

    /** LivingEntity#updateFallFlyingMovement (elytra), then the move. */
    private void glideTick() {
        double yawR = Math.toRadians(yaw);
        double pitchR = Math.toRadians(pitch);
        double lx = -Math.sin(yawR) * Math.cos(pitchR);
        double ly = -Math.sin(pitchR);
        double lz = Math.cos(yawR) * Math.cos(pitchR);
        double horizontalLook = Math.sqrt(lx * lx + lz * lz);
        double horizontalSpeed = Math.sqrt(mx * mx + mz * mz);
        double gravityNow = !clientGravity ? 0.0 : clientEffects.contains("slow_falling") && my <= 0.0 ? 0.01 : 0.08;
        double cos2 = Math.cos(pitchR) * Math.cos(pitchR);
        double length = Math.sqrt(lx * lx + ly * ly + lz * lz);
        cos2 *= Math.min(1.0, length / 0.4);
        my += gravityNow * (-1.0 + cos2 * 0.75);
        if (my < 0.0 && horizontalLook > 0.0) {
            double d = my * -0.1 * cos2;
            mx += lx * d / horizontalLook;
            my += d;
            mz += lz * d / horizontalLook;
        }
        if (pitchR < 0.0 && horizontalLook > 0.0) {
            double d = horizontalSpeed * -Math.sin(pitchR) * 0.04;
            mx += -lx * d / horizontalLook;
            my += d * 3.2;
            mz += -lz * d / horizontalLook;
        }
        if (horizontalLook > 0.0) {
            mx += (lx / horizontalLook * horizontalSpeed - mx) * 0.1;
            mz += (lz / horizontalLook * horizontalSpeed - mz) * 0.1;
        }
        mx *= 0.99;
        my *= 0.98;
        mz *= 0.99;
        if (cheatFlyY != null) {
            my = cheatFlyY; // elytra fly cheats override the glide physics
        }
        Vector wanted = new Vector(mx * cheatSpeed, my, mz * cheatSpeed);
        Vector moved = world.collide(clientBox(), wanted, false, stepHeight);
        x += moved.getX();
        y += moved.getY();
        z += moved.getZ();
        onGround = moved.getY() != wanted.getY() && wanted.getY() < 0.0;
        if (moved.getX() != wanted.getX()) {
            mx = 0.0;
        }
        if (moved.getZ() != wanted.getZ()) {
            mz = 0.0;
        }
        if (moved.getY() != wanted.getY()) {
            my = 0.0;
        }
    }

    /** The server moves a passenger with its vehicle (no movement packets). */
    public void ride(double px, double py, double pz) {
        x = sx = lastSentX = eventX = px;
        y = sy = lastSentY = eventY = py;
        z = sz = lastSentZ = eventZ = pz;
        mx = my = mz = 0.0;
    }

    /** LocalPlayer#sendPosition: sends the movement packet the server will process. */
    public void sendMovement() {
        if (vehicle != null) {
            return; // the client sends vehicle / rotation packets only
        }
        positionReminder++;
        double dx = x - lastSentX;
        double dy = y - lastSentY;
        double dz = z - lastSentZ;
        boolean position = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz > 4.0E-8 || positionReminder >= 20;
        float sendYaw = silentYaw != null ? silentYaw : yaw;
        float sendPitch = silentPitch != null ? silentPitch : pitch;
        boolean rotation = sendYaw != lastSentYaw || sendPitch != lastSentPitch;
        boolean ground = cheatGround || onGround;
        if (sprinting != lastSentSprint) {
            sSprinting = sprinting;
            lastSentSprint = sprinting;
        }
        if (position && pendingTeleport != null) {
            position = false; // Paper ignores moves until the teleport is confirmed
            lastSentX = x;
            lastSentY = y;
            lastSentZ = z;
        }
        if (position) {
            serverFall(y - sy, ground);
            sx = x;
            sy = y;
            sz = z;
            lastSentX = x;
            lastSentY = y;
            lastSentZ = z;
            positionReminder = 0;
        }
        if (rotation) {
            syaw = MathUtil.wrapDegrees(sendYaw);
            spitch = Math.max(-90.0F, Math.min(90.0F, sendPitch));
            lastSentYaw = sendYaw;
            lastSentPitch = sendPitch;
        }
        if (position || rotation || ground != lastSentOnGround) {
            if (!position) {
                serverFall(0.0, ground);
            }
            sOnGround = ground;
            lastSentOnGround = ground;
        }
        if (position || rotation) {
            Sim.current().moved(this);
        }
    }

    /** ServerPlayer#doCheckFallDamage: trusts the on ground flag of the packet. */
    private void serverFall(double dy, boolean ground) {
        if (ground) {
            sFall = 0.0;
        } else if (dy < 0.0) {
            sFall -= dy;
        }
    }

    /** Server side teleport (also moves the client). */
    public void teleportTo(Location location) {
        sx = eventX = location.getX();
        sy = eventY = location.getY();
        sz = eventZ = location.getZ();
        eventYaw = syaw;
        eventPitch = spitch;
        // the client gets there half a round trip later; until it confirms, Paper ignores its moves
        pendingTeleport = location.clone();
        confirmIn = Math.max(1, halfRtt());
    }

    /** Sends a raw position packet (cheat clients can send anything). */
    public void sendRawPosition(double px, double py, double pz, float pyaw, float ppitch, boolean ground) {
        if (pendingTeleport != null) {
            return;
        }
        serverFall(py - sy, ground);
        sx = px;
        sy = py;
        sz = pz;
        syaw = MathUtil.wrapDegrees(pyaw);
        spitch = ppitch;
        sOnGround = ground;
        // the client's network state now holds these values (it re-sends its real rotation next tick)
        lastSentX = px;
        lastSentY = py;
        lastSentZ = pz;
        lastSentYaw = pyaw;
        lastSentPitch = ppitch;
        lastSentOnGround = ground;
        Sim.current().moved(this);
    }

    // ================================================================ mouse

    /** One mouse count in degrees for the current sensitivity. */
    public double mouseUnit() {
        double f = sensitivity * 0.6 + 0.2;
        return f * f * f * 8.0 * 0.15;
    }

    /** Turns like a mouse: whole mouse counts times the sensitivity step. */
    public void mouseTurn(int countsX, int countsY) {
        double f = sensitivity * 0.6 + 0.2;
        double scale = f * f * f * 8.0;
        float dyaw = (float) (countsX * scale) * 0.15F;
        float dpitch = (float) (countsY * scale) * 0.15F;
        yaw = yaw + dyaw;
        pitch = Math.max(-90.0F, Math.min(90.0F, pitch + dpitch));
    }

    /** Human like aim at a point: moves part of the way with whole mouse counts plus noise. */
    public void mouseAim(Vector point, double gain, int noise) {
        double[] target = rotationTo(eyeClient(), point);
        double unit = mouseUnit();
        double dyaw = MathUtil.wrapDegrees((float) (target[0] - yaw));
        double dpitch = target[1] - pitch;
        int cx = (int) Math.round(dyaw * gain / unit) + (noise > 0 ? random.nextInt(noise * 2 + 1) - noise : 0);
        int cy = (int) Math.round(dpitch * gain / unit) + (noise > 0 ? random.nextInt(noise * 2 + 1) - noise : 0);
        mouseTurn(cx, cy);
    }

    public Vector eyeClient() {
        return new Vector(x, y + (sneaking ? 1.27 : 1.62), z);
    }

    public Vector eyeServer() {
        return new Vector(sx, sy + (sneaking ? 1.27 : 1.62), sz);
    }

    public static double[] rotationTo(Vector from, Vector to) {
        Vector d = to.clone().subtract(from);
        double yaw = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(-d.getX(), d.getZ()));
        double pitch = Math.toDegrees(-Math.atan2(d.getY(), Math.hypot(d.getX(), d.getZ())));
        return new double[]{yaw, pitch};
    }

    // ================================================================ the Player mock

    public BoundingBox serverBox() {
        double h = sneaking ? 1.5 : 1.8;
        return new BoundingBox(sx - 0.3, sy, sz - 0.3, sx + 0.3, sy + h, sz + 0.3);
    }

    private Object answer(String name, Object[] a, org.mockito.invocation.InvocationOnMock invocation) throws Throwable {
        switch (name) {
            case "getUniqueId": return uuid;
            case "getName": return this.name;
            case "getEntityId": return entityId;
            case "getType": return EntityType.PLAYER;
            case "getWorld": return world.world;
            case "getLocation":
                if (a.length == 0) {
                    return new Location(world.world, sx, sy, sz, syaw, spitch);
                }
                Location target = (Location) a[0];
                target.setWorld(world.world);
                target.setX(sx);
                target.setY(sy);
                target.setZ(sz);
                target.setYaw(syaw);
                target.setPitch(spitch);
                return target;
            case "getBoundingBox": return serverBox();
            case "getEyeLocation": return new Location(world.world, sx, sy + (sneaking ? 1.27 : 1.62), sz, syaw, spitch);
            case "getEyeHeight": return sneaking ? 1.27 : 1.62;
            case "isOnGround": return sOnGround;
            case "isSneaking": return sneaking;
            case "isSprinting": return sSprinting;
            case "isClimbing": return BlockUtil.isClimbable(world.type((int) Math.floor(sx), (int) Math.floor(sy), (int) Math.floor(sz)));
            case "getAttribute": return attribute((Attribute) a[0]);
            case "getGameMode": return gameMode;
            case "hasActiveItem", "isHandRaised": return usingItem;
            case "getActiveItem": return item(usingItem ? held() : Material.AIR);
            case "getActiveItemUsedTime": return usingItem ? useTicks : 0;
            case "getActiveItemRemainingTime": return usingItem ? Math.max(0, 72000 - useTicks) : 0;
            case "isBlocking": return blocking;
            case "getPing": return ping;
            case "getCurrentInput": return input();
            case "getInventory": return inventory();
            case "getNearbyEntities": return world.nearby(new Vector(sx, sy + 0.9, sz), (Double) a[0], (Double) a[1], (Double) a[2], player);
            case "getVelocity": return new Vector();
            case "getFoodLevel": return 20;
            case "getFallDistance": return (float) sFall;
            case "setFallDistance": sFall = ((Number) a[0]).doubleValue(); return null;
            case "hasGravity": return gravity;
            case "isInsideVehicle": return vehicle != null;
            case "getVehicle": return vehicle == null ? null : vehicle.entity;
            case "hasPotionEffect": return a[0] instanceof org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType type && effects.contains(type.getKey().getKey());
            case "isFlying": return sFlying;
            case "getAllowFlight": return allowFlight;
            case "isInWater": return world.type((int) Math.floor(sx), (int) Math.floor(sy), (int) Math.floor(sz)) == Material.WATER;
            case "isInLava": return world.type((int) Math.floor(sx), (int) Math.floor(sy), (int) Math.floor(sz)) == Material.LAVA;
            case "hasPermission": return a[0] instanceof String s && permissions.contains(s);
            case "isOnline", "isValid": return true;
            case "getClientBrandName": return brand;
            case "isGliding": return gliding;
            case "getProtocolVersion": return 774;
            case "getHealth": return 20.0;
            case "getTargetBlockExact": {
                Vector eye = eyeServer();
                RayTraceResult hit = world.rayTrace(eye, MathUtil.direction(syaw, spitch), ((Number) a[0]).doubleValue());
                return hit == null ? null : hit.getHitBlock();
            }
            case "teleport", "teleportAsync": {
                astralTeleports++;
                Location location = (Location) a[0];
                Sim.current().teleport(this, location);
                return true;
            }
            case "hashCode": return System.identityHashCode(invocation.getMock());
            case "toString": return "SimPlayer[" + this.name + "]";
            default: return Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
        }
    }

    private final java.util.Map<String, AttributeInstance> attributes = new java.util.HashMap<>();
    private static final java.util.Map<Material, ItemStack> ITEMS = new java.util.EnumMap<>(Material.class);
    private PlayerInventory inventory;

    private AttributeInstance attribute(Attribute attribute) {
        String key = attribute.getKey().getKey();
        return attributes.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> Sim.mock(AttributeInstance.class,
                invocation -> switch (invocation.getMethod().getName()) {
                    case "getValue", "getBaseValue" -> attributeValue(k);
                    default -> Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
                }));
    }

    private double attributeValue(String key) {
        return switch (key) {
            case "movement_speed" -> speedAttribute(sSprinting);
            case "jump_strength" -> 0.42F;
            case "step_height" -> 0.6;
            case "gravity" -> 0.08;
            case "entity_interaction_range" -> gameMode == GameMode.CREATIVE ? 5.0 : 3.0;
            case "block_interaction_range" -> gameMode == GameMode.CREATIVE ? 5.0 : 4.5;
            case "safe_fall_distance" -> 3.0;
            case "fall_damage_multiplier" -> 1.0;
            case "sneaking_speed" -> 0.3;
            default -> 0.0;
        };
    }

    public static synchronized ItemStack item(Material material) {
        return ITEMS.computeIfAbsent(material, m -> Mockito.mock(ItemStack.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly()
                .defaultAnswer((Answer<Object>) invocation -> switch (invocation.getMethod().getName()) {
                    case "getType" -> m;
                    case "getAmount" -> 1;
                    case "isEmpty" -> m.isAir();
                    default -> Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
                })));
    }

    private PlayerInventory inventory() {
        if (inventory == null) {
            inventory = Sim.mock(PlayerInventory.class, invocation -> switch (invocation.getMethod().getName()) {
                case "getItemInMainHand" -> item(held());
                case "getItem" -> invocation.getArguments().length == 1 && invocation.getArguments()[0] instanceof Integer i
                        ? item(i >= 0 && i < 9 && hotbar[i] != null ? hotbar[i] : Material.AIR) : null;
                case "getItemInOffHand" -> item(Material.AIR);
                case "getHeldItemSlot" -> slot;
                default -> Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
            });
        }
        return inventory;
    }

    private Input input() {
        final boolean f = forward > 0;
        final boolean b = forward < 0;
        final boolean l = strafe > 0;
        final boolean r = strafe < 0;
        final boolean j = jump;
        final boolean s = sneaking;
        final boolean sp = sprinting;
        return new Input() {
            public boolean isForward() { return f; }
            public boolean isBackward() { return b; }
            public boolean isLeft() { return l; }
            public boolean isRight() { return r; }
            public boolean isJump() { return j; }
            public boolean isSneak() { return s; }
            public boolean isSprint() { return sp; }
        };
    }

    /** Releases every key (what vanilla does when a screen opens). */
    public void releaseKeys() {
        forward = 0;
        strafe = 0;
        jump = false;
        sprinting = false;
    }

    public Block blockBelow() {
        return world.block((int) Math.floor(x), (int) Math.floor(y - 0.5), (int) Math.floor(z));
    }

    public static List<String> names(List<SimPlayer> players) {
        return players.stream().map(p -> p.name).toList();
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/SimRegistryAccess.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import io.papermc.paper.datacomponent.DataComponentType;
import io.papermc.paper.registry.RegistryAccess;
import io.papermc.paper.registry.RegistryKey;
import net.kyori.adventure.key.Key;
import org.bukkit.Keyed;
import org.bukkit.NamespacedKey;
import org.bukkit.Registry;
import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockType;
import org.bukkit.damage.DamageType;
import org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemType;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;
import org.mockito.MockSettings;
import org.mockito.Mockito;
import org.mockito.invocation.InvocationOnMock;
import org.mockito.stubbing.Answer;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

/**
 * Registry access for the simulation (loaded with ServiceLoader). Every registry entry is a
 * Mockito mock that knows its key; block types know whether they are air / solid / occluding.
 */
public final class SimRegistryAccess implements RegistryAccess {

    private final Map<Object, Registry<?>> registries = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Override
    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "removal"})
    @Deprecated
    public <T extends Keyed> Registry<T> getRegistry(Class<T> type) {
        return (Registry<T>) registries.computeIfAbsent(type, k -> create(new Class<?>[]{type}));
    }

    @Override
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T extends Keyed> Registry<T> getRegistry(RegistryKey<T> key) {
        Registry<?> registry = registries.get(key);
        if (registry == null) {
            registry = create(types(key));
            Registry<?> raced = registries.putIfAbsent(key, registry);
            if (raced != null) {
                registry = raced;
            }
        }
        return (Registry<T>) registry;
    }

    private static Class<?>[] types(RegistryKey<?> key) {
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.ATTRIBUTE)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{Attribute.class};
        }
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.MOB_EFFECT)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{PotionEffectType.class};
        }
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.BLOCK)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{BlockType.Typed.class};
        }
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.ITEM)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{ItemType.Typed.class};
        }
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.DATA_COMPONENT_TYPE)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{DataComponentType.class, DataComponentType.Valued.class, DataComponentType.NonValued.class};
        }
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.MENU)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{org.bukkit.inventory.MenuType.Typed.class, org.bukkit.inventory.MenuType.class};
        }
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.ENCHANTMENT)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{Enchantment.class};
        }
        if (key.equals(RegistryKey.DAMAGE_TYPE)) {
            return new Class<?>[]{DamageType.class};
        }
        return new Class<?>[]{Keyed.class};
    }

    private static Registry<?> create(Class<?>[] types) {
        Map<String, Object> entries = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        return Mockito.mock(Registry.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly().defaultAnswer((Answer<Object>) invocation -> {
            String name = invocation.getMethod().getName();
            Object[] args = invocation.getArguments();
            if ((name.equals("get") || name.equals("getOrThrow")) && args.length == 1 && args[0] != null) {
                String key = keyOf(args[0]);
                Object existing = entries.get(key);
                if (existing != null) {
                    return existing;
                }
                // not computeIfAbsent: creating an entry can initialise a class that reads this registry again
                Object created = entry(types, key);
                Object raced = entries.putIfAbsent(key, created);
                return raced != null ? raced : created;
            }
            if (name.equals("iterator")) {
                return Collections.emptyIterator();
            }
            if (name.equals("stream")) {
                return Stream.empty();
            }
            return Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
        }));
    }

    private static String keyOf(Object key) {
        if (key instanceof NamespacedKey namespacedKey) {
            return namespacedKey.toString();
        }
        if (key instanceof Key adventureKey) {
            return adventureKey.asString();
        }
        return String.valueOf(key);
    }

    private static Object entry(Class<?>[] types, String key) {
        MockSettings settings = Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly().defaultAnswer(new EntryAnswer(key));
        if (types.length > 1) {
            settings = settings.extraInterfaces(Arrays.copyOfRange(types, 1, types.length));
        }
        return Mockito.mock(types[0], settings);
    }

    /** Behaviour of every registry entry. */
    private record EntryAnswer(String key) implements Answer<Object> {
        @Override
        public Object answer(InvocationOnMock invocation) throws Throwable {
            String name = invocation.getMethod().getName();
            String path = key.contains(":") ? key.substring(key.indexOf(':') + 1) : key;
            switch (name) {
                case "getKey", "getKeyOrThrow" -> {
                    return NamespacedKey.fromString(key);
                }
                case "key" -> {
                    return Key.key(key);
                }
                case "isAir" -> {
                    return SimBlocks.isAir(path);
                }
                case "isSolid", "hasCollision" -> {
                    return SimBlocks.isSolid(path);
                }
                case "isOccluding" -> {
                    return SimBlocks.isOccluding(path);
                }
                case "toString" -> {
                    return key;
                }
                default -> {
                    return Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/SimServer.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Keyed;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.NamespacedKey;
import org.bukkit.Server;
import org.bukkit.Tag;
import org.bukkit.UnsafeValues;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.plugin.PluginManager;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitScheduler;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;
import org.mockito.Mockito;
import org.mockito.stubbing.Answer;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyString;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/** A fake Paper server: tick counter, scheduler that runs tasks immediately, tags, players. */
public final class SimServer {

    public static int tick = 1000;
    public static final List<Player> ONLINE = new ArrayList<>();

    private static Server server;

    private SimServer() {
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public static synchronized void init() {
        if (server != null) {
            return;
        }
        server = Mockito.mock(Server.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly());
        when(server.getLogger()).thenReturn(Logger.getLogger("SimServer"));
        when(server.getName()).thenReturn("Paper");
        when(server.getVersion()).thenReturn("1.21.11-sim");
        when(server.getBukkitVersion()).thenReturn("1.21.11-R0.1-SNAPSHOT");
        when(server.getMinecraftVersion()).thenReturn("1.21.11");
        when(server.isPrimaryThread()).thenReturn(true);
        when(server.getCurrentTick()).thenAnswer(invocation -> tick);
        when(server.getAverageTickTime()).thenReturn(50.0);
        when(server.getOnlinePlayers()).thenAnswer(invocation -> (Collection<Player>) (Collection<?>) ONLINE);
        when(server.getPlayer(any(UUID.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> {
            UUID uuid = invocation.getArgument(0);
            return ONLINE.stream().filter(p -> p.getUniqueId().equals(uuid)).findFirst().orElse(null);
        });
        when(server.getRegistry(any())).thenAnswer(invocation ->
                io.papermc.paper.registry.RegistryAccess.registryAccess().getRegistry((Class<Keyed>) invocation.getArgument(0)));
        UnsafeValues unsafe = Mockito.mock(UnsafeValues.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly());
        when(unsafe.getProtocolVersion()).thenReturn(774);
        when(server.getUnsafe()).thenReturn(unsafe);
        when(server.getPluginManager()).thenReturn(Mockito.mock(PluginManager.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly()));
        when(server.getTag(anyString(), any(NamespacedKey.class), any())).thenAnswer(invocation ->
                new SimTag(invocation.getArgument(1)));

        BukkitTask task = Mockito.mock(BukkitTask.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly());
        BukkitScheduler scheduler = Mockito.mock(BukkitScheduler.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly().defaultAnswer((Answer<Object>) invocation -> {
            String name = invocation.getMethod().getName();
            if (name.startsWith("runTask") && !name.contains("Timer") && !name.contains("Async")) {
                for (Object argument : invocation.getArguments()) {
                    if (argument instanceof Runnable runnable) {
                        runnable.run();
                        return task;
                    }
                }
            }
            return Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
        }));
        when(server.getScheduler()).thenReturn(scheduler);
        Bukkit.setServer(server);
    }

    /** Material tags used by Astral (climbable, beds). */
    @SuppressWarnings("rawtypes")
    private static final class SimTag implements Tag {
        private static final Set<Material> CLIMBABLE = Set.of(Material.LADDER, Material.VINE, Material.SCAFFOLDING,
                Material.TWISTING_VINES, Material.WEEPING_VINES, Material.CAVE_VINES);
        private final NamespacedKey key;

        SimTag(NamespacedKey key) {
            this.key = key;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isTagged(Keyed item) {
            if (!(item instanceof Material material)) {
                return false;
            }
            return switch (key.getKey()) {
                case "climbable" -> CLIMBABLE.contains(material);
                case "beds" -> material.name().endsWith("_BED");
                default -> false;
            };
        }

        @Override
        public Set getValues() {
            return Set.of();
        }

        @Override
        public NamespacedKey getKey() {
            return key;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/me/mrtahadarvish/astral/sim/SimWorld.java`:

```java
package me.mrtahadarvish.astral.sim;

import org.bukkit.FluidCollisionMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;
import org.bukkit.block.data.BlockData;
import org.bukkit.block.data.Levelled;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;
import org.bukkit.util.RayTraceResult;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;
import org.mockito.Mockito;
import org.mockito.stubbing.Answer;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * A voxel world with real block collisions (full blocks and slabs), vanilla style axis
 * collision + step up, and block ray tracing.
 */
public final class SimWorld {

    private final Map<Long, Material> blocks = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Long, Block> blockMocks = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Long, Double> breakSpeeds = new HashMap<>();
    public final List<SimPlayer> players = new ArrayList<>();
    public final List<Entity> entities = new ArrayList<>();
    public final World world;
    private final UUID uid = UUID.randomUUID();

    public SimWorld() {
        world = Sim.mock(World.class, invocation -> {
            Object[] a = invocation.getArguments();
            return switch (invocation.getMethod().getName()) {
                case "getUID" -> uid;
                case "getName" -> "world";
                case "hasCollisionsIn" -> hasCollisions((BoundingBox) a[0]);
                case "isChunkLoaded" -> true;
                case "getMinHeight" -> -64;
                case "getMaxHeight" -> 320;
                case "getBlockAt" -> a.length == 3
                        ? block((Integer) a[0], (Integer) a[1], (Integer) a[2])
                        : block(((Location) a[0]).getBlockX(), ((Location) a[0]).getBlockY(), ((Location) a[0]).getBlockZ());
                case "rayTraceBlocks" -> a.length >= 3 ? rayTrace(((Location) a[0]).toVector(), (Vector) a[1], (Double) a[2]) : null;
                case "getPlayers" -> {
                    List<Player> list = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (SimPlayer p : players) {
                        list.add(p.player);
                    }
                    yield list;
                }
                case "getNearbyEntities" -> {
                    if (a.length == 4 && a[0] instanceof Location center) {
                        yield nearby(center.toVector(), (Double) a[1], (Double) a[2], (Double) a[3], null);
                    }
                    if (a.length >= 1 && a[0] instanceof BoundingBox area) {
                        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                        java.util.function.Predicate<Entity> filter = a.length > 1 && a[1] != null
                                ? (java.util.function.Predicate<Entity>) a[1] : e -> true;
                        List<Entity> out = new ArrayList<>();
                        for (SimPlayer p : players) {
                            if (p.player.getBoundingBox().overlaps(area) && filter.test(p.player)) {
                                out.add(p.player);
                            }
                        }
                        for (Entity entity : entities) {
                            if (entity.getBoundingBox().overlaps(area) && filter.test(entity)) {
                                out.add(entity);
                            }
                        }
                        yield out;
                    }
                    yield new ArrayList<Entity>();
                }
                case "hashCode" -> System.identityHashCode(invocation.getMock());
                case "toString" -> "SimWorld";
                default -> Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
            };
        });
    }

    /** Drops everything this world references (called when its simulation is closed). */
    void clear() {
        blocks.clear();
        blockMocks.clear();
        breakSpeeds.clear();
        players.clear();
        entities.clear();
    }

    private static long key(int x, int y, int z) {
        return (((long) x & 0x3FFFFFFL) << 38) | (((long) z & 0x3FFFFFFL) << 12) | ((long) (y + 2048) & 0xFFFL);
    }

    public void set(int x, int y, int z, Material material) {
        if (y < -64 || y > 319) {
            return;
        }
        if (material.isAir()) {
            blocks.remove(key(x, y, z));
        } else {
            blocks.put(key(x, y, z), material);
        }
    }

    public void fill(int x1, int y1, int z1, int x2, int y2, int z2, Material material) {
        for (int x = Math.min(x1, x2); x <= Math.max(x1, x2); x++) {
            for (int y = Math.min(y1, y2); y <= Math.max(y1, y2); y++) {
                for (int z = Math.min(z1, z2); z <= Math.max(z1, z2); z++) {
                    set(x, y, z, material);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public Material type(int x, int y, int z) {
        if (y < -64 || y > 319) {
            return Material.AIR;
        }
        return blocks.getOrDefault(key(x, y, z), Material.AIR);
    }

    public void breakSpeed(int x, int y, int z, double speed) {
        breakSpeeds.put(key(x, y, z), speed);
    }

    public Block block(int x, int y, int z) {
        return blockMocks.computeIfAbsent(key(x, y, z), k -> createBlock(x, y, z));
    }

    private Block createBlock(int x, int y, int z) {
        return Sim.mock(Block.class, invocation -> {
            Object[] a = invocation.getArguments();
            return switch (invocation.getMethod().getName()) {
                case "getType" -> type(x, y, z);
                case "getX" -> x;
                case "getY" -> y;
                case "getZ" -> z;
                case "getWorld" -> world;
                case "getLocation" -> a.length == 0 ? new Location(world, x, y, z) : null;
                case "getRelative" -> {
                    if (a.length == 3) {
                        yield block(x + (Integer) a[0], y + (Integer) a[1], z + (Integer) a[2]);
                    }
                    BlockFace face = (BlockFace) a[0];
                    int distance = a.length > 1 ? (Integer) a[1] : 1;
                    yield block(x + face.getModX() * distance, y + face.getModY() * distance, z + face.getModZ() * distance);
                }
                case "getFace" -> {
                    Block other = (Block) a[0];
                    int dx = other.getX() - x;
                    int dy = other.getY() - y;
                    int dz = other.getZ() - z;
                    for (BlockFace face : BlockFace.values()) {
                        if (face.getModX() == dx && face.getModY() == dy && face.getModZ() == dz) {
                            yield face;
                        }
                    }
                    yield null;
                }
                case "getBlockData" -> blockData(type(x, y, z));
                case "getBoundingBox" -> {
                    double height = SimBlocks.height(type(x, y, z));
                    yield height > 0.0 ? new BoundingBox(x, y, z, x + 1, y + height, z + 1) : new BoundingBox(x, y, z, x, y, z);
                }
                case "getBreakSpeed" -> (float) (double) breakSpeeds.getOrDefault(key(x, y, z), 0.05);
                case "isEmpty" -> type(x, y, z).isAir();
                case "hashCode" -> System.identityHashCode(invocation.getMock());
                case "toString" -> "Block[" + x + "," + y + "," + z + " " + type(x, y, z) + "]";
                default -> Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation);
            };
        });
    }

    private static final BlockData SOLID_DATA = Mockito.mock(BlockData.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly());
    private static final Levelled LIQUID_DATA = Mockito.mock(Levelled.class, Mockito.withSettings().stubOnly()
            .defaultAnswer(invocation -> "getLevel".equals(invocation.getMethod().getName()) ? 0
                    : Mockito.RETURNS_DEFAULTS.answer(invocation)));

    private static BlockData blockData(Material type) {
        return type == Material.WATER || type == Material.LAVA ? LIQUID_DATA : SOLID_DATA;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ collisions

    public List<BoundingBox> collisionBoxes(BoundingBox region) {
        List<BoundingBox> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int x = (int) Math.floor(region.getMinX()) - 1; x <= (int) Math.floor(region.getMaxX()); x++) {
            for (int y = (int) Math.floor(region.getMinY()) - 1; y <= (int) Math.floor(region.getMaxY()); y++) {
                for (int z = (int) Math.floor(region.getMinZ()) - 1; z <= (int) Math.floor(region.getMaxZ()); z++) {
                    double height = SimBlocks.height(type(x, y, z));
                    if (height > 0.0) {
                        BoundingBox box = new BoundingBox(x, y, z, x + 1, y + height, z + 1);
                        if (box.overlaps(region)) {
                            out.add(box);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    public boolean hasCollisions(BoundingBox box) {
        return !collisionBoxes(box).isEmpty();
    }

    /** Vanilla style movement: collide Y, then X / Z, then try to step up. */
    public Vector collide(BoundingBox box, Vector movement, boolean onGround, double stepHeight) {
        Vector result = collideBox(box, movement);
        boolean collX = result.getX() != movement.getX();
        boolean collZ = result.getZ() != movement.getZ();
        boolean landing = result.getY() != movement.getY() && movement.getY() < 0.0;
        if (stepHeight > 0.0 && (onGround || landing) && (collX || collZ)) {
            Vector step = collideBox(box, new Vector(movement.getX(), stepHeight, movement.getZ()));
            if (horizontal(step) > horizontal(result)) {
                Vector down = collideBox(box.clone().shift(step), new Vector(0.0, -step.getY() + movement.getY(), 0.0));
                return step.add(down);
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static double horizontal(Vector v) {
        return v.getX() * v.getX() + v.getZ() * v.getZ();
    }

    private Vector collideBox(BoundingBox box, Vector movement) {
        double dx = movement.getX();
        double dy = movement.getY();
        double dz = movement.getZ();
        BoundingBox region = box.clone().expand(Math.max(-dx, 0), Math.max(-dy, 0), Math.max(-dz, 0),
                Math.max(dx, 0), Math.max(dy, 0), Math.max(dz, 0)).expand(0.01);
        List<BoundingBox> boxes = collisionBoxes(region);
        BoundingBox moving = box.clone();
        dy = clip(boxes, moving, dy, 1);
        moving.shift(0, dy, 0);
        if (Math.abs(dx) < Math.abs(dz)) {
            dz = clip(boxes, moving, dz, 2);
            moving.shift(0, 0, dz);
            dx = clip(boxes, moving, dx, 0);
        } else {
            dx = clip(boxes, moving, dx, 0);
            moving.shift(dx, 0, 0);
            dz = clip(boxes, moving, dz, 2);
        }
        return new Vector(dx, dy, dz);
    }

    private static double clip(List<BoundingBox> boxes, BoundingBox moving, double delta, int axis) {
        final double eps = 1.0E-7;
        for (BoundingBox b : boxes) {
            boolean overlapX = b.getMaxX() > moving.getMinX() + eps && b.getMinX() < moving.getMaxX() - eps;
            boolean overlapY = b.getMaxY() > moving.getMinY() + eps && b.getMinY() < moving.getMaxY() - eps;
            boolean overlapZ = b.getMaxZ() > moving.getMinZ() + eps && b.getMinZ() < moving.getMaxZ() - eps;
            switch (axis) {
                case 0 -> {
                    if (overlapY && overlapZ) {
                        if (delta > 0 && b.getMinX() >= moving.getMaxX() - eps) {
                            delta = Math.min(delta, b.getMinX() - moving.getMaxX());
                        } else if (delta < 0 && b.getMaxX() <= moving.getMinX() + eps) {
                            delta = Math.max(delta, b.getMaxX() - moving.getMinX());
                        }
                    }
                }
                case 1 -> {
                    if (overlapX && overlapZ) {
                        if (delta > 0 && b.getMinY() >= moving.getMaxY() - eps) {
                            delta = Math.min(delta, b.getMinY() - moving.getMaxY());
                        } else if (delta < 0 && b.getMaxY() <= moving.getMinY() + eps) {
                            delta = Math.max(delta, b.getMaxY() - moving.getMinY());
                        }
                    }
                }
                default -> {
                    if (overlapX && overlapY) {
                        if (delta > 0 && b.getMinZ() >= moving.getMaxZ() - eps) {
                            delta = Math.min(delta, b.getMinZ() - moving.getMaxZ());
                        } else if (delta < 0 && b.getMaxZ() <= moving.getMinZ() + eps) {
                            delta = Math.max(delta, b.getMaxZ() - moving.getMinZ());
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return delta;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ ray tracing / entities

    public RayTraceResult rayTrace(Vector start, Vector direction, double maxDistance) {
        Vector dir = direction.clone().normalize();
        Set<Long> visited = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        List<int[]> cells = new ArrayList<>();
        for (double t = 0.0; t <= maxDistance + 0.05; t += 0.02) {
            Vector p = start.clone().add(dir.clone().multiply(t));
            int x = p.getBlockX();
            int y = p.getBlockY();
            int z = p.getBlockZ();
            if (visited.add(key(x, y, z))) {
                cells.add(new int[]{x, y, z});
            }
        }
        for (int[] c : cells) {
            double height = SimBlocks.height(type(c[0], c[1], c[2]));
            if (height <= 0.0) {
                continue;
            }
            BoundingBox box = new BoundingBox(c[0], c[1], c[2], c[0] + 1, c[1] + height, c[2] + 1);
            RayTraceResult hit = box.rayTrace(start, dir, maxDistance);
            if (hit != null) {
                return new RayTraceResult(hit.getHitPosition(), block(c[0], c[1], c[2]), hit.getHitBlockFace());
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public List<Entity> nearby(Vector center, double rx, double ry, double rz, Entity exclude) {
        BoundingBox area = new BoundingBox(center.getX() - rx, center.getY() - ry, center.getZ() - rz,
                center.getX() + rx, center.getY() + ry, center.getZ() + rz);
        List<Entity> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (SimPlayer p : players) {
            if (p.player != exclude && p.player.getBoundingBox().overlaps(area)) {
                out.add(p.player);
            }
        }
        for (Entity entity : entities) {
            if (entity != exclude && entity.getBoundingBox().overlaps(area)) {
                out.add(entity);
            }
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```
